Hence, this third operation was divided into two substances, one visible and the other invisible to ordinary mortals, given that matter cannot see and conceive the spirit without dying or without the spirit dissolving or obliterating all matter as it appears. The invisible substance of Christ's third operation consists of having shortened the works and difficult operations that the minors performed and which conformed to the length of time that the Creator had prescribed in their general and particular universal course.

基督的第三桩运作影射其受葬的第三天，是施于两类或多或少被封闭在神性匮乏中的次者的。因此，这第三桩运作分为两种本体：一种为普通凡人所可见，另一种则为其所不可见；因为物质若不死亡、或灵若不消解或泯灭其所显现的一切物质，物质便无法看见并领会灵。基督第三桩运作那不可见的本体，在于缩短了次者所行的、并依造物主在其总体与个别之普世进程中所规定之时长的种种艰难工作与运作。

The Three Circles of Man's Progress: The Sentient, Mental, and Rational
人之进程的三个圈：感识、心识与理性

The universal course to which the minor is subject is made known by careful study, which men of all ages, including this century, have undertaken of the three spherical principles to attain the different means of traveling across the entire surface of the earth with greater sureness. Some consider these three circles only as a means to satisfy their material greed due to the little understanding that they have of these circles, and their flawed motivation for study. I acknowledge that these three circles – called sentient, visual, and rational – have in them the property to instruct man in the knowledge of space and the boundaries of universal, general, and particular creation. However, men must be in great darkness if they look at these circles only from a material point of view. Spiritually, we attach the minor circle to the sentient, the visual to the mental, and the major circle to the rational, and these circles are nothing other than a distinct expanse in which the impartial minors will complete the operation of their temporal action, invisible to corporeal man.

次者所受制的那普世进程，可藉细致的研究而为人所知；历世历代的人，包括这个世纪的人，都对这三个球形本原作过研究，以求得更可靠地遍历整个地表的种种途径。有些人由于对这三个圈所知甚少、研究动机又有偏差，便仅把它们看作满足其物质贪欲的手段。我承认：这三个圈——名为「感识圈」「视识圈」与「理性圈」（sentient, visual, rational）——里面确有教导人认识空间、并认识普世、总体与个别创造之界限的属性。然而，人若仅从物质的角度看待这些圈，便必处于极大的黑暗之中。在属灵的意义上，我们把「次者之圈」系于感识，把「视识之圈」系于心识，把「高位者之圈」系于理性；而这些圈无非是一片独特的广延，公正的次者将在其中完成其属时行动的运作，为属形体之人所不能见。

This operation starts in the sentient circle. From there, the minors enter the visual circle where the height of their spiritual operation is accomplished, and which we call operational reaction since the extent of this second circle is infinitely greater than the first where the minors completed their natural course of operation. Then they go on to enjoy their deserved rest in the obscurity of their reconciliation, into the circle we call rational. All the different planetary and elementary bodies reside in the spaces of these three principal circles, which we recognize by the three principal divine powers certainly active in them, as I will explain by the three following numbers: the number 4 is given to the minor, the number 7 is given to the spirit, and the number 8 is given to the double spirit which is the Christ. The Christ presides over the spirit, the spirit over the minor, and the minor presides over the earthly form. It is the first substance of the Christ's third operation, therefore, which shortens the course and activities of the minors so that they can then rest in the obscurity of their reconciliation.

这一运作始于感识圈。次者从那里进入视识圈，在其中成就其属灵运作的巅峰；我们称之为「运作的反作用」（operational reaction），因为这第二个圈的广度无限地大于次者在其中完成其自然运作进程的第一个圈。然后他们进入我们称为「理性」的圈，在其修和的幽暗中享受应得的安息。一切不同的行星之体与元素之体，都居于这三个主要之圈的空间中；我们藉那确在其中活动的三种主要神性权能来辨认它们，正如我要用以下三个数字所解释的：数字「4」赐予次者，数字「7」赐予灵，数字「8」赐予那双重之灵、即基督。基督统辖灵，灵统辖次者，次者统辖属地之形。因此，正是基督第三桩运作的第一种本体，缩短了次者的进程与活动，使他们随后得以在其修和的幽暗中安息。

§40  The Plan Outlined to Men by the Christ
§40  基督向世人勾画的方案

The second visible substance to corporeal men is made up of the plan that the Christ himself outlined for them, either by his resurrection or by the teachings that he left to his elected faithful through his divine spiritual word. In full sincerity, this is what I know and what I have been told concerning the reconciliation made by the Christ, a reconciliation truly prepared by the Christ's elected righteous, to whom he gave the first example, as I shall explain to you.

那为属形体之人所可见的第二种本体，由基督亲自向他们勾画的方案所构成——无论是藉其复活，还是藉祂经其神性属灵之言留给祂所拣选之信徒的教导。我满怀诚实地说：关于基督所成就的修和，这便是我所知、并我所被告知的；这修和实在是由基督所拣选的义人所预备的，祂给了他们最初的榜样，我将向你解释。

§41  Manifestations of the Christ before the Christ
§41  基督之前的基督显现

Hely reconciled the first man with the Creator, by the mediation of his spirit which joined with the first emanated minor. Enoch, through his justice, acted for the benefit of the generation of Seth's children, the living as well as the deceased, to whom he passed on the feature or authentic seal of his operation. With this seal, he marked those who were worthy of accompanying the Christ when he was to account to his father Creator about the operations that he had performed for his greatest glory and for the shame of his enemies. Noah repeated the same things, as did Melchizedek, Elijah, Zorobabel, and the Christ. These are the ones who were appointed by the order of the Creator to mark the minor spiritual beings, who were to accompany the triumph of the manifestation of divine justice operated by the power of the divine God-man in accordance with the Creator.

赫利藉其灵的中介——这灵与最初受流溢的次者相联合——使首位之人与造物主修和。哈诺客藉其公义，为舍特儿女之世代（无论生者还是死者）之益而行动，把他运作的印征、即其确真的印记传给了他们。他用这印记标记了那些配在基督向其圣父造物主交代——交代祂为其至大荣耀、并为其仇敌之羞辱所行之运作——之时伴随祂的人。诺厄重复了同样的事，默基瑟德、厄里亚（Elijah；和合本：以利亚）、则鲁巴贝耳（Zorobabel；和合本：所罗巴伯）与基督亦然。这些便是奉造物主之命受指派、去标记那些属灵次者存有的人；这些次者将伴随那由神性「神-人」之权能、依造物主而施行的神性公义之彰显的凯旋。

§42  The Righteous Cooperated in the Identification of the Marked
§42  义人在辨识受印者上的协作

I will not describe the details of the different operations that the righteous made to cooperate in the identification of those marked and who were definitely to form the Christ's court when he went in spirit to face the Creator, father of all authority and immutable divine power. But you may well ask me how the reconciliation of the minors was performed by the righteous just mentioned. Was not every event, temporal or spiritual, forewarned by the immutable laws that the Creator had given to His universal creation? My answer is that you should realize that God could not prevent what He had not foreseen, being unable to read into thought when it is conceived, as I have already explained, and being unable to destroy the freedom of spiritual beings. We know that without this freedom, Adam could not have transgressed, and his transgression produced such a change that the Creator was forced to amend the operation of general and particular creation. By general creation is meant the earth, and by particular creation is meant all the minors living in an earthly body as well as a celestial one. Yes, you must know about this transgression which forced the Creator to apply the divine laws throughout all of creation, although you may not understand it perfectly.

我不打算详述义人为协作辨识那些受印者所行的种种不同运作；这些受印者，必将在基督以灵去面见造物主——一切权柄与不可更易之神性权能之父——时，构成基督的廷臣。但你大可问我：方才所提的义人是如何成就诸次者之修和的？难道每一桩事件，无论属时还是属灵的，不都已为造物主赐予其普世创造的不可更易之律法所预先警示了吗？我的回答是：你应当明白，天主无法阻止祂未曾预见之事，因为正如我已解释过的，祂无法在思想被构想之际窥读它，也无法摧毁属灵存有的自由。我们知道，若无这自由，亚当便不可能悖逆；而他的悖逆造成了如此之大的变化，以致造物主不得不修订总体与个别创造的运作。「总体创造」指大地，「个别创造」指一切住在属地之体、亦住在属天之体中的次者。是的，你必须知晓这桩悖逆——正是它迫使造物主把神性律法施行于全部创造之中，尽管你或许不能完全理解。

§43  Moses, Example of Adam's Transgression and Reconciliation
§43  梅瑟，亚当之悖逆与修和的例证

You know that the Creator emanated Adam, God-man, just from the earth and that he was placed in a glorious incorruptible body. You know that when he transgressed, the Creator personally cursed him because of his tainted work, and then cursed the entire earth. You also know that by this transgression, Adam degenerated from his glorious form into a form of earthly matter. Through me, you will come to know that all these things would not have been able to occur within particular and general nature, if the Creator had not for a time suspended and withdrawn towards Himself the powers that He had given to the first man in his state of justice. The change which took place from Adam's glorious body to his earthly one heralded the new laws that the Creator would give him when he was reconciled. At the time of this reconciliation, the Creator blessed him a second time, pardoned his error, and gave him a power inferior to the one that he had had before his crime, which would last him until the time of his perfect reconciliation.

你知道，造物主把神-人亚当公义地从大地流溢出来，并将他安置于一个荣耀不朽的身体之中。你知道，当他悖逆时，造物主因他受玷之工而亲自咒诅了他，继而咒诅了整个大地。你也知道，藉这悖逆，亚当从他荣耀之形堕落退化为一个属地物质之形。藉着我，你将得知：倘若造物主没有一度把祂在公义之态中赐予首位之人的权能暂时收回、敛于祂自身，这一切便都不可能在个别与总体的自然之中发生。从亚当荣耀之体到其属地之体的这一变化，预告了造物主在他蒙修和时将赐给他的新律法。在这修和之时，造物主第二次祝福了他，赦免了他的过错，并赐给他一种低于他犯罪之前所拥有之权能的权能，这权能将伴他直到他完全修和之时。

This is clearly and physically demonstrated to us by the two different laws that Moses brought down from the mountain. Moses only gave the people of Israel the first set of laws. The transgression of these people forced Moses to break the tables, and in this way deprive the Israelites of the divine law that they so earnestly desired to receive. After this event, Moses reconciled himself with his people and promised them a second law from the Eternal, a law that the Eternal was pleased to grant because of the reconciliation that He had made with His elected people. This reconciliation could not come directly from the sole power and will of Moses, but only from the Creator's power. This is demonstrated by the fact that all of the powers of one man are not capable of reconciling twenty others by his will, and if Moses had operated only on his own and without the assistance of a superior being, all of his words and efforts would have been useless.

这一点，藉梅瑟（Moses；和合本：摩西）从山上带下的两套不同律法，清楚而有形地向我们显明了。梅瑟只把第一套律法颁给以色列（Israel）民。这民的悖逆迫使梅瑟摔碎了法版，如此便剥夺了以色列人他们如此恳切渴望领受的神性律法。这事之后，梅瑟与他的民修和，并应许他们从永恒主而来的第二套律法；永恒主因祂与其拣选之民所成的修和而乐意赐下此律法。这修和不可能直接出于梅瑟独有的权能与意志，而只能出于造物主的权能。这一点可由以下事实证明：一个人的全部权能，都不足以凭其意志使另外二十人修和；倘若梅瑟仅凭自己、而无一位上位存有的相助来运作，他一切的言语与努力都将归于徒然。

Let us judge this by comparing the efforts of men of this century who speak crudely of those of the first centuries. How and in what way can those of this century be reconciled if they have never seen any physical manifestation of a divine or spiritual kind, manifestations of immutable laws which should activate and maintain universal creation for the period of time prescribed by the Creator? Perhaps you would like to know what the length of time this period is, but it is not the place to discuss this with you now. I shall pursue the explanation of Adam's transgression, because it is from this that all of the different events have arisen since the beginning of the world until our day, and which will continue until the end of time.

让我们藉比较来判断此事：这个世纪的人粗鄙地谈论最初诸世纪的人。这个世纪的人若从未见过任何神性或属灵之有形彰显——即那应当激发并维系普世创造、直到造物主所规定之时段的不可更易之律法的彰显——他们又如何、藉何方式得以修和呢？你或许想知道这一时段的时长，但此处并非与你讨论此事之所。我将继续解释亚当的悖逆，因为自世界之初直到我们这一日、并将持续到时间终结的种种不同事件，皆由此而生。

Houva or "Hommesse" — Fruit of Adam's Transgression
胡瓦或「Hommesse」——亚当悖逆之果

The pain Adam felt by becoming pensive and thinking was nothing other than what had to manifest during the first of these unfortunate eras which arose with his posterity, and which resulted in Adam understanding most profoundly the gravest consequence of his transgression. He experienced it by the trouble, agitation, and different struggles which took place within him when he was restricted in his second body of earthly matter. In this state, he lamented to the Creator, he begged for clemency from the living God – who is the Christ – and the vivifying God. In his somnolence, the spirit presented him with the fruit of his transgression, which filled him with consternation and which increased the acuteness of his remorse while reflecting on his works. He thought about what the Creator was asking of him. This poor man felt that he had to sincerely acknowledge his error, confess that he had committed it, and how he committed it. Obedient to divine will, Adam admitted with the greatest sincerity to the work of his cursed thought and the action of his own will, which linked him to the fruit of his work for time immemorial. He confirmed this confession by giving to the fruit of his transgression the name of Houva, or Hommesse, which means flesh of my flesh, bones of my bones, and the work of my operation conceived and exercised by my soiled hands. This is what you were seeking to know regarding Adam's transgression.

亚当因变得「思虑」而思想所感受的痛苦，无非就是那必在与其后裔一同兴起的第一个不幸时代中显现之物；这一痛苦使亚当极深地领悟了其悖逆最严重的后果。当他被禁锢于其属地物质的第二个身体中时，他藉里面所生的烦扰、激荡与种种挣扎而亲历了这痛苦。在此态中，他向造物主哀诉，他向那活的天主——即基督——并向那赐生命的天主恳求宽仁。在他的昏沉中，那灵把他悖逆之果呈于他面前，使他充满惊惶，并在他省思自己所行之工时，加剧了他悔恨的尖锐。他思想造物主对他的要求。这可怜的人觉得，他必须真诚地承认自己的过错，承认他犯了此罪、并承认他是如何犯的。亚当顺服神性的意志，以至大的诚实承认了他那受咒诅之思想的工作与他自己意志的行动——正是这工作，把他与其所作之果亘古地联结在一起。他藉着给其悖逆之果起名「胡瓦」（Houva），或「Hommesse」，来确认这一承认；其意为「我骨中的骨、肉中的肉，是我以受玷之手所构想并施行的运作之工」。这便是你想就亚当之悖逆所要知道的。

§45  The Decree of Original Sin
§45  原罪的判令

What I have just said to you about Adam's transgression and the fruit which came from it demonstrates to you quite clearly what our spiritual and physical nature is like, and how much both have degenerated because the soul became subject to the suffering of privation and the form became passive in contrast to what it would have been had Adam united his will with the Creator's. From this you can also more or less recognize what we spiritually call decree, pronounced in the name of the Eternal against Adam's generations until the end of time, and what we commonly call original sin.

我方才就亚当的悖逆及由之而生之果向你所说的，已相当清楚地向你显明：我们属灵与属形体的本性是怎样的，又因灵魂沦于匮乏之苦、形体相较于「亚当若使其意志与造物主之意志相合本应如何」而变为被动，二者堕落退化到何等地步。由此，你也多少能认出我们在属灵意义上所称的「判令」——即奉永恒主之名、针对亚当世代直到时间终结所宣告者——以及我们通常所称的「原罪」。

§46  Change to Ceremonial Laws
§46  礼仪律法的变更

Now I must explain more clearly the change of the ceremonial laws operating in general and particular creation in relation to the first man's crime. I have explained to you the power, virtue, command, and authority of the first emancipated minor in his glorious body. I have also shown you how, by his crime, he transmuted himself from this glorious form into one of earthly matter. But this second material body had the same apparent image as the glorious body in which Adam had been emanated. There was therefore a change only in the laws by which Adam would be governed.[脚注：Eve，即「女人」] When a temporal created being changes his action, he necessarily changes the laws of operation. When the Creator reconciled general, universal, and particular creation, there was a change in the laws which directed this creation before it was cursed and then reconciled. It was the same for the first man. Having changed his first glorious state, it was absolutely necessary that the Creator change the first laws of operation which He had given him, these laws being no longer suitable for the action and direction of a bodily form as restricted as the one that Adam was bound to assume by divine authority.

现在我须更清楚地解释那与首位之人之罪相关、运作于总体与个别创造中的礼仪律法之变更。我已向你解释过那解放独立的首位次者在其荣耀之体中的权能、德能、命令与权柄。我也已向你显明：他如何藉其罪，把自己从这荣耀之形转变为一个属地物质之形。但这第二个物质身体，与亚当受流溢时所在的荣耀之体有着相同的表面形象。因此，所变更的仅在于那将要治理亚当的律法。〔脚注：Eve，即「女人」。〕当一个属时的受造存有改变其行动时，他必然改变其运作的律法。当造物主使总体、普世与个别创造修和时，那在此创造被咒诅、继而被修和之前所主导它的律法，便发生了变更。首位之人的情形亦然。他既改变了其最初荣耀之态，造物主便绝对必须变更祂先前赐给他的运作之律法，因为这些律法已不再适于像亚当奉神性权柄不得不披上的那样受限之形体的行动与导引了。

The laws governing the corporeal forms of apparently passive matter are not, as you may appreciate, those which govern every minor spirit, agent, and governor of a glorious bodily form and which does not draw its origin from the matter which we see physically condensed. The glorious form does not embody the minor spirit or any other in divine privation, because this form is known by the minor or every other spirit sent by the Eternal to manifest the glory of this divine Being in men and anywhere else that the Creator pleases. Moreover, I can say that Adam and his posterity, being held in this form of earthly matter, did not have to worship the Creator in the same way for which the first man had been emanated. If the first minor changed form, he would of necessity have had to change in operation. This new action is extremely limited by the force of the laws that the Creator exercised against Adam and his generations until the end of time.

你可领会：那治理表面被动之物质形体的律法，并不是那治理每一次者之灵——荣耀形体的施动者与主宰、且其本源并非取自我们所见之有形凝结之物质者——的律法。荣耀之形并不为处于神性匮乏中的次者之灵或任何其他灵充作体；因为这形乃是为永恒主所差、用以在世人中、并在造物主所悦的任何他处彰显这神性存有之荣耀的次者或每一其他灵所识。此外，我可以说：亚当及其后裔既被拘于这属地物质之形中，便无须以首位之人受流溢时所当用的同一方式来敬拜造物主。倘若首位次者改变了形，他便必然不得不在运作上有所改变。这新的行动，被造物主针对亚当及其世代直到时间终结所施行之律法的力量，极度地加以限制。

The Word of Creation Put to Adam by the Eternal in His Image
永恒主按其形象置于亚当的创造之言

This limited action should not surprise you, given Adam's iniquitous use of the first Word that the Eternal had given him so that he could produce a divine posterity. This Word, which you may not know, and perhaps consider to be something incomprehensible, was only the intent and the will that the first man had to carry out by his powerful Word. But to understand more clearly the Word of God's posterity that Adam had innate in him, it is necessary to go back to the knowledge of the different Words that the Creator used for His universal creation, in which are found the particular and the general, according to His intent, His will, and His Word, from which every action, form, and spiritual minor is derived. By joining these last three things – intent, will, and word – to the three preceding ones, I shall make the three principal Words that the Eternal used to create all things understood.

这受限的行动不该使你惊讶，因为亚当不义地使用了永恒主所赐他、用以产生神性后裔的那最初之「言」（Word）。这「言」——你或许并不知晓，或许以为是某种不可理解之物——不过就是首位之人当藉其大能之言所要实行的「意向」（intent）与「意志」（will）。但要更清楚地理解亚当内具的那「天主后裔之言」，就必须回溯到造物主用于其普世创造的种种不同之「言」的知识；个别与总体皆寓于其普世创造之中，按祂的意向、祂的意志与祂的言而定，每一行动、形体与属灵次者皆由此而出。把后三者——意向、意志与言——联于前述三者，我便能使永恒主用以创造万物的三个主要之「言」得以为人所理解。

Intent is linked to the creation of the universe, which is illustrated by a large circle inside which the general and the particular are put into action and movement. Will is linked to the creation of the general, or the earth, which is illustrated by a triangle, just like the image that the Creator conceived in His thinking imagination, and word is linked to the specific emanation of the spiritual minors living in the particular corporeal form, similar to that of the earth and which was similarly produced according to the image of divine thought. It is by this and what is to follow that we can understand the Word of creation that Adam had in his power. If the Creator had no intent, He would not have had any will; if He had no will, He would not have had word or action. Now, since the spiritual minor being is only the fruit of the operation of these three divine principles, the first man to be the God-man on the earth had to bear the marks of his origin and these three principles innate in him when the Eternal detached him from His divine immensity. We have previously seen that God could not be the author of evil, thus Adam was emanated in goodness and justice. Adam therefore had within him a powerful Word, because from his spoken command, according to good intent and divine spiritual will, there were to come forth glorious impassive forms like those which appeared in the Creator's imagination.

意向与宇宙的创造相联，以一个大圈来表征，总体与个别在其内被付诸行动与运动。意志与总体（即大地）的创造相联，以一个三角形来表征，正如造物主在其思想的想象中所构思的形象；而言则与那些住在个别形体中之属灵次者的具体流溢相联，这形体与大地之形相似，同样是按神性思想的形象所产生的。藉此、并藉随后所述，我们便能理解亚当所掌握的「创造之言」。倘若造物主没有意向，祂便不会有任何意志；祂若没有意志，便不会有言或行动。如今，既然属灵次者存有只是这三项神性本原运作之果，那么，那要作大地上神-人的首位之人，在永恒主把他从其神性无量界中分离出来之时，便必然要带着其本源的印记、并带着这三项内具于他的本原。我们先前已看到，天主不可能是恶的作者，故亚当乃在善与公义中被流溢出来。因此，亚当里面有一大能之言，因为从他口出的命令，按善的意向与神性属灵的意志，本要生出荣耀的无受苦之形，如同那些显现于造物主想象中的形一般。

These glorious forms could not be earthly material forms which were destined according to the Creator's will to serve as a prison to the transgressor spirits, and the form into which Adam was placed was purely spiritual and glorious, so that he could have dominion over all of creation, and freely exercise over it the power and command that the Creator had given him. This glorious form is nothing but an apparent image which the spirit conceives and nurtures according to the needs and orders that it receives from the Creator. This form is as swiftly reintegrated as it is fathered by the spirit. We call it impassive because it is not subject to any elementary influence whatsoever, pure and simple. It is not susceptible to any food, unless the spirit itself provides it. No particle of the central fire can act on it. Finally, this glorious form would have been perpetuated by Adam for the reproduction of his spiritual posterity, just as his carnal posterity is known to us, but without any principal material operation, however, as the advent and the resurrection of the Christ and the descent of the divine spirit in the temple of Solomon have shown us.

这些荣耀之形不可能是属地的物质之形——后者按造物主的意志注定要充作悖逆之灵的牢狱；而亚当所被安置于其中的形，则是纯属灵而荣耀的，好使他能统辖整个创造，并在其上自由行使造物主所赐他的权能与命令。这荣耀之形无非是灵按其从造物主所领受的需要与命令而构思、滋养的一个表面形象。此形被灵所生出何等迅速，便何等迅速地被重新融合。我们称之为「无受苦的」，因为它纯然单一，绝不受任何元素的影响。除非灵自身供给，它不需任何食物。中央之火的任何微粒都不能作用于它。最后，这荣耀之形本要由亚当延续下去，以繁衍其属灵的后裔，正如我们所知的他属肉身的后裔一般，但其间却无任何主要的物质运作——正如基督的降临与复活、以及神性之灵降于撒罗满圣殿所向我们显明的。

§48  Intent, Will, and Action Determine the Word of Creation
§48  意向、意志与行动决定创造之言

By everything which has been said, we cannot doubt the considerable changes to the laws of operation resulting from the first man's transgression, whether on the general and particular body, or on the minors and the very operations they have to carry out today, and which are contrary to those for which they had been emanated. Furthermore, we have seen a glimmer of this famous ternary number of creation of all forms by the joining of intent, will, and word, which produces divine action, and which is certainly the Word. In fact, of what use would intent be without will, will without word, and word without effect or action? There must have been intent, will, and word to operate each of these parts of creation, but it is word which determined the action of divine intent and will.

藉所说的一切，我们不能怀疑首位之人的悖逆所导致的运作律法之重大变更——无论是在总体与个别之体上，还是在诸次者及其今日所必须执行、却与他们受流溢之初衷相反的种种运作上。此外，我们已瞥见那著名的、创造一切形之「三元数」的一线微光：它由意向、意志与言的相合而成，产生神性的行动，而这行动无疑即是「言」。事实上，意向若无意志，意志若无言，言若无效果或行动，又有何用？创造的每一部分都必须有意向、意志与言方能运作，但决定神性意向与意志之行动的，乃是「言」。

It is by this determination that the Word came to be, and therefore it is certainly within the Creator's Word that the ternary number of universal, general, and particular creation exists, and nowhere else, for intent, will, and word produce a spiritual effect or action, which shows that the Word of creation is not produced from itself, because it is emanated from the intent, will, and word of the Creator. It is also by this Word and its emanation that we recognize with certainty that the first ternary number of whatever creation is co-eternal with God as follows: intent (1), will (2), and word (3), from which action or the Word arises. Add these three numbers and you will find 6, as follows: 1 and 2 make 3 and another 3 make 6. This completes the six thoughts of universal, general, and particular creation of the Eternal, and this number is certainly in universal, general, and particular creation.

正是藉这一「决定」，「言」才得以成就；因此，普世、总体与个别创造的三元数，必然存于造物主的「言」之内，而别无他处；因为意向、意志与言产生一种属灵的效果或行动，这表明「创造之言」并非由其自身所产生，乃是从造物主的意向、意志与言流溢而出。也正是藉这「言」及其流溢，我们确切地认出：任何创造的第一个三元数都与天主共永恒，如下所述：意向（1）、意志（2）与言（3），行动或「言」即由此而生。将这三个数相加，你便得「6」，如下：1 与 2 得 3，再加一个 3 得 6。这便成全了永恒主在普世、总体与个别创造中的六个思想，而这数字必然存于普世、总体与个别创造之中。

§49  Necessity of the Reconciliation of Men
§49  人之修和的必要

By everything that I have just told you, you should understand from where every created spiritual and material being came, as well as the great power which the first man long ago enjoyed and which his generations can still enjoy today. This power, however, is of little consequence if man does not make his reconciliation with the Creator. I will go so far as to say that it is worth nothing without it, and that the brute has more virtue in his passive instinct alone than the spiritual minor when he has degenerated and what he destroys through divine spiritual inaction to the point of becoming the tomb of death. What I mean by the expression, the tomb of death, is that the poor minors who are not reconciled will become the prey of perverse spirits and, by joining them, will cause them to remain in their reprobation for an eternity.

藉我方才告诉你的一切，你应当明白：每一受造的属灵与物质存有从何而来，以及首位之人昔日所享、而其世代今日仍可享有的那伟大权能。然而，人若不与造物主修和，这权能便无足轻重。我甚至要说：没有修和，它便一文不值；而当属灵次者堕落退化、并藉神性属灵的怠惰自我毁坏以致沦为「死亡之坟」时，那畜类单凭其被动的本能，反倒比他更有德能。我所说「死亡之坟」一语的意思是：那些不曾修和的可怜次者，将沦为悖逆之灵的猎物，并因投靠它们，使它们永远停留在其遭弃的状态中。

§50  The Triple and Quadruple Divine Essence in Dealing with the Minor
§50  论及次者的三重与四重神性本质

This then will be the fate of minors who have not followed the Creator's law. See how much we have to keep on our guard and strive to imitate Adam who, after having sincerely confessed his crime and with the bitterest regret obtained his reconciliation from the Creator and was partly restored in his former virtues and powers upon the three kinds of temporal creation, on the condition, however, that his future intent and will conform to the laws of his reconciler. Reflect on this reconciliation and you will always see the ternary number – namely, Adam, the Christ, and the Creator. You will see that this triple divine essence very clearly forms the three principles of all creation as follows: the intent of the father (1), the will of the Christ (2), and the word of the spiritual minor which arises from the intent and the will of the two former (3). I put the minor among the first three divine essences because he is the product of the intent of the Father, the will of the regenerator son, and the action, of divine spirit. I shall explain this more clearly when I describe the quadruple divine essence about which I do not intend to keep you ignorant, even though I have not yet mentioned it.

这便是那些不曾遵循造物主律法之次者的命运。看哪，我们当何等地保持警醒，竭力效法亚当：他真诚地承认了自己的罪，怀着最痛切的悔恨从造物主得着了修和，并在三类属时创造之上被部分地恢复了他先前的德能与权能——条件却是：他日后的意向与意志须合于其修和者的律法。省思这修和，你便会始终见到那「三元数」——即亚当、基督与造物主。你将看到，这三重神性本质极清楚地构成了一切创造的三项本原，如下：父的意向（1）、基督的意志（2），以及那由前二者之意向与意志而生的属灵次者之言（3）。我把次者列于这头三重神性本质之中，因为他乃是圣父之意向、重生之圣子之意志、并神性之灵之行动的产物。当我描述「四重神性本质」时，我将更清楚地解释此点；对于四重神性本质，我虽尚未提及，却无意让你蒙在鼓里。

Part II — CAIN
第二部 — 加音（Cain；和合本：该隐）
§51  Cain, Born from a Wild Passion
§51  加音，生于狂乱的情欲

I shall continue to discuss the first man's perfect reconciliation. When the Creator blessed Adam and his tainted work, He said: "Adam, enhance your work so that together you may reproduce a posterity of particular forms in which the universal and general form will be contained beyond doubt and with certainty, just as the image is contained in the form that you are directing for the period of time that I have set down." The Scriptures relate these words as follows: "Grow and multiply." That is, when Adam and Eve were sent from their first place of action, He commanded them to reproduce forms like their own. They carried out this command with such a furious passion of their material senses so as to delay the first man's entire reconciliation. Nevertheless, they created the bodily form of their first son whom they named Cain, which means "son of my sorrow." This name was given to him by Adam because he really felt that he had conducted an action driven by a wild passion and contrary to the moderation that he should have used (Let us say in passing that the command made directly to Adam by the Creator for his reproduction teaches us that the Creator had made him the guardian of his reproductive seed.)

我将继续讨论首位之人的完全修和。当造物主祝福亚当及其受玷之工时，祂说：“亚当，增益你的工，使你们一同繁衍出一个个别形体的后裔；在其中，普世与总体之形必无疑而确定地被含容，正如形象被含容于你在我所定之时段中所导引的形体之内一般。”圣经如此记述这些话：“你们要生养众多。”也就是说，当亚当与厄娃（Eve；和合本：夏娃）被从他们最初的行动之所遣出时，祂命令他们繁衍出与他们自己相似的形。他们以其物质感官如此狂暴的情欲来执行这命令，以致延迟了首位之人的全部修和。尽管如此，他们仍造出了其长子的形体，给他起名加音（Cain；和合本：该隐），意为「我忧苦之子」。这名是亚当给他起的，因为他确实觉得：他所行的乃是一桩出于狂乱情欲、且违背他本应有之节制的行动。（顺带一提：造物主为其繁衍而直接向亚当所下的命令，教导我们：造物主已立他为其生殖之种的守护者。）

Adam was right to name his first-born "son of my sorrow" because through this act his reconciliation was deferred, and by this same act and the name of Cain that he had given to his first-born, he prophesied the great sorrow that he would experience in the future by the severe transgression of his descendants, who would contravene the divine laws, precepts, and commands, and this is why Adam was acknowledged as the first prophet by these descendants. However, this son, begotten by a passion contrary to the Creator's command, had to contribute to the first father's reconciliation through the intense suffering that he would make him feel, by repeating the evil of his first transgression which Cain mysteriously carried out in Adam's presence. This was the cruelest blow and the most bitter remorse that he could create in his father's heart. It is also not possible to imagine what Adam's pain and despondence were like when he saw his first son fall victim to diabolical powers. No one but this first father could judge the sorrow that he and his son had to experience, since it was not long since Adam himself had been delivered through the Creator's absolute mercy from the hands of these same demons who had just seduced his first son and plunged him forever in divine privation.

亚当给长子起名「我忧苦之子」是对的，因为藉这一行动，他的修和被延迟了；并且藉这同一行动、并藉他给长子所起的「加音」之名，他预言了自己日后将因其后裔严重的悖逆而要经历的巨大忧苦——这些后裔将违背神性的律法、诫命与命令；这也正是亚当何以被这些后裔公认为第一位先知的缘由。然而，这位由一桩违背造物主命令之情欲所生的儿子，却不得不藉他要使其父亲所感受的剧烈痛苦，来促成首位之父的修和：他重蹈了其父第一次悖逆之恶，而加音乃是在亚当面前奥秘地行了此事。这是他所能在其父心中造成的最残酷的打击与最痛苦的悔恨。当亚当眼见自己的长子沦为魔性权能的牺牲品时，他的痛苦与沮丧实非笔墨所能想象。除这位首位之父外，无人能体察他与其子所必经历的忧苦；因为亚当本人不久前才藉造物主绝对的慈悲，从这同一群魔——刚刚诱惑了他的长子并把他永远抛入神性匮乏中的群魔——的手中得了拯救。

§52  Kani, Worthy Sister of Cain
§52  卡尼（Kani），加音相配的妹妹

Through this double sorrow, Adam strengthened in faith and confidence in the Creator. He bemoaned more than ever of having cooperated in the conception of this poor son outside the Creator's prescribed limits. He voluntarily submitted to the Creator's will by a genuine oath and promised never, under any circumstances, to stray from the laws, precepts, and commands that the Creator would outline. But this first father's submission was only apparent. He did not have the perseverance to which he had sworn; on the contrary, with his companion Hommesse or Eve, he conceived a female generation that he named conjointly Kani, which means "child of confusion," because this conception was brought about according to the same laws under which Cain had been conceived.

藉这双重的忧苦，亚当在对造物主的信心与笃信中得了坚固。他比以往任何时候都更痛悔自己曾在造物主所定的界限之外参与了这可怜儿子的孕育。他藉一个真诚的誓言自愿臣服于造物主的意志，并应许无论在任何情形下，都绝不偏离造物主将要勾画的律法、诫命与命令。但这位首位之父的臣服只是表面的。他没有他所起誓要持守的那份坚忍；相反，他与其伴侣「Hommesse」或厄娃，孕育了一个女性的后代，二人共同给她起名「卡尼」（Kani），意为「混乱之女」，因为这次孕育乃是按孕育加音时所循的同一律法而成的。

§53  Abac, Worthy Sister of Cain and Kani
§53  阿巴克（Abac），加音与卡尼相配的妹妹

Five years later, the liaison that he saw between his first two children made him believe that the time had come when all his sorrows would end. Again, he shut his eyes to the truth and conceived with Houva, or Eve, a third descendant, who was a female, and whom he named Abac, which means "child of matter," or "child of divine privation." Adam thus remained six years without producing descendants because, during this interval and from the time of the birth of his third child, he fell into a great despondency. Such an intense disgust with himself took hold of him that he did not know what to become. He fell into total inaction whether for divine or diabolical spiritual purposes, no longer being sensitive to either good or bad impressions. Eventually, he became like a newly born child. What made him like this was the undeniable knowledge that he had of all his past crimes against the Creator. The good spirit in him gave him this knowledge and made him clearly understand that the earth that he had cultivated up to then, against the Creator's commands, would produce only sorrow and bitterness in him and would be the poison of discord for all his descendants.

五年之后，他在头两个孩子之间所见的亲密往来，使他相信：他一切忧苦得以终结之时已经来临。他再次对真理闭上了眼睛，与「胡瓦」（Houva）或厄娃孕育了第三个后代，是个女性，他给她起名「阿巴克」（Abac），意为「物质之女」或「神性匮乏之女」。亚当此后六年未再产生后代；因为在这段间隔中、自其第三个孩子出生之时起，他陷入了极大的沮丧。一种对自身如此强烈的厌恶攫住了他，以致他不知自己将成何样。他陷入彻底的无所作为之中，无论对神性还是对魔性的属灵目的皆然，对善或恶的印象都不再敏感。最终，他变得像个新生的婴孩。使他落到这般地步的，是他对自己一切过去得罪造物主之罪行那无可否认的知识。他里面的善灵把这知识赐给他，使他清楚明白：他迄今违背造物主命令所耕耘的土地，只会在他里面生出忧苦与苦涩，并将成为毒害其一切后裔的不和之毒。

Part III — ABEL
第三部 — 亚伯尔（Abel；和合本：亚伯）
§54  Abel and Three Younger Ones Conceived in Purity
§54  在洁净中受孕的亚伯尔及三名弟妹

When chasing Adam from earthly paradise, the Creator made these threatening words, as the Scriptures report: "Go and cultivate the earth; it will give you only brambles." I ask whether there are bramble thorns sharper than those that a criminal descendant can pierce into the heart of a good father? The Creator had announced that these evils would happen to the first man's acts in earthly matter, but it was in this ruined state that He intended to forgive the first man's weaknesses by preparing him and his companion to work together in a pure and simple conception without excessive participation of their material senses. And so Adam's descendants were not limited to the three children that I have just mentioned, but he had four more, two males and two females, and it was the first of these four – a son – who had to bring about his father's reconciliation. Thus Adam, along with his companion Eve, carried out an operation pleasing to the Creator, and she conceived the seed that Adam had scattered into her womb and which she happily kept until its maturity.

当造物主把亚当逐出地上乐园时，祂说了这些带着威吓的话，正如圣经所载：“去耕种土地罢，它只会给你生出荆棘。”我要问：世上可有比这更尖利的荆棘之刺——即一个犯罪的后裔所能刺入一位慈父之心的那种刺？造物主早已宣告：这些祸患必临到首位之人在属地物质中的作为；然而正是在这败坏之态中，祂有意宽赦首位之人的软弱，预备他与其伴侣一同以一种纯洁而单纯的孕育来协力——不让他们的物质感官过度参与其中。因此，亚当的后裔并不限于我方才所提的三个孩子；他另有四个孩子，两男两女，而这四个中的头一个——一个儿子——正是那要促成其父修和的。于是，亚当连同其伴侣厄娃（Eve；和合本：夏娃），行了一桩蒙造物主喜悦的运作；厄娃孕育了亚当撒入她胎中的种，欢欢喜喜地怀到它成熟。

§55  Abel, Child of Peace, or a Being Elevated above Every Spiritual Sense
§55  亚伯尔，和平之子，或高越于一切属灵感识之上的存有

It was not particularly surprising that Eve took special care of this new fruit, because she felt a peace, a joy, and an inexpressible satisfaction grow within her instead of the intense and cruel pain that she felt during the whole time that she had carried her first three children. This difference came from the gifts that the grace of the Eternal had impregnated into the soul of this fourth child, which in turn communicated to her its innocence, candor, and pureness. Even Adam became content and joyous again, which increased Eve's gladness even more. They looked with particular happiness upon the time this last fruit was conceived; it was during the seventh year after Adam had his first three children. He could not help praising the Lord for this event by saying, "The Eternal, Creator of heaven and earth and his servant, Adam, or Reaux, be forever blessed for all that He has created! It is from Him that I have a fourth descendant who will be my complete satisfaction in life here below and in times to come." He called this child Aba 4, which means "child of peace," or Abel 10, which means a being elevated above every spiritual sense.

厄娃对这新的果实格外用心，这并不特别令人惊讶；因为她觉得在里面生出一种平安、一种喜乐、并一种难以言表的满足，取代了她怀头三个孩子那整段时间所感受的剧烈而残酷的痛楚。这一差别，源于永恒主的恩宠浸注于这第四个孩子灵魂中的种种恩赐，而这灵魂又把它的天真、纯朴与洁净传通给了她。连亚当也重又变得知足喜乐，这更增添了厄娃的欢欣。他们格外欢喜地回想这最后一个果实受孕之时；那是在亚当有了头三个孩子之后的第七年。亚当禁不住为此事赞美上主，说：“愿永恒主——天地及其仆人亚当（或称王者之人 Reaux）的造物主——为祂所创造的一切永远受颂扬！正是从祂，我得了第四个后裔，他将在今世与来世成为我完全的满足。”他给这孩子起名「阿巴」（Aba，4），意为「和平之子」，或「亚伯尔」（Abel，10），意为「高越于一切属灵感识之上的存有」。

§56  Like Adam and Eve We have Elizabeth and Zacharias, Mary and Joseph
§56  如同亚当与厄娃，我们有依撒伯尔与匝加利亚、玛利亚与若瑟

Everything that I have just said was repeated in physical kind, towards the middle of time, by the pregnancy of Mary and of Elizabeth, by the thrill that Elizabeth felt growing in her soul when she greeted her cousin, Mary, who had come to visit her, and by the contentment that the two temporal fathers felt, one for the physical operation of their work and the other for the unique spiritual operation that the Eternal had manifested for the benefit of his adopted wife. The explanation of this will be seen elsewhere. Let us here follow Adam's descendants.

我方才所说的一切，在时间的中途，藉玛利亚（Mary；和合本：马利亚）与依撒伯尔（Elizabeth；和合本：以利沙伯）的怀孕、藉依撒伯尔在迎接前来看望她的表姊玛利亚时灵魂中所感的悸动、并藉两位属时之父所感的满足，以有形的方式重演了一次；这两位父亲，一位是为其工作的有形运作而满足，另一位是为永恒主为其所领之妻的益处而彰显的那独特属灵运作而满足。关于此事的解释，将于别处得见。此处让我们继续追述亚当的后裔。

§57  Abel's Devotion, a Type of Christ
§57  亚伯尔的虔敬，基督的预表

Adam and Eve took particular care of this fourth child. They never let him out of their sight although neither yet knew completely what fruit they would reap later. They did not grow weary of admiring his conduct, either towards his two sisters and his brother, Cain, or towards his mother and father. From the tenderest age, no more than three years old, he tried to win their friendship, and all the while his goodness, wisdom, virtue, and good example increased while living among the earth's righteous. This blessed child constantly endeavored to direct spiritual devotion to the Creator, which surprised his entire family.

亚当与厄娃对这第四个孩子格外用心。他们从不让他离开自己的视线，尽管二人都还不完全知道日后会收得怎样的果实。他们百看不厌地叹赏他的行为——无论是对他的两个姊姊和哥哥加音，还是对他的父母。他从最稚嫩的年岁起，尚不足三岁，便努力赢得他们的友爱；与此同时，他的良善、智慧、德能与好榜样，在这地上义人之间的生活中不断增长。这蒙福的孩子不住地竭力把属灵的虔敬引向造物主，这使他全家都感惊奇。

All of his acts aimed to calm God's justice towards His first minor creature and descendants, knowing by these acts how severely this generation was going to be struck by divine justice. In effect, Abel behaved towards the Eternal like Adam should have done in his first state of glory; the devotion Abel directed to Him was exactly the kind that the Creator expected from his first minor. Abel was still a striking example of the manifestation of divine glory which was carried out by the true Adam, or Reaux, or the Christ, for the perfect reconciliation of past, present, and future descendants of the first man, in return for which these descendants would make full use of the plan of action pure divine mercy would set out for them, just as Abel had already predicted by all his actions towards Adam and Adam's three first-born.

他一切的作为都旨在平息天主对其首位次者受造物及其后裔的公义，因为他藉这些作为知晓：这一世代将何等严厉地遭神性公义的击打。事实上，亚伯尔对永恒主的行事为人，正如亚当在其最初荣耀之态中本应有的；亚伯尔所引向祂的虔敬，恰是造物主对其首位次者所期望的那种。亚伯尔还是那由真正的亚当（或王者之人 Reaux，或基督）所施行、为首位之人过去、现在与将来之后裔的完全修和而设的神性荣耀之彰显的鲜明例证；作为回报，这些后裔将充分利用纯粹的神性慈悲为他们所安排的行动之策，正如亚伯尔藉他对亚当及亚当三名长子长女的一切行动早已预示的。

§58  Rivalry between Abel and His Three Older Siblings
§58  亚伯尔与三名兄姊之间的相争

The conduct of Adam's three first-born was quite the opposite of Abel's. The more that Adam and Eve were impressed by their son, Abel, the more their three first-born became their brother's enemy. Adam and Eve regarded Abel as an interpreter of the spiritual and the divine, and they observed carefully everything he said and made them do, in joy and holiness. The three first-born, on the contrary, opposed everything that Abel carried out for their own benefit, and for their father's and mother's benefit. They even went so far as to set traps in order to destroy and physically remove him, which they did as we shall see.

亚当三名年长儿女的行为，与亚伯尔的恰恰相反。亚当与厄娃越是为其子亚伯尔所打动，他们那三名长子长女便越发成为其弟的仇敌。亚当与厄娃视亚伯尔为属灵与神性之事的诠释者，他们在喜乐与圣洁中细心留意他所说、并所吩咐他们去做的一切。那三名年长者却相反，凡亚伯尔为他们自己之益、并为其父母之益所行的一切，他们都加以反对。他们甚至设下圈套，要毁灭他、从肉身上把他除去——他们果真这样做了，我们下文便会看到。

§59  Adam Acts with Abel and Cain
§59  亚当与亚伯尔、加音一同施作

One day, Adam, with his two sons, wanted to make a divine spiritual offering to the Creator, but his female descendants could not be present because of their lack of virtue and spiritual power, and, because of their lack of strength and steadfastness to sustain similar acts, Adam kept the women away at a distance of 45 cubits from the place that he had chosen for his work. When everything was in readiness, Adam commanded and consecrated his last-born, Abel, to be the first to exercise the spiritual functions of the proposed offering. Abel immediately prepared himself to fulfill this obligation. He set up the altar, or the proper circles, in the center of which he offered the first fragrances. These fragrances were his own corporeal form offered in sacrifice to the Creator while prostrating himself in humility. At the same time, he submitted his spiritual minor being to the Eternal, to be the receptacle of divine justice, where the Eternal would manifest His most merciful glory for the benefit of Adam, His first minor creature. (Adam occupied the top end of the altar, or the side of the circles facing North, and Cain occupied the side facing South.)

一日，亚当想与他的两个儿子一同向造物主献上一次神性的属灵祭献；但他的女性后裔因缺乏德能与属灵权能、又因缺乏支撑此类行动的力量与坚定，不能在场；因此亚当让这些女子远离他为其工所选之地四十五肘（cubits）之外。当一切齐备之后，亚当命定并祝圣他的幼子亚伯尔，作头一个来执行所拟祭献之属灵职司的人。亚伯尔立即预备自己去履行此本分。他设立了祭坛，或曰适当的诸圈，在其中心献上最初的馨香。这馨香乃是他自己的形体，在谦卑俯伏之中献于造物主为祭。同时，他把自己属灵的次者存有交托于永恒主，作神性公义的容器，永恒主将在其中为亚当——祂首位次者受造物——之益，彰显祂至为慈悲的荣耀。（亚当占据祭坛的上端，即诸圈朝北的一侧，加音占据朝南的一侧。）

After Abel fulfilled his spiritual functions as instructed, he withdrew from his prostrate position and told his father what he had learned from the Creator. Adam then put Abel in his position on the northern side, and, trembling, prostrated himself as Abel had done. When finished, he called his two sons, placing Abel on his right and Cain on his left, and in this position he related what he had learned from the Creator. "I want to let you know," he said, "in the name of the Eternal creator, that I have obtained grace before Him; His justice will no longer be upon me by the intermission and intervention of the act of worship of my son, Abel, by whom the Creator has granted holiness on my behalf. Come, my two sons, that I may share my joy with you, and tell you about the two feelings that I have just experienced – that of evil and goodness which make my reconciliation perfect with the Creator."

亚伯尔照所受的指示履行完其属灵职司后，便从俯伏之位起身，把他从造物主所领受的告诉他的父亲。亚当随即让亚伯尔立于他自己朝北一侧的位置，自己战兢地照亚伯尔所做的俯伏下去。完毕后，他叫来两个儿子，把亚伯尔置于其右、加音置于其左，在此位置上讲述他从造物主所领受的。他说：“我愿奉永恒之造物主的名让你们知晓：我已在祂面前蒙了恩宠；藉我儿亚伯尔敬拜之举的居间与介入，祂的公义必不再临于我身上——造物主已藉他为我赐下了圣洁。来罢，我的两个儿子，让我与你们分享我的喜乐，并把我方才所经历的两种感受——即那使我与造物主的修和得以完全的恶与善之感受——告诉你们。”

Then addressing himself to Cain, he said, "My first-born son, may your future works be like those of your younger brother. Learn from me that the Creator offers his trust without any distinction of temporal or spiritual origin, and that he grants all divine, spiritual, and superior power to whomever deserves it, and to whom it is due. May your will in the future, Cain, be the same as your brother's and like mine, which, from now on, will be inviolably that of the Creator."

接着，他转向加音说：“我的长子啊，愿你日后的工作像你弟弟的一样。你要从我学到：造物主赐下祂的信任，并不分属时或属灵的出身；祂把一切神性、属灵与上位的权能，赐给凡配得的、并当得的人。加音啊，愿你日后的意志，与你弟弟的、并与我的一样；而我的意志，从今以后必不可动摇地与造物主的意志相合。”

Example of the Double Ternary in the Preceding Ceremonial: Good and Evil
前述礼仪中双重三元数的例证：善与恶

This ceremonial act started in the middle of the day and lasted only about an hour. When the work was finished, the participants left, each for their normal destination, Cain alongside his sisters and Abel alongside his mother and father. This division of three persons on each side offers us an image too striking to overlook. The image is a true example of the separation of good and evil, and shows us again the three spiritual essences which make up the corporeal forms of apparent matter, as well as those of rational and irrational being. Join these two ternary numbers together and you will see, by its senary product, the number of divine creation, or the six thoughts of the Creator for universal, general, and particular creation. See if you will not find what the Scriptures teach; that three (3) are above, as three (3) are below. Look further to see which of the two ternary numbers represents evil. Finally, reflect on this matter and search within yourself for satisfactory convictions and conclusions.

这一礼仪之举始于正午，仅持续约一个时辰。工作完毕后，参与者各自离去，回到各自惯常的去处：加音与他的姊妹们同行，亚伯尔与他的父母同行。每一侧三人的这种划分，给了我们一个鲜明得不容忽视的形象。这形象是善恶分离的真实例证，并再次向我们显明那构成表面物质之形体、以及有理性与无理性存有之形体的三种属灵本质。把这两个三元数相合，你便会藉其「六元」之积，看见神性创造之数，即造物主为普世、总体与个别创造所怀的六个思想。看你是否会发现圣经所教导的：三（3）在上，正如三（3）在下。再进一步看：这两个三元数中，哪一个代表恶。最后，省思此事，在你自己里面寻求令人满意的确信与结论。

§61  Cain, Dismissed, Acts Diabolically against Abel
§61  加音被遣退后，魔性地敌对亚伯尔

Cain, having withdrawn to the place that Adam had designated for him, revealed to his two sisters the so-called insult that his father had made to him by taking away his birthright, to make him dependent on his younger brother, Abel, and to make him subordinate to Abel's will. Cain's two sisters encouraged him to use all his strength and power against his father and brother, even against the Creator who had allowed such a heinous thing to favor a younger brother who had amazed the good faith of their father and corrupted his good thought through the ceremony of false and unjust devotion.

加音退到亚当为他指定之处后，向他的两个姊姊揭露了他父亲对他所谓的侮辱：夺去他的长子名分，使他依附于其弟亚伯尔，并使他臣服于亚伯尔的意志。加音的两个姊姊怂恿他倾尽全力与权能去对付他的父亲和弟弟，甚至去对付那容许如此可憎之事发生的造物主——因为造物主偏爱一个幼弟，而这幼弟以虚假不义的虔敬礼仪蒙骗了他们父亲的诚信、败坏了他良善的思想。

Consequently, Cain conceived to carry out an act of worship to the false gods or princes of demons, so that they might give him a superior power like that given to his brother, Abel, by the Creator, and to avenge the supposed wrong that he had received from his father because of his brother. He asked his two sisters to be present, just as Abel and he had witnessed with their father's act. He sanctioned his younger sister to perform the same functions that Abel had carried out, and he followed in detail the ceremony that he had seen performed. And when it was his turn to prostrate himself, he placed his other sister in the position that he had at the altar, or in the circles, and prostrating himself, offered the form and life of Abel as sacrifice to the princes of demons (the form is the body and the life is the soul).

于是，加音起意向假神、即群魔之君行一次敬拜之举，好叫它们赐他一种如造物主赐其弟亚伯尔那样的上位权能，并为他因其弟而自认从父亲所受的冤屈复仇。他要两个姊姊在场，正如亚伯尔与他曾见证他们父亲之举一般。他授命他的妹妹执行亚伯尔所曾执行的同样职司，并逐一照他所见施行过的礼仪而行。当轮到他自己俯伏时，他把另一个姊姊安置在他先前在祭坛、或在诸圈中的位置上；他俯伏下去，把亚伯尔的形与生命献给群魔之君为祭（形即身体，生命即灵魂）。

§62  The Murder of Abel by Cain
§62  加音谋杀亚伯尔

After this ceremony, Abel went to his brother, Cain, who reproached him bitterly. Abel took this calmly and with humility, and answered him: "It is not against me nor your temporal father that you should direct your anger, but fight against yourself and against the one who is directing you at this moment, for I tell you that you have just performed a false and impious act of worship before the Eternal. The extent of your crime surpasses Adam's; you have offered your god of darkness a sacrifice which neither you nor he is free to do, and you have wrongly sought to shed the blood of the righteous for the justification of the guilty."

这礼仪之后，亚伯尔来到他哥哥加音那里，加音狠狠地斥责他。亚伯尔平静而谦卑地承受，回答他说：“你不该把怒气发在我、或你属时之父身上，倒要与你自己、并与此刻在指使你的那一位争战；因为我告诉你：你方才在永恒主面前行了一次虚假而不虔的敬拜。你罪的深重超过了亚当：你向你那黑暗之神献上了一个你和它都无权献的祭，你更错谬地企图倾流义人的血，以求为有罪者开脱。”

Abel then turned to find Adam and revealed to him all that had happened, which grieved his poor father very much and caused him the greatest consternation. Abel tried to console Adam and questioned him about his sadness and despondency, but Adam did not answer. It seemed as if he could see what was going to happen to his beloved son, and dared not tell him. Abel reassured Adam about all these worries saying firmly: "Father, what is decreed by the Creator for you and your descendants' benefit must come to pass, for good or for bad, because the general creation which you see is nothing but the place that the Eternal has set aside to make manifest His omnipotence for His greatest glory. For that reason, father, the Creator will place suitable subjects into your bodily descendants to be His true instruments for the triumph of His justice, to the advantage of the good, and to the shame of the wicked. It is useless for man to go against what the Creator has planned for or against His spiritual creature."

亚伯尔随即转身去找亚当，把所发生的一切都告诉了他，这使他可怜的父亲极为忧伤，陷入极大的惊惶。亚伯尔试图安慰亚当，问他为何悲伤沮丧，亚当却不作答。他仿佛能看见将要临到其爱子的事，却不敢告诉他。亚伯尔坚定地宽慰亚当，打消他这一切忧虑说：“父亲，造物主为你和你后裔之益所判定的，无论善恶，都必成就；因为你所见的总体创造，无非是永恒主为彰显祂的全能、成就祂至大的荣耀而留出之地。为此缘故，父亲，造物主必把合适的对象置于你肉身的后裔中，作祂公义得胜的真正器具，使善者得益、使恶者蒙羞。人若违抗造物主为其属灵受造物所定的（无论是为之还是敌之），都属徒然。”

Adam appeared calm, and, speaking to the Creator, said, "O Thou Eternal! May what is conceived by Thy thought and Thy will be accomplished by Thy faithful servant, father of the many nations which will inhabit and operate in Thy universal circle! Amen." Adam and Abel then visited Cain, who greeted them with his two sisters. The daughters embraced their father, and Cain embraced his brother, Abel, but in this embrace, Cain struck Abel three times with a wooden instrument shaped like a dagger. The first blow pierced his throat, the second his heart, and the third his stomach. This murder took place in Adam's presence without this poor father realizing it. But as soon as the murder had been committed, Adam felt a terrible shock.

亚当显得平静，对造物主说：“永恒主啊！愿你思想与意志所构想的，藉你忠信的仆人——那将居住并运作于你普世之圈中的众多民族之父——得以成就！阿们。”随后，亚当与亚伯尔去探望加音；加音与他的两个姊姊迎接他们。两个女儿拥抱她们的父亲，加音则拥抱他的弟弟亚伯尔；然而就在这拥抱之中，加音用一件形如匕首的木器击打了亚伯尔三下。第一下刺穿他的咽喉，第二下刺穿他的心，第三下刺穿他的腹。这桩谋杀发生在亚当面前，而这可怜的父亲却毫未察觉。但谋杀一经犯下，亚当便感到一阵可怕的震动。

Cain's and Abel's two sisters experienced the same, and all three, struck by this shock, fell on the ground shouting, "Our conciliator, O Lord, is taken away from us by the hand of the impious, we call for Thy justice and to Thee alone we will leave our revenge!" (See with what guile the demon's subjects disguised themselves in the eyes of the creature by supposed spiritual and laudable words.) This shock, although quite natural among the aforementioned, and based on the sympathy of their material senses, came from yet another cause, which was the same cause that had brought them down. It came from their perception of Abel's spiritual major and minor, and which they could not withstand without fainting.

加音与亚伯尔的两个姊姊也经历了同样的震动；三人都被这震动击中，仆倒在地，喊道：“上主啊，我们的和解者被不虔者的手夺走了；我们呼求你的公义，并单单把我们的复仇交托于你！”（且看魔鬼的属下何等狡诈地以貌似属灵而可嘉的言辞在受造物眼前伪装自己。）这震动，在上述之人中间虽相当自然，且基于其物质感官的同感，却出于另一个缘由——正是那使他们堕落的同一缘由。它出于他们对亚伯尔属灵之高位者与次者的觉知，而他们不昏厥便无法承受。

Adam stood up first and went back along with Abel's spiritual minor and major to find Eve, to whom he told everything that the Creator had required from him for his total reconciliation; that his crimes had just been atoned for by Abel's sacrifice, and that all was thus consummated. I will leave you to think about what the sorrow must have been for this poor father and his companion. Aren't these the famous brambles which pierced Adam's heart? Isn't this the fatal bramble produced on Earth by Adam's transgression? And so it is Eve who produced in Cain the instrument of poor Adam's curse, having conceived with him this son by an act of confusion, as the number two (2) shows us, and which I shall explain in detail here and with sincerity.

亚当首先起身，带着亚伯尔属灵的次者与高位者回去找厄娃，把造物主为他完全的修和而向他所要求的一切都告诉了她：他的诸罪方才已藉亚伯尔的祭献得了赎，一切于是完成了。这可怜的父亲与其伴侣的忧苦何等深重，我留给你去思想。这岂不正是那刺入亚当之心的著名荆棘吗？这岂不正是亚当的悖逆在大地上所生出的致命荆棘吗？如此，正是厄娃在加音里造出了这可怜之亚当受咒诅的器具——她曾藉一桩「混乱之举」与他孕育了这个儿子，正如数字「二」（2）向我们所显明的；对此我将在此真诚地详加解释。

§63  Two, Number of Confusion
§63  二，混乱之数

The number of confusion is the one that directs what we call the simple and particular operation, which is carried out by the minor's will with the diabolical spiritual major. These two subjects are one through the intimate connection of their thought, intent, and action. However, they are always two separate subjects in that they are always susceptible to disunity, which happens when a more powerful mediator comes between them and carries out an opposite reaction. In this way, a considerable change comes into being for the benefit of the minor in containing the action of the diabolical major. And so, it is the joining with this diabolical being that we call the operation of confusion, and which we illustrate by the number 2.

混乱之数，乃是主导我们所称「单纯而个别之运作」的那个数；这运作是由次者的意志与那魔性的属灵高位者一同施行的。这两个对象藉其思想、意向与行动的紧密联结而合为一。然而，它们始终是两个分立的对象，因为它们始终易于分离；当一个更有力的居间者插入其间、施行一种相反的反作用时，分离便发生。如此，一个重大的变化便为次者之益而生，把那魔性高位者的行动加以约束。因此，与这魔性存有的联合，正是我们所称的「混乱之运作」，我们以数字「2」来表征它。

§64  Two, Three, Four
§64  二、三、四

You may well ask, when the minor itself is joined to the good spiritual major, whether this also makes the number 2 or confusion. But my answer is no, seeing that the good spirit which joins itself with a minor cannot do so unless it has first communicated its spirit intellect to the minor, which we call the minor spiritual power, and thereby prepares the particular minor soul to receive impressions from the good major spirit, according to the will and desire of this same major spirit and particular minor. The soul, by this junction, acquires the number 2, which, joining with the spirit, forms a ternary number – namely, the innate power of the first minor which is the soul (1); the minor power of the intellect (2); and the direct power of the major spirit (3).

你大可发问：当次者本身与那善的属灵高位者联合时，这是否也构成数字「2」、即混乱？我的回答是：不。因为那与次者联合的善灵，除非先把它的灵之智识传通给次者——我们称之为「次者的属灵权能」——从而按这同一高位之灵与该个别次者的意志与愿望，预备该个别次者的灵魂去领受善的高位之灵的印象，否则它便无法如此联合。灵魂藉这一联结获得数字「2」；此数与灵相合，便构成一个三元数——即：首位次者的内具权能，也就是灵魂（1）；智识的次位权能（2）；以及高位之灵的直接权能（3）。

This is how the soul of the minor makes the ternary number in its first principle of spiritual junction. We do not count the joining of the soul with the diabolical intellect and then with the bad spirit in the same way because, in this joining, the soul entirely abandons its good spiritual power to become itself a demon's intellect; whereas in this joining with the good spirit, the soul maintains and strengthens its divine spiritual power, which consequently deserves to be counted in this numbering. When the good major spirit receives its immediate action from Divinity, the soul consequently has its regular connection with the four divine powers, which we call quadruple essence as follows: the minor soul, 1, which is in spiritual connection with the intellect, 2, intellect with the spirit, 3, and the spirit with Divinity, 4. This demonstrates the precise correspondence of every spiritual being with the eternal Creator.

这便是次者的灵魂在其属灵联结的第一本原中构成三元数的方式。灵魂与魔性智识、继而与恶灵的联合，我们不以同样方式计数；因为在这种联合中，灵魂完全舍弃了它善的属灵权能，使自己成为魔鬼的智识；而在与善灵的这种联合中，灵魂却保守并强化了它神性的属灵权能，因此配被计入此计数之中。当善的高位之灵从神性领受其直接行动时，灵魂便因此与四种神性权能有其规律的联结，我们称之为「四重本质」，如下：次者灵魂（1）与智识（2）有属灵的联结，智识与灵（3），灵与神性（4）。这表明每一属灵存有与永恒造物主之间精确的对应。

Furthermore, I want to bring to your attention the correspondence of the heart of man with every spiritual being. Man's body is the organ of the soul and, through its use, the minor makes his intent and will of spiritual action known to others by the different movements and actions that he performs. The soul of the minor is the organ of the intellect, intellect is the organ of the major spirit, and the major spirit is the organ of the divine Creator. Such is the beautiful organic harmony of the principal divine spiritual beings, either of man's particular form or of the general and universal form, and this teaches us with certainty that everything has truly been emanated from the first necessary Being to every other being, spiritual or temporal.

此外，我要提请你注意人心与每一属灵存有之间的对应。人的身体是灵魂的器官；藉身体之用，次者以其所行的种种动作与行动，把他属灵行动的意向与意志向他人显明。次者的灵魂是智识的器官，智识是高位之灵的器官，高位之灵是神性造物主的器官。这便是那些主要神性属灵存有——无论是人的个别之形，还是总体与普世之形——美妙的有机和谐；这确切地教导我们：一切确乎是从那首要的「必然存有」流溢到每一其他存有（无论属灵还是属时）的。

In fact, by the numbers I use, you should come to know the triple and quadruple divine essence. These numbers are those that the Eternal Himself made use of to carry out general, particular, and universal creation, and the emanation of spirits, whether those who have become evil or those who have maintained the purity of their divine spiritual nature. The ternary number will make known the ternary unity of spirituous essences which the Creator used for the creation of different apparent material forms, and the quaternary number will make known the divine spiritual number the Creator used for the spiritual emanation of every living spiritual being, which are the major spirits given to the Christ and to the demons and minors in privation who have fallen under their power.

事实上，藉我所用的这些数字，你应当得以认识三重与四重的神性本质。这些数字正是永恒主亲自用以施行总体、个别与普世创造、并流溢诸灵（无论是已变为恶的，还是保守了其神性属灵本性之洁净的）所用的。三元数将使人认识造物主用以创造种种不同表面物质之形的「三种精气本质的三元合一」，四元数将使人认识造物主用以属灵地流溢每一活的属灵存有所用的神性属灵之数——即赐给基督、并赐给群魔与那些落于其权下、处于匮乏中之次者的高位诸灵。

§65  Virtue of Numbers
§65  数字的德能

It is this virtue of numbers which has led sages of all ages to state that no man can be learned without knowledge of numbers, whether in matters of the spiritually divine, the celestial, the earthly, or general and particular. Knowledge of the laws of spiritual nature is one thing; knowledge of the order and conventions of material man is something else again. The laws of men change like the shadows; those of spiritual nature are immutable, everything being innate in them since their first emanation. These truths will become more evident as we go along in this treatise.

正是数字的这种德能，使历世历代的贤者都断言：人若不通晓数字，便不能算有学问，无论是在属灵神性之事、天界之事、属地之事，还是总体与个别之事上。通晓属灵本性的律法是一回事，通晓物质之人的秩序与成规又是另一回事。人的律法如影子般变迁，属灵本性的律法却不可更易，一切自其最初流溢之时便内具于其中。随着本论的推进，这些真理将愈发显明。

§66  Numbers
§66  数字总表

1. Unity, first principle of every being, whether spiritual or temporal, belonging to the divine Creator. 2. Number of confusion belonging to woman. 3. Number belonging to Earth or man. 4. Quadruple divine essence. 5. Diabolical spirit. 6. Daily operations. 7. Holy spirit belonging to the septenary spirits. 8. Doubly powerful spirit belonging to the Christ. 9. Diabolical belonging to matter. 10. Divine number.

1. 「一」（Unity），每一存有（无论属灵还是属时）的第一本原，属于神性造物主。2. 「混乱之数」，属于女人。3. 属于大地或人之数。4. 四重神性本质。5. 魔性之灵。6. 日常的运作。7. 圣神之灵，属于「七元诸灵」（septenary spirits）。8. 双重大能之灵，属于基督。9. 魔性的，属于物质。10. 神性之数。

§67  Abel Explained to Adam and Eve by the Eternal
§67  永恒主向亚当与厄娃解释亚伯尔

Let us continue with Adam and Eve's reconciliation. Having experienced the cruel pain of which we have spoken and not seeing anything positive about what this event foreshadowed, either for themselves, their first descendants, or those to come, Adam and Eve prostrated themselves in the greatest sorrow and faith before the Creator to ask for His grace and mercy for the crime that Cain had committed on their son, Abel. Having neither the power nor the strength to seek revenge by their own authority, through bloodshed against the guilty one, they knew that vengeance belongs only to the Creator. The Eternal answered Adam and Eve's prayers and lamentations upon the death of their son, Abel; he sent them a spiritual interpreter who appeared before them and explained the nature of the crime committed by Cain, saying:

让我们继续讲述亚当与厄娃的修和。他们经历了我们所说的残酷痛苦，又看不出这一事件对他们自己、对他们最初的后裔、或对将来之人预示着任何积极之事；于是亚当与厄娃怀着至深的忧苦与信心俯伏于造物主面前，为加音在他们儿子亚伯尔身上所犯之罪，求祂的恩宠与慈悲。他们既无权能、也无力量凭自己的权柄、藉向有罪者流血来复仇，便知道复仇单单属于造物主。永恒主垂听了亚当与厄娃在其子亚伯尔之死上的祈祷与哀哭；祂差来一位属灵的诠释者，显现在他们面前，解释加音所犯之罪的性质，说：

"You are right to look upon Abel's death as a great loss and as a sign of God's wrath which must fall upon your descendants until the end of time. You should also consider it as the remaining curse of divine justice for the complete forgiveness of your first crime and your perfect reconciliation. But the Creator, who knows your sincere change of heart and submission, has sent me to soothe your pain and alarm about this unfortunate event which you see as irreparable."

“你们把亚伯尔之死看作巨大的损失、并看作天主之愤怒的征兆——那愤怒必临到你们的后裔直到时间终结——这是对的。你们也当把它看作神性公义为完全宽赦你们第一桩罪、并使你们完全修和而余下的咒诅。但造物主既知你们真诚的回心转意与顺服，便差我来抚慰你们的痛苦，并平息你们对这桩你们视为无可挽回之不幸事件的惊恐。”

"The Creator wants you to know, through my message, that you have both produced Abel's posterity only to be the true example of the one who will come in time to be the genuine and sole reconciler of all of your descendants. You should both know that Cain, whom you rightly consider a criminal, is no more a criminal than Adam was towards the Creator. Cain only struck matter, but Adam took God's throne by force; see which is the more criminal! Your son Cain is still an example of the transgression of the perverse spirits who seduced Adam and who really gave him spiritual death in plunging his minor being into a form of passive matter, which made him susceptible to divine privation and changed his glorious form to a material one subject to destruction, without being able to return to its first state of apparent form after its reintegration in the first principle of apparent forms, which the central axis will dissipate as quickly as it forms. Be firm and persevere in your trust in the Eternal; the term of your reconciliation is fulfilled."

“造物主愿你们藉我的讯息知晓：你们二人所生的亚伯尔之后裔，只是为作那一位——将在时候满足时前来、作你们一切后裔真正而唯一的和解者——的真实榜样。你们二人都当知道：加音，你们理当视为罪犯者，其罪并不比亚当对造物主之罪更重。加音只击打了物质，而亚当却强夺了天主的宝座；看哪，哪一个罪更重！你们的儿子加音，仍是那些诱惑亚当之悖逆诸灵的一个例证——它们实在给了亚当属灵的死亡：把他的次者存有抛入一个被动物质之形，使他易于陷入神性匮乏，并把他荣耀之形变为一个会被毁灭的物质之形，以致在其于「表面诸形之第一本原」中被重新融合之后，也无法返回其最初的表面之形之态——那本原，中央之轴将随其成形之速而将其消散。你们要坚定，在对永恒主的信靠中持守；你们修和的期限已经满足。”

Adam replied, "May the will of my Creator be mine!"

亚当回答说：“愿我造物主的意志成为我的意志！”

First Type of Preceding Events: The Major Spirits
前述事件的第一层预表：高位诸灵

I will now begin an explanation of the true types which make up all of the events that I have described. Adam, by his temporal posterity, can be taken as the Creator, and Adam's temporal posterity can be taken as the spirits that the Creator had emanated from Him for His greatest glory and spiritual devotion. You have seen that these spirits can consider themselves as Adam's elders, having emanated before him. You also know that having transgressed, these spirits were cast out from the Eternal presence, and that He emanated and emancipated from His divine immensity a minor spiritual being to contain them in privation. This minor, whom we call Adam, or Reaux, was therefore only the second-born spiritually from these first spirits and, like them, came from the divine Father, Creator of all things.

现在我要开始解释那构成我所描述之一切事件的真正预表。亚当，藉其属时的后裔，可被看作造物主；而亚当属时的后裔，可被看作造物主为其至大荣耀与属灵虔敬而从祂流溢出来的诸灵。你已看到，这些灵可自视为亚当的尊长，因为它们在他之先受流溢。你也知道，这些灵既已悖逆，便被逐出永恒主的临在之外；而祂从其神性无量界中流溢并解放独立出一个次者属灵存有，以把它们约束在匮乏之中。因此，这位次者——我们称之为亚当，或王者之人 Reaux——在属灵意义上只是这些最初诸灵之后「第二个所生的」，且与它们一样，都出于神性之父、万有的造物主。

I want to point out to you that Cain, Adam's eldest son, is an example of the first spirits emanated by the Creator, and that his crime is of the kind that the first spirits committed against the Eternal. Abel, Adam's second-born, by his innocence and holiness, is the example of Adam emanated after these first spirits in his first state of divine righteousness and glory. The destruction of Abel's body, which was carried out by his elder brother Cain, is the kind of operation that the first spirits carried out to destroy the glorious form in which the first man was clothed and, by this means, made him susceptible to be like them in divine privation. Here then is the true explanation of the first example that Adam, Cain, and Abel demonstrated by the regrettable events which happened to them.

我要向你指明：加音，亚当的长子，是造物主所流溢之最初诸灵的一个例证，他的罪属于最初诸灵得罪永恒主的那一类。亚伯尔，亚当次子，以其天真与圣洁，是那在这些最初诸灵之后、以其最初神性公义与荣耀之态受流溢之亚当的例证。亚伯尔身体的被毁——由其兄加音所施行——正是最初诸灵为毁灭首位之人所披的荣耀之形、并藉此使他易于像它们一样陷于神性匮乏所施行的那类运作。这便是亚当、加音与亚伯尔藉临到他们身上的种种可悲事件所展示之第一层例证的真正解释。

Second Type of Preceding Events: The Triangular Earth
前述事件的第二层预表：三角形的大地

The second thing represented by these three minors is no less significant, either by the correlation that they have with every celestial and terrestrial corporeal being, or by the events that they announced would occur to the first man's descendants. To be convinced of this, we only have to note that Adam, by the three spirituous principles which make up his form of apparent matter and its inherent proportions, is the exact image of the general earthly temple, which we know to be an equilateral triangle, as we shall demonstrate later.

这三位次者所代表的第二样事物同样意味深长——无论是就他们与每一天界及地界形体存有的关联而言，还是就他们所预告将临到首位之人后裔的事件而言。要确信此点，我们只须注意：亚当，藉那构成其表面物质之形及其内在比例的三种精气本原，正是「总体之地上圣殿」的精确形象；而我们知道，这圣殿乃是一个等边三角形，这一点我们将在后文加以证明。

§70  Power to Adam's Corporeal Vegetation
§70  亚当形体的植生之能

Adam had corporeal vegetation within his power, just as it is in Earth's nature to vegetate. Adam could produce only two sorts of vegetation, the masculine and the feminine. Similarly, the earth can produce only two species of vegetation, whether in passive animals or in plants. But I shall teach you that, besides the power that the body of man has to reproduce itself in corporeal form, it also has the means to produce passive animals which are really innate in the substance of this material form. How this is shall be explained next.

亚当在其权能之内握有形体的植生之能，正如植生本是大地的本性。亚当只能产生两种植生，即雄性与雌性。同样，大地也只能产生两类植生，无论是在被动的动物中还是在植物中。但我要教导你：除了人的身体在形体上自我繁衍之能以外，它还具有产生被动动物的方法，这些被动动物其实内具于这物质之形的实质之中。此事如何，容下文解释。

§71  From the Putrefaction of Forms
§71  论诸形的腐化

When the spiritual being has left its form, this form putrefies. After this putrefaction is complete, corporeal beings called reptiles emerge from this form and survive until the first three spirituous principles that have cooperated to make up the corporeal form of man are reintegrated. You must not think that this putrefaction occurs of itself nor directly from the corporeal form, but realize that the seminal fluid of all things subject to vegetation is innate within the envelope, be it terrestrial or aquatic. Man's body, having come from the general earth and having innate within its material form the three principles which have cooperated to form his earthly or aquatic envelope, there no doubt exists within this particular form yet another animal seminal fluid that is likely to vegetate. It is through this seminal fluid that putrefaction occurs in the body after what we commonly call death.

当属灵存有离开其形体时，这形体便腐化。腐化完成之后，被称为「爬虫」（reptiles）的形体存有便从这形体中出来，并存活到那协力构成人之形体的最初三种精气本原被重新融合之时为止。你切不可以为这腐化是自行发生、或直接从形体而来的；要知道：一切受制于植生之物的「种液」（seminal fluid），都内具于其外壳之中，无论那外壳属地还是属水。人的身体既出自总体之大地，又在其物质之形中内具那协力构成其属地或属水外壳的三本原，那么在这个别之形中，无疑还存有另一种可能植生的动物种液。正是藉着这种液，身体才在我们通常所称的「死亡」之后发生腐化。

The seminal ovaries throughout the entire body are knocked together by the reaction of the three principles we call sulphur, salt, and mercury, operating for their reintegration. In this way, the ovaries again receive an elementary warmth, stripping the reptile of its envelope, and this envelope, once dissolved, connects itself intimately with the cadaver's damp material. The joining of this reptile envelope with the putrefied cadaver produces the general decay of man's body and finally ends its apparent form. It is therefore always by the reaction of the three operating principles that putrefaction comes about, and this putrefaction brings about the explosion of reptile animals whose seminal fluid is scattered throughout man's body. It is absolutely vital that this last work be done, and this is what is called the pain or toil of the body.

遍布全身的「种卵」（seminal ovaries），因我们称为硫、盐、汞（sulphur, salt, mercury）之三本原为其重新融合而运作的反作用，而彼此撞合。如此，种卵重又获得一种元素之暖，剥去爬虫的外壳；这外壳一经溶解，便与尸体潮湿的质料紧密相连。这爬虫外壳与腐化尸体的联合，造成人身体的普遍腐坏，最终终结其表面之形。因此，腐化总是藉这三种运作本原的反作用而发生；而这腐化又带来爬虫动物的迸发，这些爬虫的种液散布于人的全身。这最后一项工作绝对必须完成，这便是所谓身体的「痛苦」或「劳苦」。

The life and activity that the animals have in the base fluid come from no other source than the central fiery axis, which, through its last operation, gets rid of all of the impurities surrounding the three spirituous essences which are still within the cadaver. The elementary fire, along with the central fire, maintains the apparent form of these reptiles by the refraction of their rays of spirituous fire, which consequently fold back into themselves when they find no more fluid to operate. In explaining to you how these reptiles have life, I mean that they have the same general life and corporeal form as all irrational animals which receive their being only through these two fires. This is enough about putrefaction; let me continue with an explanation of the types of children that Adam had.

这些动物在其基质流液中所有的生命与活动，别无他源，惟出于「中央火轴」（central fiery axis）；这火轴藉其最后一次运作，除去环绕那仍在尸体内之三种精气本质的一切杂质。元素之火连同中央之火，藉其精气之火之射线的折射，维系着这些爬虫的表面之形；当这些射线再找不到可运作的流液时，便随即折返于自身之内。我向你解释这些爬虫如何有生命，意思是：它们与一切无理性的动物有着同样的普遍生命与形体，而这些动物惟藉这两种火领受其存在。关于腐化，言此已足；让我继续解释亚当诸子女的预表。

§72  Cain, An Example of the Seduction of Bad Spirits
§72  加音，恶灵诱惑的例证

Besides the type of transgression of the first spirits and their victorious attack on the first man, Cain is again a classical example of the impious and fatal seduction that bad spirits will exercise upon the future generations of Adam, as they had just done in his first generation. After his transgression, Cain was obliged to go live with his two sisters in the southern part where he was relegated permanently by the Creator's command and by Adam's authority. This is the type of place to which the demons had been relegated so as to exercise their evil will and intent either against the Creator or the minors of both sexes, who were liable to retain some impression of the diabolical intellect.

除了作为最初诸灵之悖逆、并它们得胜地攻击首位之人的预表以外，加音又是一个典型的例证，表明恶灵将对亚当未来的世代施行何等不虔而致命的诱惑，正如它们方才在其第一个世代中所行的一般。加音悖逆之后，不得不与他的两个姊妹前往南方居住；他奉造物主之命、并凭亚当的权柄，被永久贬放于此。这正是群魔被贬放之地的预表——群魔在此施展其恶意与恶念，或与造物主为敌，或与两性的次者为敌，因为这些次者容易存留某种魔性智识的印象。

This southern place is again an example of the universal part where the Creator will manifest His justice and glory at the end of time. The southern part, having been cursed by the Creator, is marked by the Scriptures as the refuge of the majors and minors who have transgressed. I will say, moreover, that the three persons, Cain and his two sisters, by their ternary number, indicate the transgression of the earthly corporeal form of man, which the diabolical intellect seduces through its conjunction with the three spirituous principles making up every corporeal form. It is from these three subjects that we come to the nonary number (the number nine), of prevaricating matter, either of the demons or of the minors, as I will explain to you.

这南方之地，又是那普世之部分的例证——在时间的终结，造物主将在其中彰显祂的公义与荣耀。南方之部分既受造物主咒诅，圣经便标之为悖逆之高位者与次者的避难所。此外我要说：加音与其两个姊妹这三人，藉其三元数，指示了人属地形体的悖逆；魔性智识藉与那构成每一形体之三种精气本原的结合，诱惑了这形体。正是从这三个对象，我们得出那「悖逆之物质」（无论属魔还是属次者）的九元数（数字九），我将向你解释。

The Three Principles of Forms: Sulphur, Salt, and Mercury
诸形的三本原：硫、盐、汞

You know that the ternary number is given to Earth, or to the general form, and to the corporeal forms of its inhabitants, as well as to the celestial inhabitants. This ternary number originates from the three substances which make up any form whatsoever and which we call the spirituous principles of sulphur, salt, and mercury, emanated from the imagination and intent of the Creator. The central axis has placed and operated these three principles, produced in a state of indifference, to allow them to take on a more strengthened or consistent form, and from this action of the central axis come all corporeal forms in the same way that the perverse spirits garb themselves for their greatest subjection. It is also from these same substances that the corporeal forms of Cain and his two sisters are composed.

你知道，三元数赐予大地（即总体之形）、赐予其居民的形体、并赐予天界的居民。这三元数源于那构成任何形体之物的三种实质，我们称之为硫、盐、汞的精气本原，它们从造物主的想象与意向流溢而出。中央之轴安置并运作这三种以「无别之态」产生的本原，使它们能够取得一种更为强固或坚实之形；一切形体都从中央之轴的这一作为而来，正如悖逆诸灵为其至深的受制而披上外衣一般。加音与其两个姊妹的形体，也正是由这同样的实质所构成。

§74  Of the Nonary Number
§74  论九元数

In regard to the nonary number, I will say that it is not surprising that the major perverse spirits and their agents prefer to bind themselves more willingly to the bodily form of man than to any other, since this human form had first been destined for them. A proof of the intimate liaison of the evil spirits with man's body can be found in the words of the Christ to his apostles at the end of his last temporal operation on the Mount of Olives. When he came back to join them, he found them asleep and, waking them up, said, "Do not sleep, for the flesh is weak and the spirit is quick." It is the ease with which the evil spirit communicates with the bodily form of man that allowed the spirituous principles within these three persons' forms to be corrupted. The diabolical intellect wormed its way into and joined itself entirely with their form, and seduced the spiritual agent within who directs and governs this form according to the Creator's will.

论到九元数，我要说：主要的悖逆诸灵及其爪牙宁愿更甘心地把自己束缚于人的形体、而非任何其他形体之上，这并不足为奇；因为这人之形本是最先为它们所注定的。恶灵与人身体紧密勾连的一个明证，可见于基督在橄榄山（Mount of Olives；和合本：橄榄山）上其末次属时运作之终，对其宗徒（apostles；和合本：使徒）所说的话。当祂回来与他们会合时，见他们睡着了，便唤醒他们说：“不要睡了，因为肉体软弱，心灵却是切愿的。”正是恶灵与人形体传通之易，使这三人形体中的精气本原得以被败坏。魔性智识钻入并全然联于他们的形体，诱惑了那按造物主意志导引并治理此形的属灵施动者。

There was among them only one intent, one thought, and one action. Never had such a union been seen among men throughout the centuries, and it is impossible that three different and free persons can act in this way if they are not reconciled and led by a good or bad spirit. It is therefore from these three people, possessed by the prince of demons, that we derive the nonary number of matter, namely by adding the three spirituous principles or first essences: their three virtues and their three demoniacal powers. Firstly, three principles to Cain, three to his elder sister, and three to his younger sister, making nine; secondly, three virtues each, making nine; thirdly, three powers each, making nine.

在他们中间只有一个意向、一个思想、一个行动。历世历代人间从未见过这样的联合；三个各异而自由的人，若非被一个善灵或恶灵所修和、所引领，绝不可能如此行动。因此，正是从这三个被群魔之君所附的人，我们得出物质的九元数，即把三种精气本原、或曰第一本质相加：他们的三种德能与三种魔性权能。其一，加音三本原、其姊三本原、其妹三本原，共九；其二，各三德能，共九；其三，各三权能，共九。

To convince you that the nonary number of matter comes from the three minors, we have only to see their first diabolical action and how they perpetuated their criminal works until the punishment that the Creator exercised over their entire generation – punishment in which the Scriptures tell us that the Eternal had struck the entire earth and its inhabitants by floods, and by this means destroyed the guilty generation of the three minors, as well as men that they had seduced. It is since this period that the nonary number has come to our knowledge, including its mysterious addition (three added three times gives the product 27; 2 and 7 summed make 9; 27 multiplied by 9 again gives 9; and however many infinite times this product is multiplied, it will always give 9).

要使你确信物质的九元数出自这三位次者，我们只须看他们最初的魔性行动、以及他们如何延续其罪恶之工，直到造物主施于其整个世代的惩罚——圣经告诉我们，永恒主在这惩罚中以洪水击打了整个大地及其居民，藉此毁灭了这三位次者有罪的世代，以及他们所诱惑的众人。正是自这一时期起，九元数才为我们所知，连同其奥秘的加法（三相加三次得积二十七；2 与 7 相加得 9；27 乘以 9 仍得 9；无论将此积乘以无穷多少次，其结果恒为 9）。

§75  Cain's Punishment
§75  加音的惩罚

I will tell you that Cain is the example of the choice of prophets that the Creator was to send amongst Cain's generation in the times which followed. You were told that when Cain had destroyed his brother Abel, he went back to his ordinary abode, where, reflecting upon his crime, a divine spiritual voice came to him and asked what he had done with his brother. Cain sharply replied, "Am I my brother's keeper?" After this response, the spirit made such an effect on his corporeal form or minor being that he was immediately overwhelmed and, in this situation, called upon the Creator, saying, "Lord, those who will meet me will kill me."

我要告诉你：加音是造物主日后要差往加音世代中之众先知拣选的预表。前面已告诉你：加音毁灭其弟亚伯尔之后，回到他惯常的居所；在那里，当他省思自己的罪时，一个神性属灵的声音临到他，问他把他弟弟怎么了。加音尖刻地回答：“我岂是看守我弟弟的吗？”这回答之后，那灵在他形体、即其次者存有上施以如此的作用，以致他顿时被压倒；在此境况中，他呼求造物主说：“上主啊，凡遇见我的，都必杀我。”

The Eternal, Father of mercy, considering Cain's consternation, and wishing to protect him from the reproach and revenge that his generation would be capable of exercising against him, ordered that he be marked with a protective seal, and the spirit which marked him thus said, "In the name of the Eternal, whosoever slayeth Cain shall be punished by death sevenfold." Then Cain withdrew with his sisters to the place where he had been sent by the Eternal. Here he had a generation of ten males and eleven females. He built a city that he named Enoch. To assist in this enterprise, he thought of the idea of digging into the bowels of the earth. He undertook this operation with his first-born, whom he called Enoch. He left his secret, either for the discovery of mines or the beating of metals, to his second son, called Tubalcain. From this, it has come to us that Tubalcain was the first to discover the smelting of metals.

永恒主，慈悲之父，鉴于加音的惊惶，又愿保护他免受其世代可能对他施行的责难与复仇，便下令给他打上一个护佑的印记；那给他打印记的灵如此说：“奉永恒主的名，凡杀加音的，必遭七倍的死亡之罚。”于是加音与他的姊妹们退到永恒主所遣他去的地方。他在此有了十个男丁、十一个女子的世代。他建了一座城，起名「哈诺客」（Enoch；和合本：以诺）。为助成此业，他想到了向地腹深掘的主意。他与他的长子——他称之为哈诺客——一同着手此项运作。他把或用于探矿、或用于锻打金属的秘诀，留给了他的次子，名叫「突巴耳加音」（Tubalcain；和合本：土八该隐）。由此传下：突巴耳加音是第一个发现冶炼金属的人。

§76  Cain Killed by His Son Boaz
§76  加音被其子波阿次所杀

Cain was a great hunter and he had also raised his male children in the art of hunting, especially his tenth son, of whom he was very fond. Cain named his tenth son Boaz which means "son of occision." It was this last son who killed his father Cain, which happened in the following manner. Cain, having resolved to go hunting for wild animals, accompanied by Enoch's two children, his grandchildren, did not tell his son Boaz about the hunting expedition that he had planned two days later. For his part, Boaz planned with his two nephews, sons of Tubalcain, to go hunting the same day as his father, but did not notify him either. Unknowingly, Boaz took the same route as his father, Cain, both being in the thick bushes, called Onam, meaning "sorrow," that they were accustomed to scouring.

加音是一个了不起的猎人，他也把他的众男丁培养于狩猎之术，尤其是他极钟爱的第十子。加音给他的第十子起名「波阿次」（Boaz），意为「杀戮之子」。正是这最末之子杀了他的父亲加音，事情是这样发生的。加音决意去猎捕野兽，由哈诺客的两个孩子、即他的两个孙子陪同；他没有把自己两天后所计划的这次狩猎告诉他的儿子波阿次。而波阿次这一边，则与他的两个外甥、即突巴耳加音的儿子们，计划在与其父同一天去打猎，也没有通知他父亲。波阿次不知不觉地走上了与其父加音相同的路线，二人都身在那片浓密的、名叫「敖南」（Onam，意为「忧苦」）、他们惯常搜猎的灌木丛中。

Then, Boaz saw the shadow of a figure. He let fly an arrow which pierced the heart of his father, having taken him for a wild animal. Imagine Boaz's surprise and shock when he went to the place that he had shot the arrow, and when he saw that he had killed his father by his own hand. His pain was all the greater because he knew the punishment and the threat that the Creator had leveled against the one who would strike Cain. Boaz called his two nephews and showed them the body. As soon as they recognized that it was Cain, they uttered a great cry of surprise and horror. After he had told them how he was the innocent cause of the destruction of the bodily form of his father, Cain, he said: "My friends, you are witnesses to my crime; although I have transgressed against the commands of the Creator unwillingly, I am guilty before the Eternal and before men. I am the youngest son of Cain, the last of his posterity. Avenge upon the person of this last-born the death of his father and the scandal that I have brought upon you."

这时，波阿次看见一个人影的影子。他把那影当作野兽，射出一箭，正刺穿了他父亲的心。想象一下波阿次的惊愕与震骇：当他走到自己射箭之处，看见自己竟亲手杀了父亲。他的痛苦格外深重，因为他知道造物主对那击打加音者所立下的惩罚与威吓。波阿次唤来他的两个外甥，指给他们看那尸体。他们一认出是加音，便发出一声既惊且骇的大喊。波阿次告诉他们自己如何无辜地成了毁灭其父加音形体的缘由之后，说道：“朋友们，你们是我罪行的见证；我虽是无意中违背了造物主的命令，却在永恒主面前、在世人面前有罪。我是加音最小的儿子，他后裔中的末一个。你们就在这末子身上，为其父之死、并为我给你们带来的祸事复仇罢。”

The diabolical intellect, which knows the weakness of men in trouble, immediately roused an excessive passion in Boaz's two nephews to avenge Cain's death. They each armed their bow with an arrow to shoot their uncle. But when they were ready to shoot him, a voice could be heard which said: "Whosoever shall slay the one who has killed Cain shall be punished by death seventy times sevenfold." Upon hearing this frightening divine spiritual voice, the two nephews fell to the ground, but as soon as they had come to, they took their weapons to Boaz saying: "The Creator has spared you, Boaz, for the death of your father. We are now the most guilty before the Eternal since we willingly conceived to carry out upon you our vindictive thought." Boaz answered his two nephews: "May the will of the Creator be done!"

那知晓身处困境之人软弱的魔性智识，立即在波阿次的两个外甥里面激起一种过度的情欲，要为加音之死复仇。他们各自搭箭于弓，要射他们的舅舅。但当他们正要射他时，可听见一个声音说：“凡杀那杀加音之人的，必遭七十个七倍的死亡之罚。”一听见这可怖的神性属灵之声，两个外甥便仆倒在地；然而他们一苏醒过来，便把兵器交给波阿次，说：“波阿次，造物主为你父之死饶了你。如今我们在永恒主面前才是最有罪的，因为我们甘心起意要在你身上施行我们复仇的念头。”波阿次回答他的两个外甥说：“愿造物主的旨意成就！”

After this acceptance from Boaz, they all went back to the city of Enoch. This pain increased even more when this generation learned that the death of their father Cain was committed by the last-born of this same father. Poor Boaz, having been crushed by a general enmity from Cain's first generation and their descendants, was forced to withdraw from this mob of possessed diabolical intellects, and took refuge in the desert of Jezanias, which means "listen to the Creator." Here Boaz ended his days in penance and contrition. This then is the classical example of prophecy, as shown when he said after the crime that he had committed against his brother Abel: "Those who will meet me, Lord, will kill me." Wasn't he met by his son in the thicket? Wasn't he, in fact, killed by a man, as he had said?
