波阿次这样应允之后，众人都回到哈诺客城。当这世代得知他们父亲加音之死竟是这同一父亲的末子所为时，这痛苦便更加剧。可怜的波阿次，被加音第一世代及其后裔普遍的仇恨所压垮，不得不从这群被魔性智识所附的乌合之众中退出，避入「耶匝尼雅」（Jezanias，意为「听从造物主」）的旷野。波阿次在此以补赎与痛悔了结了他的余生。这便是那预言的典型例证，正如加音在其对弟弟亚伯尔所犯之罪后所说：“上主啊，凡遇见我的，都必杀我。”他岂不是在灌木丛中被他的儿子遇见了吗？他岂不是果真如他所说，被一个人所杀了吗？

§77  An Error of Men of Our Times on the Last Patricide
§77  今人论这末次弑父的一个错误

I want to bring to your attention how ridiculous and absurd the observation is that men of our times make about Cain's patricide by his son Boaz. This type, unknown to the greatest part of men today, confirms their belief and even assures them that Adam was not the first man because, they say, when Cain killed his brother Abel, he said to the Creator, "Lord, what will become of me? Those who will meet me will kill me." If these men had been instructed about the one who uttered these words to the Creator, they would have clearly understood that they were words of a prophet and of prophecies to come, as we have already seen happen among men on Earth and to Cain himself.

我想提请你注意：今人就加音被其子波阿次弑杀一事所作的评说是何等荒唐悖谬。这一预表，为今日大多数人所不知，却坚固了他们的一种看法，甚至使他们确信亚当并非第一个人；因为他们说：加音杀其弟亚伯尔时，曾对造物主说：“上主啊，我将如何？凡遇见我的，都必杀我。”倘若这些人受过关于「向造物主说这些话之人」的教导，他们便会清楚明白：这些乃是先知之言、并将来预言之言，正如我们已看到这类事发生于地上众人、并发生于加音本人身上一般。

But, you may say, how could the Creator send prophets to men so as to limit their actions, when you say that the Creator takes no part in secondary causes? I will answer that the Creator is aware of the demoniacal thinking being who continually carries out seductive and pernicious acts for the spiritual minor, just as it had already taken place in the seduction of Adam and his generation. Consequently, the Creator judged it necessary, for the benefit of man, to elect spiritually minor beings and to endow them with the prophetic spirit, not only to contain man within the laws, precepts, and commands that He had given them, but also for the greatest molestation of evil spirits and for the manifestation of His greatest divine glory. The Eternal becomes aware of secondary causes by the good and bad thoughts and actions of a spiritual being before the Creator.

但你或许会说：你既说造物主不参与第二因，祂又怎能差先知给人、以限制他们的行动呢？我要回答：造物主知晓那不住地为属灵次者施行诱惑与毒害之举的「魔性思想存有」，正如在亚当及其世代所受的诱惑中已经发生的那般。因此，造物主为人之益，判定有必要拣选一些属灵次者存有，赋予他们先知之灵，不仅为把人约束在祂所赐的律法、诫命与命令之内，也为至大地搅扰恶灵、并彰显祂至大的神性荣耀。永恒主藉一个属灵存有在造物主面前的善恶思想与行动，而知晓第二因。

§78  Retreat of Boaz into the Desert of Jezanias
§78  波阿次退入耶匝尼雅旷野

Let us now see what type of retreat Boaz's flight into the desert of Jezanias represents. Boaz, being the last son of Cain's immediate generation, completes the denary number by his rank. There is no doubt that he was endowed with spiritually divine gifts to be an image and a real example of the great mercy that the Creator grants, in whatever circumstance, whether for the benefit of the spiritual minor being or for the benefit of the perverse major, when spirits sincerely call upon Him. You should understand very clearly the grace that the Creator granted Boaz, who was doubly criminal: firstly for having assisted in the worship to the demons rather than to the Creator; and secondly for having put his father Cain to death, and having contravened what the Creator forbade.

现在让我们看看波阿次逃入耶匝尼雅旷野这一退隐所代表的预表。波阿次既是加音直系世代的末子，便以其位次成全了十元数。毫无疑问，他被赋予了属灵神性的恩赐，为要作造物主所赐之大慈悲的形象与真实例证——无论在何种境况下，只要诸灵真诚地呼求祂，这慈悲便临到，无论是为属灵次者存有之益，还是为悖逆之高位者之益。你当极清楚地理解造物主赐予波阿次的恩宠；而波阿次是双重的罪人：其一，因他曾协助敬拜群魔而非造物主；其二，因他把父亲加音处死，违背了造物主所禁止的。

Why the Demons Attack the Bodies of Men Rather than Those of Beasts
群魔为何攻击人身而非兽身

The diabolic spirits attach themselves to the form of the minor rather than that of the brute, because the form of man is the image and general copy of the Creator's great work. Man bears upon his form the real image of the apparent form which appeared in the Creator's imagination. Those spirits attach themselves even more willingly to man's form, because it contains the minor spiritual being who is more powerful, and which they continually try to seduce and turn away from the Creator. This is why the prince of demons, through his intellectual spirits, gets them to attack the bodily form of man rather than beasts, because animals are not an image of the Creator's great work and they have no divine spiritual being on which demoniac spirits can make some impression.

魔性诸灵依附于次者之形，而非畜类之形，因为人之形乃是造物主伟大工作的形象与总体摹本。人在其形上承载着那曾显现于造物主想象中之表面形体的真实形象。这些灵更甘愿依附于人之形，因为其中含有那更为强大的次者属灵存有，它们不住地试图诱惑它、使它转离造物主。这便是群魔之君何以藉其智识诸灵，驱使它们去攻击人的形体、而非畜类；因为动物并非造物主伟大工作的形象，它们身上没有魔性诸灵可施加印象的神性属灵存有。

Three Sorts of Life in Man: Passive, Demoniacal, and Divine
人里面的三种生命：被动的、魔性的、神性的

The first is the life of matter, which we call instinct or passive life, and which is innate in the form of the rational animal as well as the irrational. The second is the demoniacal spiritual life, which can incorporate itself into the passive life, and the third is the divine spiritual life, which presides over the first two. You will not find the same thing among beasts; they have only a being of passive life issued from the divine spiritual operation of the central fiery axis. Such is the difference which remains between rational and irrational beings, and the reason that the demoniacal operations attack the corporeal forms of man rather than those of beasts. This is the digression that I intended to make, so as to instruct you about the most essential things that are of greatest consequence for the man of desire.

第一种是物质的生命，我们称之为本能或被动生命，它内具于有理性的动物之形、亦内具于无理性的动物之形。第二种是魔性的属灵生命，它能并入被动生命之中；第三种是神性的属灵生命，它统辖前二者。在畜类中你找不到同样之物；它们只有一种从中央火轴之神性属灵运作而出的被动生命存有。这便是有理性与无理性存有之间所存的差别，也是魔性运作攻击人之形体、而非兽之形体的缘由。这便是我所要作的题外话——为要就那些对渴慕之人（man of desire）关系至为重大的最要紧之事教导你。

§81  Man Is Freed Before the Demons
§81  人得以在群魔面前获释

It is by Boaz's retreat that we should understand that it is within the power of the spiritually divine minor to separate himself when he wishes from the possession and the connection that he has established with the prince of demons through the diabolic intellect. I will not enter here into the details of the different types which Cain's posterity represent because I will do so later. However, I should give you an explanation again of Abel's birth, just as I can give you some examples of a similar kind when the opportunity arises.

正是藉着波阿次的退隐，我们应当明白：属灵神性的次者有此权能——当他愿意时，便可脱离他藉魔性智识与群魔之君所建立的附着与联结。我在此不深入加音后裔所代表之种种预表的细节，因为我将于后文详述。然而，我应当再向你解释一次亚伯尔的诞生，正如我可在时机出现时给你一些同类的例证。

§82  Types of Abel
§82  亚伯尔的预表

I will tell you, therefore, that Adam and Eve cooperated to form their son Abel by a very succinct material operation; that is to say, without the excessive participation of their material senses. And so they completely submitted themselves to the Creator through a perfect and spiritual surrender. The Creator could not refuse to connect with their operation by composing within their produced form a minor being endowed with virtue and divine spiritual wisdom. This spiritual being was to be a type of the manifestation of divine justice for the benefit of the minors and to the shame of the demons, as he was to be the principal instrument of Adam's and Eve's reconciliation. Moreover, Abel is a true example of minors endowed with divine grace which the Creator brings among men to be the spiritual instruments of His justice, either for the reward or for the punishment of his creatures, according to whether their works conform to divine law or not.

因此我要告诉你：亚当与厄娃以一次极简略的物质运作协力形成了他们的儿子亚伯尔；也就是说，未让他们的物质感官过度参与。如此，他们藉一种完全而属灵的降服，把自己完全交托于造物主。造物主不能不与他们的运作相连——祂在他们所产之形中构成了一个被赋予德能与神性属灵智慧的次者存有。这属灵存有本要作神性公义之彰显的预表，为诸次者之益、并为群魔之羞辱，因为他本要作亚当与厄娃修和的主要器具。此外，亚伯尔是那些被赋予神性恩宠之次者的真实例证——造物主把这些次者带到世人中间，作祂公义的属灵器具，或赏赐、或惩罚祂的受造物，全看他们的作为是否合于神性律法。

Part IV — ENOCH
第四部 — 哈诺客（Enoch；和合本：以诺）
§83  Enoch, an Example of the Divine Spirit and Its Action
§83  哈诺客，神性之灵及其行动的例证

Among the minors destined for these kinds of spiritual operations, we shall first consider Enoch, the seventh of his generation who replaced Abel, namely the generation of Seth. He is by rank of birth a true example of the divine spirit for the support, conduct, and defense of the minors against persecution by the demons. By the mission, work, and worship that he professed, he is also an example of the true type of direct action of the doubly powerful spirit which, at that time, laid down for men the conduct necessary to protect them from attacks by their enemies. This same conduct was to guide them in their natural temporal and spiritual operations, and to serve as a fundamental basis in the rituals of their worship conforming to divinity.

在那些注定要行此类属灵运作的次者中，我们首先要论及哈诺客（Enoch；和合本：以诺）——他是接替亚伯尔之世代（即舍特 Seth；和合本：塞特 的世代）中第七位。他以其出生的位次，是神性之灵为扶持、引导并护卫诸次者抵御群魔逼迫的真实例证。藉着他所承担的使命、工作与敬拜，他也是那双重大能之灵直接行动之真实预表的例证；这灵在那时为世人立定了保护他们免受仇敌攻击所必需的行为准则。这同一准则要引导他们进行属自然、属时与属灵的运作，并要在他们合于神性的敬拜礼仪中充作根本的基础。

§84  An Examination of the Worship Practiced by Enoch
§84  考察哈诺客所行的敬拜

Let us examine, therefore, what worship Enoch practiced among the descendants of Seth. He was the first to build an altar of white stone that is different from what we call marble. Enoch received the fruit of his worship on the center of this altar and where he offered himself in sacrifice. It was he who prophesied the justice of the Creator to be fulfilled upon the face of the Earth in punishment of the crimes of Cain's generations and the remainder of Seth's generation, who joined with those of Cain. He determined the covenant of Seth's generation by forbidding the divine Creator's children to form liaisons with the children of men. It was Enoch who prophesied that the Creator's truly elected were to be born from the Eternal by electing ten individuals to promote the divine cult among Seth's generation.

那么，让我们考察哈诺客在舍特后裔中所行的敬拜。他是第一个用白石——不同于我们所称的大理石——筑坛的人。哈诺客在这坛的中心领受其敬拜的果实，并在那里把自己献为祭。正是他预言了造物主的公义将在地面上得以成就，以惩罚加音诸世代之罪、以及舍特世代中那些与加音之人相联合的余众。他确立了舍特世代的盟约，禁止神性造物主的子女与「人的子女」结合。（藉我就亚当悖逆及其后果所说的，你便会明白我所谓「人的子女」是何意。）正是哈诺客预言：造物主真正的选民将从永恒主而生，他为此拣选了十个人，以在舍特世代中推行神性的敬拜。

Enoch, who was simply a holy spirit in a corporeal form of apparent matter, held a divine spiritual assembly in the northern region. This generation he called the children of the Creator and, struck by the force of Enoch's sacred operations, they could not avoid calling him Enoch, the holy man, a name which means dedicated or devoted to the Creator. Consequently, he organized an election from among them of ten persons to whom he declared the Creator's will and set out a ceremony and code of life so that the Eternal could be invoked in sacredness. He then made them erect a building of only one room in which he and his ten chosen met to assist him in the sacred work. He gave each of them the first letter of the holy names of God, which made ten letters in all, allowing them to practice with regularity all kinds of operations favorable to the Creator and to the advantage of the reconciled minors. After this first act, he sent them back to their allocated tents, just as Moses had demonstrated by the encampment of the Levites around the Ark.

哈诺客不过是一个居于表面物质之形体中的圣灵，他在北方之地举行了一次神性的属灵集会。他称这一世代为「造物主的子女」；他们被哈诺客神圣运作之力所折服，不由得称他为「圣人哈诺客」——这名意为「奉献于、或献身于造物主」。于是，他在他们中间组织了一次拣选，选出十人，向他们宣告造物主的意志，并订立了一套礼仪与生活准则，好使永恒主得以在圣洁中被祈求。随后他叫他们竖起一座只有一间房的建筑，他与所选的十人在其中聚集，协助他行神圣之工。他把天主诸圣名的头一个字母各授一人，合共十个字母，使他们能有规律地施行各样有益于造物主、并有利于已修和之次者的运作。这第一桩举动之后，他打发他们回到各自分得的帐幕，正如梅瑟（Moses；和合本：摩西）藉肋未人（Levites；和合本：利未人）环绕约柜（the Ark；和合本：约柜）安营所展示的。

Every tenth week, Enoch held this meeting of divine work with his ten elect, and at each meeting, he gave them a new letter of the sacred name of God, so that after seven gatherings, each had two powerful words by which to command all created things from the terrestrial to the celestial planes. These two words consisted of seven letters, four of which formed the awesome, invincible, and powerful name of the Eternal, which governed and subjected every created being in the celestial realm, and the remaining three formed a holy name which subjected and ruled every created being in the earthly realm. These ten chiefs, their virtues and spiritual divine powers restored by Enoch, performed such wondrous sacred works that they converted several members of their family. They instructed minors truly called by the Holy Spirit in the sciences that they possessed through the power and ministry of Enoch, who was an example of the reconciliation of human kind.

每逢第十周，哈诺客便与他所选的十人举行这神性之工的聚会；每次聚会，他都授给他们天主圣名的一个新字母，以致七次聚集之后，各人都拥有两个大能之词，藉以命令从地界到天界的一切受造之物。这两个词由七个字母构成，其中四个构成永恒主那可畏、不可胜、大能的名，统辖并制伏天界一切受造存有；其余三个构成一个圣名，制伏并治理地界一切受造存有。这十位首领，其德能与属灵神性权能既已被哈诺客恢复，便行出如此奇妙的神圣之工，以致使其家族的若干成员归化。他们把自己藉哈诺客——人类修和的例证——之权能与职事所拥有的诸般学问，教导那些真正为圣神所召的次者。

§85  Revolt of the Sixth Disciple of Enoch
§85  哈诺客第六位门徒的叛离

In a short time, the number of converts became quite considerable, but these new converts did not maintain their virtues and powers. What perverted several of them was the atrocious conduct of one of their ten leaders admitted by Enoch to divine reconciliation and who provoked dissension amongst the others to spread mistrust about Enoch's teachings. This spirit of revolt increased so much among the newly called that they eventually abandoned the Creator entirely to the delight of matter and to the chief transgressor, and so no more than nine righteous men remained on Earth. These nine called upon their strength and knowledge that they had received from the holy Enoch, and begged him to meet with them again to replace one of their brothers that the demon had taken away.

不多时，归化者的数目便相当可观，然而这些新归化者并未保守住他们的德能与权能。使他们中若干人堕落的，是哈诺客所接纳进入神性修和之十位首领中一人的凶恶行为——此人在其余人中挑起纷争，散布对哈诺客教导的猜疑。这叛离之灵在新蒙召者中间大为增长，以致他们最终完全离弃造物主，转向物质之逸乐、并转向那首恶者；于是地上只剩下不多于九个义人。这九人求告他们从圣人哈诺客所领受的力量与知识，恳求他再与他们相会，以补上一位被魔鬼夺去的弟兄。

Enoch, receptive to their prayers, met with these nine to whom he communicated the entire secret. At this meeting, he elected the one to replace the transgressor, adding that the one chosen would enter into virtue and power only after they themselves had atoned for their sins. During this time, Enoch made his invocation to the Creator on behalf of the nine disciples who then saw all of the plagues that the Creator would summon to strike the earth and its inhabitants. They uttered a great cry at Enoch and said to him: "How is it, Master, that everything that we have just seen must happen on this earth? Could you not calm by your prayers the wrath of the One who sent you among us? The vision that we have had is not false; the Creator is just, and you are holy, strong, and invincible."

哈诺客垂听他们的祈祷，便与这九人相会，把全部的秘密都传通给他们。在这次聚会上，他拣选了那要接替叛离者之人，并补充说：所选之人惟有在他们自己为其罪作了补赎之后，才能进入德能与权能。这期间，哈诺客代九位门徒向造物主祈请；他们于是看见了造物主将要召来击打大地及其居民的一切灾祸。他们向哈诺客发出一声大喊，对他说：“师父，我们方才所见的一切竟都必临到这地上，这是怎么回事？你岂不能藉你的祈祷平息那差你到我们中间者的愤怒吗？我们所得的异象并非虚假；造物主是公义的，而你是圣洁、刚强、不可胜的。”

Enoch replied, "Who has thus instructed you about me? Be all one man, and you will be equally holy. Be all under the same law, and you will be strong. Be all under the same code of life which I have prescribed for you, and you will be eternal invincible minors. Such is the will of the Father and of the Holy Spirit upon his son. Always be the son of the Almighty here below, and you will know that the one named Enoch is the spirit of the Father who is above."

哈诺客回答说：“是谁这样教导你们认识我的？你们众人当合为一人，你们便同样圣洁。你们众人当在同一律法之下，你们便刚强。你们众人当在我为你们所定的同一生活准则之下，你们便成为永恒不可胜的次者。这正是圣父、并圣父之圣神加于其子的意志。你们在这下界要常作全能者之子，你们便会知道：那名叫哈诺客的，乃是在上之圣父的灵。”

§86  Enoch Taken to Heaven
§86  哈诺客被接升天

Enoch had scarcely finished speaking and blessing the nine disciples when a fiery cloud descended from Heaven and quickly raised him to carry this Holy Spirit away to his destination. His disciples, who had lost sight of him, lamented and said, "What is to become of us, O Eternal, without the help of our master Enoch? Why do you take him from his brothers and his disciples? If the earth is culpable, why are we corporeal men responsible if it is material blood only which we have received from the earth and which we leave to Thy holy justice? Grant, Lord, our wishes and have pity on Thy sons and servants."

哈诺客话音刚落、祝福完这九位门徒，一朵火云便从天而降，迅速把他举起，将这圣灵带往其归宿之处。他的门徒们见他不见了，便哀哭说：“永恒主啊，没有我们师父哈诺客的帮助，我们将成何样？你为何把他从他的弟兄与门徒中取去？倘若大地有罪，我们这些属形体之人又何须担责——既然我们从大地所领受的只是物质之血，而我们把这血交托于你圣洁的公义？上主啊，求你成全我们的心愿，怜悯你的儿子与仆人。”

§87  Noah Takes Up Enoch's Religion Again
§87  诺厄重拾哈诺客的宗教

Soon afterwards, Enoch was to be a new type, following the will of the Creator. The first principle of divine spiritual religion that he had established among Seth's posterity was preserved and reactivated by the vigor and power of Noah, who is yet another example of the spiritually elect for general and particular reconciliation. We will clearly see this when we examine his entering the Ark with the different kinds of animals, the tranquility and security of the Ark during the flood, the spiritual instructions that Noah gave to his legitimate sons, and finally his conduct safeguarding all those who had been entrusted to him against the terrible curse that God sent to exterminate the earth and all its other inhabitants.

不久之后，哈诺客又要按造物主的意志成为一个新的预表。他在舍特后裔中所确立的神性属灵宗教之第一本原，藉诺厄（Noah；和合本：挪亚）的活力与权能得以保存并重新激活；诺厄是又一个为总体与个别之修和而蒙拣选之属灵选民的例证。当我们考察他带着各类动物进入方舟、洪水期间方舟的安宁与稳固、诺厄给其合法诸子的属灵教诲、以及他护卫一切托付于他之人抵御天主为灭绝大地及其一切其他居民所降之可怕咒诅的行为时，我们便会清楚看见这一点。

The Duty of the Messiah and the 70 Years of Servitude
默西亚的职分与七十年的奴役

It is enough to note that what has just been said is sufficient to clearly see that the true Messiah has always been with God's children but unknown most of the time. We shall also find with this same explanation the interpretation of what the prophet Daniel meant in one of his prophecies when he spoke symbolically of the captivity of Israel for 70 weeks, converted to 70 years under Nebuchadnezzar, a prophecy confirmed by the bondage of the Israelites and from which they were delivered by the powerful actions of Zerubbabel after 70 years of servitude to which the Creator had condemned them for the errors that they had committed against God and their brothers.

只须注意：方才所说的已足以清楚看出：真正的默西亚（the Messiah）一直与天主的子女同在，却在大多数时候不为人知。藉这同一解释，我们也将寻得先知达尼尔（Daniel；和合本：但以理）在其一则预言中所指之意的诠释——他曾象征性地论及以色列（Israel）七十周的被掳，在拿步高（Nebuchadnezzar；和合本：尼布甲尼撒）治下转为七十年；这预言由以色列人的被奴役所证实，而他们在七十年奴役之后，藉则鲁巴贝耳（Zerubbabel；和合本：所罗巴伯）大能的行动得了拯救；造物主曾为他们得罪天主与其弟兄所犯的过错，把这奴役判于他们。

§89  The Ten Types of Messiahs
§89  默西亚的十个预表

But it is not only with the advent of Enoch that we find proof of the presence of the Christ among God's children. Abel, who was an example of an appointed minor for the manifestation of His divine justice, also represented a true kind of Messiah. The elected minors since Abel and Enoch are Noah, Melchizedek, Joseph, Moses, David, Solomon, Zerubbabel, and the Messiah. All of these subjects, appointed for the manifestation of divine glory, make up the complete divine spiritual denary number from which all material and spiritual things have arisen. In effect, you will be able to convince yourselves of the truth of what I have said, by the similarity and connection that these minors' works have with Abel's works; this will allow you to recognize clearly that Abel was a true representation of the Christ just as Cain truly represented the operations of the prince of demons.

但我们并非仅从哈诺客的降临，才找到基督临在于天主子女中间的凭证。亚伯尔——那为彰显神性公义而受指派之次者的例证——也代表了一种真正的默西亚。自亚伯尔与哈诺客以来蒙拣选的诸次者有：诺厄、默基瑟德（Melchizedek；和合本：麦基洗德）、若瑟（Joseph；和合本：约瑟）、梅瑟、达味（David；和合本：大卫）、撒罗满（Solomon；和合本：所罗门）、则鲁巴贝耳，以及默西亚。这些为彰显神性荣耀而受指派的对象，共同构成完整的神性属灵十元数，一切物质与属灵之物皆由此而生。事实上，藉这些次者之工与亚伯尔之工的相似与关联，你们便能确信我所说的真实；这将使你们清楚认出：亚伯尔真正是基督的表征，正如加音真正代表了群魔之君的运作。

§90  The Abominations Represented by Cain's Crime
§90  加音之罪所代表的种种可憎

By the murder of his brother Abel, Cain, in effect, represents the rage of the demons who had sworn to dissolve and to destroy any form of creation, and that by making use of men, in whom they insinuate and suggest a multitude of material passions, they set minors against one another and keep them in confusion. We also see that among men of matter, no two thoughts, two actions, or two operations agree. The determination of the demons to sow dissension only leads men to give rise to immodest thoughts of pride and ambition, so that they continually live in divine spiritual discord, which keeps them in ignorance of their corporeal and spiritual origins. Eventually, they lose completely the idea of the devotion that they ought to show towards the Creator.

加音藉谋杀其弟亚伯尔，实际上代表了群魔的暴怒——它们曾发誓要瓦解并毁灭任何形式的创造，并藉利用世人（它们向世人潜移暗示种种物质的情欲）使诸次者彼此相争、陷于混乱。我们也看到：在属物质之人中间，没有两个思想、两个行动、或两个运作是相合的。群魔挑拨纷争的决意，只导致人生出骄傲与野心的种种不端思想，以致他们不住地活在神性属灵的不和之中，这使他们对自己形体与属灵的本源无知。最终，他们完全丧失了本应向造物主表现之虔敬的观念。

§91  The Expulsion of Adam and the Incarnation of the Christ
§91  亚当的被逐与基督的降生成人

Abel was truly his earthly brother, both having emanated from the same man, but there is no comparison between the corporeal formation of either one. The corporeal form of Cain was conceived through excessive voluptuous pleasure, and it traces for us the transgression of the first man. Abel, on the other hand, was conceived without the excesses of the material senses but instead by the purity of the laws of nature. Moreover, his form was more spiritual than material and it is by this spiritual conception that we see Abel's form as a true representation of the Christ's form, issued spiritually from an ordinary woman without the assistance of physical action or the involvement of the material senses. This doubly powerful divine spirit, superior to every emanated being, lived in a pure and glorious circle of divine immensity before he came to operate the divine justice among men. But when sent by the Creator, he left this spiritual abode to enter the womb of a virgin girl.

亚伯尔真是加音属地的弟兄，二人皆从同一人流溢而出；然而二者形体的构成却无可比拟。加音的形体是藉过度的肉欲之乐所孕育的，它为我们勾勒出首位之人的悖逆。亚伯尔则相反，是不藉物质感官之过度、而藉自然律法之洁净所孕育的。此外，他的形体属灵多于属物质；正是藉这属灵的孕育，我们看出亚伯尔之形乃是基督之形的真实表征——基督之形是属灵地从一个普通女子而出，不借助任何形体的行动、也不涉及物质的感官。这双重大能的神性之灵，高于每一受流溢的存有，在祂前来于世人中施行神性公义之前，居于神性无量界一个纯洁而荣耀之圈中。但当祂为造物主所差时，祂离开这属灵的居所，进入一个童贞女子的胎中。

Now, does the relinquishment of his true home, which the minor Christ made, not remind us of the expulsion of the first man from his glorious body? Does not the entering of this spiritual major or word of the Creator into the body of a virgin, remind us clearly of the entering of the first minor into the earth's abyss to clothe itself in a body of matter? The destruction of the Christ as a physical individual, carried out by men in the presence of two women (Mary Magdalene and Mary of Zebedee) was represented by Cain's murder of his brother Abel in the presence of his two sisters. The two women that I have just mentioned followed the Christ in all his divine spiritual work, just as Cain's two sisters followed their brother in his demoniacal work.

那么，次者基督对其真正家园的舍弃，岂不使我们想起首位之人从其荣耀之体的被逐？这属灵高位者、即造物主之言进入童贞女的身体，岂不清楚地使我们想起首位次者进入大地深渊、披上物质之体？基督作为一个有形个体的被毁——由世人在两位妇女（玛利亚玛达肋纳 Mary Magdalene；和合本：抹大拉的马利亚，以及载伯德的玛利亚 Mary of Zebedee）面前所施行——正由加音在其两个姊妹面前谋杀其弟亚伯尔所表征。我方才所提的这两位妇女，在基督一切神性属灵之工中跟随祂，正如加音的两个姊妹在其魔性之工中跟随她们的兄弟一般。

§92  The Blood of Christ Foreshadowed by That of Abel and of Abraham
§92  基督的血由亚伯尔与亚巴郎的血所预示

We cannot ignore that the blood which flowed from the body of the righteous Abel has a certain resemblance to the blood that the Christ had to spill. The blood spilled by Abel on Earth was a genuine example of the action of divine grace which brings peace and mercy on Earth. Was this not repeated clearly by Abraham's circumcision, by which the father of many obtained his perfect reconciliation with the Creator, and was it not by the shedding of his blood that the patriarch realized the covenant that the Eternal made with him? Is it not therefore understandable that the shedding of Christ's blood is a confirmation of all those types who have gone before Him, since this bloodshed, causing an earthquake, made His reconciliation and the covenant that the Creator made with the earth and its inhabitants felt throughout the whole of nature?

我们不能忽视：从义人亚伯尔身体流出的血，与基督必须倾流的血有某种相似。亚伯尔在地上所倾流的血，是那把平安与慈悲带到地上之神性恩宠之行动的真实例证。这岂不由亚巴郎（Abraham；和合本：亚伯拉罕）的割损清楚地重演了一次？「多人之父」藉此获得他与造物主的完全修和；这位圣祖岂不正是藉其血的倾流，成全了永恒主与他所立的盟约？由此岂不可以理解：基督之血的倾流，是对一切在祂之前之预表的确认——因为这血的倾流引发了地震，使祂的修和、并造物主与大地及其居民所立的盟约，遍及整个自然而为人所感？

The Sun Eclipsed by the Christ's Death, as It Will Be at the End of Time
基督之死时日蚀，一如时间终结时之所将有

You may well wonder why the sun darkened at the same time. I will tell you that the eclipse in the celestial region is a real example of the scourge from the demoniacal spirits, whom the Christ, by his operation, reduced more considerably than they were in their privation of power. This eclipse recalls, moreover, the shadows of ignorance into which the Hebrews were plunged when they had eclipsed from their memory the holy divine names which had previously guided them. Lastly, this eclipse was the true example of general matter which will entirely disappear at the end of time and fade from the presence of man like a painting fades from the imagination of a painter. By this comparison you will understand that the principle of matter of the general body is none other than a spiritual picture that the Creator had conceived in his imagination, and in this spiritual picture was included every corporeal being but without material substance.

你大可发问：为何太阳同时变暗？我要告诉你：天界的日蚀是魔性诸灵之灾祸的真实例证——基督藉其运作，把它们削弱得比它们在其权能之匮乏中更为厉害。此外，这日蚀使人想起希伯来人所陷入的无知之暗影——那时他们已把先前引导他们的神性圣名从记忆中蚀去。最后，这日蚀是「总体物质」的真实例证：总体物质将在时间终结时完全消失，从人的面前隐去，正如一幅画从画家的想象中隐去。藉这一比较，你便会明白：总体之体的物质本原，无非是造物主在其想象中所构思的一幅属灵图画；在这属灵图画中，含有每一形体存有，却无物质之实质。

The Veil of the Temple Torn Away: An Example of the Destiny of the Hebrews
被撕裂的圣殿帐幔：希伯来人命运的例证

The tearing of the veil of the Temple is a significant benefit for the spiritual minor. The torn veil is the true example of deliverance of the minor deprived of the Creator's presence. It explains the reintegration of apparent matter, which veils and separates every minor being from perfect knowledge. Moses's example leads us to understand clearly this image, in giving the Hebrews knowledge of the divine law, which he recited to them with his face covered in a red veil. This red veil, which hid Moses's face from the people, represented the perverse spirits who are a scandalous veil to all minors associated with them. The red color of the veil represents the demoniacal intellect's insinuation of the minor's principal senses. It is because of these criminal covenants that the Hebrews were called children of darkness and children of the blood of matter, and replaced by the children of divine grace. But these new children should be careful not to become complacent about the grace that they have at the expense of the Hebrew people, whose reprobation is a striking example of the reality which the whole world will have to face one day.

圣殿帐幔的撕裂，对属灵次者是一项重大的益处。被撕裂的帐幔，是那被剥夺造物主临在之次者得拯救的真实例证。它解释了表面物质的重新融合——表面物质如帐幔般把每一次者存有与完满的知识隔开。梅瑟的例证使我们清楚理解这一形象：他把神性律法的知识授予希伯来人时，是以一块红色帕子蒙着脸向他们诵读的。这红帕遮住梅瑟的脸不让百姓看见，代表那些悖逆诸灵——它们对一切与其相联的次者是一重使人跌倒的帐幔。帕子的红色，代表魔性智识对次者主要感识的潜移暗示。正因这些罪恶的盟约，希伯来人被称为「黑暗之子」与「物质之血之子」，被「神性恩宠之子」所取代。但这些新的子女应当谨慎，不可因自己所得的恩宠——那是以希伯来民族为代价的——而自满；希伯来民族的被弃，乃是全世界有朝一日必须面对之现实的鲜明例证。

§95  Seth, Abel's Heir
§95  舍特，亚伯尔的继承者

I have shown how Adam had been perfectly reconciled through Abel. As the Creator had given Abel all the necessary gifts during his life to bring about the manifestation of divine glory, it was vital that after his death these gifts be passed on to another minor. Adam therefore conceived, with the Creator's sanction, a third descendant, whom he named Seth, which means "admitted to God's posterity." This spiritual minor being inherited all of the powerful gifts that Abel possessed. Moreover, these gifts transmitted to Seth had much more spiritual power and activity than when Abel had them, because Abel was only ever to be a simple type of spiritual reconciliation, whereas Seth had not only this to bear, but also the stability of the laws of nature, the course of various cyclic upheavals, and temporal events which were to take place among his people.

我已显明亚当如何藉亚伯尔得了完全的修和。造物主既在亚伯尔在世时赐了他一切必需的恩赐，以促成神性荣耀的彰显，那么在他死后，这些恩赐便必须传给另一位次者。因此，亚当经造物主的准许，孕育了第三个后裔，给他起名舍特，意为「蒙准进入天主的后裔」。这属灵次者存有承继了亚伯尔所拥有的一切大能恩赐。此外，这些传给舍特的恩赐，比亚伯尔拥有时具有多得多的属灵权能与活力；因为亚伯尔始终只是属灵修和的一个单纯预表，而舍特所要承担的不仅是此，还有自然律法的稳定、种种周期性剧变的进程、以及要在其民中发生的属时事件。

§96  Seth Instructed in Numbers by Hely
§96  舍特由赫利在数字上受教

To this end, the Creator Himself, through His spiritual envoy named Hely, instructed the blessed Seth in the secret spiritual divine forces which contain and direct all of nature. Through the spirit, he received directly from the Creator everything that there was to know about the Eternal's immutable laws, and he learned that every law of temporal creation and every divine action was based upon different numbers. From Hely, he learned that every number was coeternal with the Creator, and that by these various numbers, the Creator formed every image, every convention of creation, and formed His creature. So that you may not doubt this truth, I shall give you the knowledge of these coeternal numbers which are innate in the Creator.

为此，造物主亲自藉其名为赫利（Hely）的属灵使者，在那含容并导引整个自然的秘密属灵神性之力上教导蒙福的舍特。舍特藉那灵，直接从造物主领受了关于永恒主不可更易之律法所当知的一切；他晓得：属时创造的每一律法、每一神性行动，都建基于不同的数字之上。他从赫利学到：每一个数都与造物主共永恒，造物主藉这些不同的数，形成了每一形象、每一创造的成规，并形成了祂的受造物。为使你不致怀疑这一真理，我将把这些内具于造物主之共永恒诸数的知识授予你。

§97  Superiority of the Denary Number, First Divine Power
§97  十元数的优越——第一神性权能

Sages, past and present, have always regarded the denary number as a respectable number in every sense. Knowledge of the famous denary number was perpetuated among this spiritual posterity, a number which contained all kinds of numbers of creation. I shall teach you, therefore, that the denary number contains the four numbers of divine power. I place before you the denary number in four different illustrations: 1, 2, 3, and 4. Add these four figures in the following manner: 1 and 2 make 3, 3 and 3 make 6, and 6 and 4 make 10. You will find your denary number which is the great and divine power in which the other three are contained: 3 and 4 produce the number 7, which is the second power of the Creator; 1 and 2 make 3, and 3 and 3 make 6, which is the third power; finally, add 1 and 3, and you have 4, which is the quaternary number that concludes the four divine powers of the Creator contained in His coeternal denary number.

古今的贤者，一向从各个意义上视十元数为可敬之数。对这著名十元数的知识在这属灵后裔中代代相传；此数含有创造的各样之数。因此我要教导你：十元数含有神性权能的四个数。我把十元数以四种不同的图示置于你面前：1、2、3、4。将这四个数字如下相加：1 加 2 得 3，3 加 3 得 6，6 加 4 得 10。你便得出你的十元数——那伟大而神性的权能，其余三者皆含于其中：3 加 4 生出数字 7，即造物主的第二权能；1 加 2 得 3，3 加 3 得 6，即第三权能；最后，1 加 3，你便得 4，即那结束造物主含于其共永恒十元数中之四神性权能的四元数。

§98  One, Two, Three, Four, and Ten
§98  一、二、三、四与十

The denary number is indivisible, or that which cannot take any division. It is the number which completes, divides, and subdivides every kind of number within the spiritual divine, corporeal and animal, universal, general, and particular temples. This is why this well-known number has always been regarded by the sages as a unique number and representing the quadruple divine essence. This is also why the number can be operated only by the Creator and not by any doubly powerful, simple and minor, spiritual being, and no sage makes use of this number through respect, always leaving this to Divinity. Here then is the use of the denary number, or the first divine power: by this number the divine-thinking imagination conceived divine temporal spiritual creation. Let us pass on to the septenary number.

十元数是不可分的，即那不能承受任何分割之数。它是那在属灵神性的、形体与动物的、普世、总体与个别之诸圣殿中，成全、分割并细分每一种数的数。正因如此，贤者一向视这著名之数为独一之数，代表四重神性本质。也正因如此，此数只能由造物主运作，而不能由任何双重大能的、单纯的与次位的属灵存有运作；没有一位贤者出于敬重而使用此数，总是把它留给神性。这便是十元数、即第一神性权能的用处：藉此数，那神性思想的想象构思了神性的属时属灵创造。让我们转到七元数。

§99  The Septenary Emanates from the Denary, the Second Divine Power
§99  七元数从十元数流溢——第二神性权能

The septenary number, which came from the absolute denary number, is the more-than-perfect number that the Creator used for the emancipation of every spirit outside His divine immensity. The category of septenary spirits was to serve as the first agent and positive cause to contribute in the operation of every kind of movement in forms created in the universal circle. What do we observe in all of these forms? Resonance, movement, action, and reaction. All of these actions and movements of material forms cannot be due to a sole innate principle; this particle of uncreated fire would never produce any effect in corporeal forms if it were not activated by a principal and superior cause. This superior cause is none other than these divine spiritual septenary agents who preside like chiefs over the different actions and movements of all bodies. This then is the virtue of the septenary number, its emanation from the denary number; this number is the second power of Divinity.

七元数出自那绝对的十元数，是造物主用以把每一灵解放独立于其神性无量界之外的「超乎完全之数」。七元诸灵这一等级，本要充作第一施动者与积极之因，参与在普世之圈中所造诸形一切运动的运作。我们在这一切形中观察到什么？共鸣、运动、行动与反作用。物质诸形的这一切行动与运动，不可能仅归于一个内具的本原；这未受造之火的微粒，若不被一个主要而上位的因所激发，便绝不会在形体中产生任何效果。这上位之因，无非就是这些神性属灵的七元施动者，它们如首领般统辖一切物体的种种行动与运动。这便是七元数的德能、并其从十元数的流溢；此数是神性的第二权能。

§100  The Senary Also Emanates from the Denary, Third Divine Power
§100  六元数亦从十元数流溢——第三神性权能

The third divine power, or the senary, is likewise a number emanated from the famous denary number. The number six is not as perfect or as powerful as the septenary, because the senary number can be divided into two equal parts, or two times three, which cannot be done to the number seven without destroying it. The senary number is the one by which the Creator manifested from His thought every image of apparent corporeal forms within the universal circle. Does not Genesis teach that God created everything in six days? This is not to suggest that Genesis wanted to limit the power of Divinity by restricting it in time. The Creator is a pure spirit superior to time, but He could have operated six divine thoughts for universal creation. Moreover, we learn from this that every corporeal being will reintegrate itself into its first principle of emanation by the same number which produced it. Let us move on to the quaternary number.

第三神性权能，即六元数，同样是从那著名十元数流溢而出的数。数字六不如七元数完全、也不如它有力，因为六元数可被分为两个相等的部分，即二乘三，而这对数字七却行不通——那样便会毁灭它。六元数是造物主用以从其思想中彰显普世之圈内一切表面形体之形象的数。《创世纪》岂不教导说，天主在六天内创造了万有吗？这并非暗示《创世纪》有意藉时间的限制来限制神性的权能。造物主是一个高于时间的纯灵，但祂本可为普世创造运作六个神性思想。此外，我们由此学到：每一形体存有都将藉那产生它的同一数，重新融合于其最初的流溢本原。让我们转到四元数。

§101  The Quaternary Completes the Quadruple Divine Essence
§101  四元数成全四重神性本质

The quaternary number, which completes the quadruple divine essence, is infinitely more perfect and significant than the number six, because it is the number which contributes to the perfection of the forms taken from indifferent matter, since it gives movement and action to corporeal form and presides over every created being as the principal number from which all has come. We also call it the number of the powerful word of the Creator. That is why it is in the quaternary number that man must come to know all of the numbers of spiritual powers innate within him, because he had the misfortune of being deprived of this knowledge. The quaternary number is, in effect, the one that the Creator used for the emanation and the emancipation of man, or spiritual minor, which means that the soul is called eternal or impassive life.

四元数成全四重神性本质，比数字六无限地更为完全、更为重大；因为它是那有助于「从无别之物质所取诸形」之完美的数——它赋予形体以运动与行动，并作为万物由之而出的主要之数统辖每一受造存有。我们也称它为「造物主大能之言之数」。正因如此，人必须在四元数之中，认识那内具于他之内的一切属灵权能之数——因为他不幸被剥夺了这一知识。四元数实际上正是造物主用以流溢并解放独立「人（即属灵次者）」所用的数，这意味着灵魂被称为永恒的、或无受苦的生命。

§102  The Ternary, Type of Three Spirituous Essences
§102  三元数，三种精气本质的预表

The triangular figure has always been regarded as very important among the sages of various nations. Adam, Enoch, Moses, Solomon, and the Christ all made great use of this figure. We see that even today this triangle is placed upon our altars and on the frontispiece of buildings. Nor can we believe that the triangle is the figure of the Trinity, even though we give the angles of an equilateral triangle the names of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, because Divinity cannot be represented by any perceptible form to material eyes. This figure represents nothing other than the three spirituous essences which have cooperated in the general terrestrial form. The lower point represents mercury, the southern angle represents sulphur, and the northern one salt. It is only the joining of the quaternary number to these three essences which has given them one single figure. We shall see that every being of creation is subject to and comes from the quadruple divine essence, and that the minor spirit, by its quaternal emanation, truly bears this number of quadruple essence.

三角形这一图形，在各民族的贤者中一向被视为极其重要。亚当、哈诺客、梅瑟、撒罗满与基督，都大量使用了这一图形。我们看到，即便今日，这三角形仍被置于我们的祭坛之上、并置于建筑物的正面。我们也不可相信三角形是「天主圣三」（the Trinity；即圣父、圣子、圣神）的图形，尽管我们把一个等边三角形的三个角命名为圣父、圣子与圣神；因为神性不能以任何为物质之眼所能见的形来表征。这一图形所代表的，无非是那协力构成总体地界之形的三种精气本质。下端之点代表汞，南角代表硫，北角代表盐。惟有四元数与这三种本质的联合，才赋予它们独一的图形。我们将看到：创造的每一存有都受制于、并出于四重神性本质；而次者之灵，藉其四元的流溢，真正承载着这四重本质之数。

§103  Seth, Equal to Primitive Adam
§103  舍特，与原初亚当同等

These are the sublime spiritual instructions that Seth received from the Creator by way of His representative Hely. He acquired complete power and knowledge of divine operations, and he was not, as has been said, instructed in all of the sciences by his father Adam. That was not possible since Adam, by his transgression, was stripped of all spiritual power, and, after his reconciliation, he obtained but one simple minor power. Adam, in his first state of justice, truly received from the divine spirit all of the sciences and spiritual knowledge, but having made criminal use of his powers, the Creator withdrew them immediately and left poor Adam, even after his reconciliation, prone to making mistakes in all of his works, which happens to man every time he operates only by virtue of the three ternary powers which are airy, earthly, and fiery.

这些便是舍特藉造物主的代表赫利从造物主所领受的崇高属灵教诲。他获得了对神性运作完全的权能与知识；而他并非如人所说，是由其父亚当在一切学问上受的教。那是不可能的，因为亚当藉其悖逆已被剥夺了一切属灵权能，在其修和之后，只获得了一种单纯的次位权能。亚当在其最初公义之态中，确曾从神性之灵领受了一切学问与属灵知识；但他既已罪恶地使用了其权能，造物主便立即收回了它们，使可怜的亚当即便在修和之后，仍在其一切工作上易于犯错——每当人仅凭那三种三元权能（即气、地、火之权能）而运作时，这便临到他。

It is very dangerous for the man of desire to use these three powers in any operation without first having obtained from the Creator the quaternary power withdrawn from us by Adam's transgression. The lack of this quaternary power makes us feel that man, since Adam's transgression, is a man of error, and that man is truly in divine spiritual privation. The Creator, who is immutable, expressly said to his reconciled that no knowledge of divine science would be given back to him until after he had earned it through the works prescribed for him for the second time, because since that time, man has been ignorant and narrow-minded, which would not have happened if he had directed his quaternary powers according to the Creator's will.

对渴慕之人而言，若不先从造物主获得那因亚当悖逆而从我们身上收回的四元权能，便在任何运作中使用这三种权能，是极其危险的。这四元权能的缺乏，使我们感到：自亚当悖逆以来，人是一个「谬误之人」，人确乎处于神性属灵的匮乏之中。造物主既不可更易，便明白地对其已修和者说：在他第二次藉那为他所规定之工作赚得之前，神性之学的知识绝不会归还给他；因为自那时起，人便无知而狭隘——倘若他曾按造物主的意志导引其四元权能，这本不会发生。

§104  Seth, Also an Example of Divine Mercy
§104  舍特，也是神性慈悲的例证

Besides the types that Seth represents in spiritual reconciliation and the consolidation of natural laws, he also represents real divine mercy because he replaced Abel, and, because he prayed for the grace of his brother Cain, appears to have gained it, either by the atonement that Cain made for his crime by the kind of death which struck him, or by the penance that Boaz made for the unintentional murder of Cain. You should be in no doubt that the two minors received the Creator's mercy by the virtue and sanctity of the blessed Seth. When you have the good fortune of knowing the nature of Seth's work, or that which wise men, such as Moses or the Christ, carried out after him, you will not ask those kinds of questions any more.

除了舍特在属灵修和与自然律法之巩固上所代表的种种预表之外，他也代表真实的神性慈悲，因为他接替了亚伯尔；又因为他为其兄加音的恩宠祈祷，看来已经求得了——无论是藉加音以那击中他的死亡方式为其罪所作的补赎，还是藉波阿次为其无意中弑杀加音所作的补赎。你当毫不怀疑：这两位次者是藉蒙福的舍特之德能与圣洁而领受了造物主的慈悲。当你有幸得知舍特之工、或诸如梅瑟或基督在他之后所行之工的性质时，你便不会再问那类问题了。

§105  Seth Instructs His Son Enos in Divine Practice
§105  舍特在神性修习上教导其子厄诺士

Honorable Seth, descendant of God by birth, was responsible for instructing his descendants in the divine practice. He transmitted to his son Enos (which means "weak mortal"), every ceremonial of divine, spiritual, earthly, celestial, watery, and fiery operation. He warned him upon the threat of the most terrible suffering to not abuse the Eternal's knowledge that he had confided in him. He forbade, among other things, any liaison with the profane, or the children of men – that is, the concubine girls of Cain's descendants – and that this race could never have union with the children of God who were Seth's posterity.

可敬的舍特，生而为天主的后裔，负责在神性的修习上教导其后裔。他把神性、属灵、属地、属天、属水与属火之运作的一切礼仪，都传给了他的儿子厄诺士（Enos；和合本：以挪士，意为「软弱的必死者」）。他以最可怕之苦楚相威吓，告诫他不可滥用他所托付给他的永恒主的知识。他尤其禁止与凡俗之人、即「人的子女」——也就是加音后裔的妾女——有任何结合，并禁止这一族与作为舍特后裔的「天主之子女」有任何联合。

§106  Seth's and Enos' Posterity Perverted until Enoch
§106  舍特与厄诺士的后裔堕落，直到哈诺客

It was in this posterity that the Creator was to give birth to the minors appointed for the manifestation of His glory. You will see that it did not take long for Seth's posterity, and that of his son Enos, to be corrupted through marriage with Cain's descendants and thus forfeit all divine spiritual knowledge that Seth had taught. Enos' descendants remained in abomination until the seventh generation, from which came the patriarch Enoch, of whom I have already spoken, and with whom I am going to deal extensively now.

造物主本要在这一后裔中，生出那些为彰显祂荣耀而受指派的次者。你将看到：舍特的后裔、并其子厄诺士的后裔，不久便藉与加音后裔通婚而遭败坏，从而丧尽了舍特所教的一切神性属灵知识。厄诺士的后裔一直陷于可憎之中，直到第七代，从中出了圣祖哈诺客——我已论及过他，如今要详加论述。

§107  Enoch, or Dedication, Son of Jared
§107  哈诺客，或「奉献」，耶勒得之子

Enoch was from Seth's descendants and his father was Jared, which means "man illuminated by God." The father gave his son the name Eliacim, which signified resurrection of the Lord in Seth's posterity, and he surnamed Enoch with an E instead of an H. This name means "dedication." Jared was a righteous man before the Creator, and more powerful in divine virtue than all other patriarchs, because of the force of divine practice that he exercised for the atonement of the crimes of Enos' descendants. The light that he received daily from the divine spirit prepared him to be the forerunner of a righteous being that he knew was to emanate from him. Jared learned through the spirit that his son Enoch was to operate among Cain's and Seth's descendants and the female descendants of Adam, which together form the three nations inhabiting the earth.

哈诺客出自舍特的后裔，他的父亲是耶勒得（Jared；和合本：雅列），意为「为天主所光照之人」。这位父亲给他儿子起名「厄里雅金」（Eliacim），意指「上主在舍特后裔中的复活」；他又给「哈诺客」（Enoch）之名加上以 E 起首（而非 H）——此名意为「奉献」。耶勒得在造物主面前是义人，其神性德能之大胜过其余一切圣祖，因为他为厄诺士后裔之罪的补赎而施行了大有力量的神性修习。他每日从神性之灵所领受的光，预备他作那位他所知将从他流溢而出之义者的先驱。耶勒得藉那灵得知：他的儿子哈诺客将在加音与舍特的后裔、以及亚当的女性后裔中运作——这三者共同构成居于地上的三个民族。

§108  Tripartition of Universal Creation and Tripartition of the World
§108  普世创造的三重区分与世界的三重区分

The general convention is to distinguish four nations on Earth – namely, Ishmael, Israel, the Christians, and the idolatrous or unbelievers. But as soon as this division into four parts is made by men without the divine aspect, it can only be false. Now, only three people remained: Adam, Cain, and Seth. Adam divided the earth into three parts and not four. Even if Adam had had 100 children, it would not have been possible to divide the earth into more than three parts; the earth has no more than three parts and its shape is perfectly triangular. Thus, Adam divided it thus: the West for Adam, the South for Cain, and the North for Seth. Just as there are only three spherical circles – the sentient, the visual, and the rational – and only three terrestrial angles, so universal creation is also divided into three parts, and the proof is the fact that one cannot find what is called the squaring of the circle.

一般的成规是把地上分为四个民族——即依市玛耳（Ishmael；和合本：以实玛利）、以色列、基督徒，以及拜偶像者或不信者。但这四分之法一旦由人不带神性眼光而作，便只能是虚假的。如今只剩下三个人：亚当、加音与舍特。亚当把大地分为三部分，而非四部分。纵然亚当有一百个孩子，也不可能把大地分为多于三部分；大地至多只有三部分，其形状是完美的三角形。于是，亚当如此划分：西方归亚当，南方归加音，北方归舍特。正如球形之圈只有三个——感识、视识与理性——地界之角也只有三个，普世创造同样分为三部分；其证据便是：人无法找到所谓「化圆为方」（化圆为方形、即把圆分为四部分）之法。

These three nations were again represented by Noah's three children, for whom this earth had been divided into three equal parts – namely, Ham in the South, Shem in the West, and Japheth in the North. The command that Adam received from the Creator to divide the earth in this manner was very painful for him, as it reminded him of the difference between his former state of glory and his subsequent state of reprobation. This division of the Earth heralded the strife which has since reigned and will do so until the end of time, and the state of war and dissension into which Adam plunged his entire posterity.

这三个民族又由诺厄的三个孩子所代表；为他们，这地曾被分为三个相等的部分——即含（Ham）在南方、闪（Shem）在西方、耶斐特（Japheth；和合本：雅弗）在北方。亚当从造物主所领受的这一如此划分大地的命令，对他极为痛苦，因为它使他想起他昔日荣耀之态与其后被弃之态之间的差别。大地的这一划分，预告了自那以来一直主导、并将持续到时间终结的纷争，以及亚当把其全部后裔投入其中的战乱与不和之境。

From the Planetary Sign to the Birth of Enoch
从行星之兆到哈诺客的诞生

The birth of Enoch brought great spiritual satisfaction among Seth's descendants. His advent in the world was indicated in the heavens by a planetary sign which greatly surprised Seth's descendants and those of Cain even more so. The sign was a star which left its planetary circle; it had come closer to Earth than usual, so that it appeared as quite a contrast to the other stars. This caused men to name it Lathau, which means sign of confusion and earthly trouble, and is what is commonly called a comet. Every celestial body is susceptible to six divisions. A planetary circle is composed of six principal stars which receive their order from a superior star at the center. These signs are arranged in sevens; each has seven virtues, multiplied by their own number – that is, 7 times 7, which makes 49, which equals 13, which equals 4.

哈诺客的诞生，在舍特后裔中带来极大的属灵满足。他降临于世，藉一个行星之兆在诸天中得以指明，这大大惊动了舍特的后裔，更惊动了加音的后裔。这兆是一颗离开其行星之圈的星；它比往常更靠近大地，以致它显得与其他星辰截然不同。这使人给它起名「拉桃」（Lathau，意为「混乱与地上祸患之兆」），也就是通常所称的彗星。每一天体都可作六重划分。一个行星之圈由六颗主要之星构成，它们从居于中心的一颗上位之星领受其秩序。这些征象以七为一组排列；每一个都有七种德能，再乘以它们自身之数——即七乘七，得四十九，其数字和为十三，再化为四。

§110  Planets and Spirits
§110  行星与诸灵

You know that every being of corporeal form is born from the three spirituous essences of mercury, sulphur, and salt, and that the axial spirits have been put into action to cooperate in the formation of all bodies. This is what we call passive life to which every form is subject, whether celestial or terrestrial. The star in the center is the superior planetary being. It governs the major and inferior planetary bodies and is called superior because the solar influence acts immediately on it. The superior star communicates what it has received to the major planetary stars, which in turn communicate with an infinite number of little stars we call inferior planetary bodies, and these spread the effect with precision upon the gross earthly bodies. The planetary circles can also be inhabited by bad spirits who oppose the good influential actions of the good spiritual planetary beings. From this has come a proverb among men that there are bad planetary influences, and this is very true.

你知道，每一形体存有都由汞、硫、盐三种精气本质而生，而「轴之诸灵」已被发动，以协力构成一切物体。这便是我们所称的、每一形（无论属天还是属地）所受制的被动生命。居于中心的星是上位的行星存有。它统辖高位与下位的行星之体，之所以称为上位，是因为太阳的影响直接作用于它。上位之星把它所领受的传通给高位的行星诸星，后者又与无数我们称为下位行星之体的小星传通，这些下位者又精确地把其效果散布于粗重的地界之体上。行星诸圈中也可住有恶灵，它们抵挡善的属灵行星存有那善之影响行动。由此，世人中间有一句谚语说「有恶的行星影响」，这话极为真实。

§111  Demons Fight the Corporeal Forms of Men and Spirits
§111  群魔与人及诸灵的形体争战

What you do not know is whether the demon was in a corporeal form when he tempted the first man. I will tell you that the demon was from the outset in a glorious body, because it would have been impossible for pure and simple spirits to have tempted or entrapped each other had they been clothed in a corporeal form. Each man is free to communicate with or to hide his thoughts from others; a spiritual being, however, cannot conceive a thought without other spirits noticing it immediately. Everything is in the open among spirits free of matter, while the contrary is the case among minors enveloped in a form of apparent matter.

你所不知道的是：魔鬼试探首位之人时，是否处于形体之中。我要告诉你：魔鬼从一开始便处于一个荣耀之体中；因为纯粹单一的诸灵若披着形体，便根本不可能彼此试探或诱陷。每个人都可自由地向他人传达、或向他人隐藏自己的思想；然而一个属灵存有，一构想出一个思想，便立即为其他灵所察觉。在脱离物质的诸灵中间，一切都是敞开的；而在被裹于表面物质之形中的次者中间，情形却恰恰相反。

Every superior, major, and inferior planetary spirit, confined in a corporeal form, is subject like humans to attacks and struggles in its daily operations. But the difference is that these spirits do not succumb to the attacks of demons, because they are not susceptible to corruption or seduction, and the forms that they inhabit are not susceptible to putrefaction. Man, on the other hand, strays daily from the spiritual laws within him, and he cannot hope for his reconciliation until after a long and difficult effort, and the reintegration of his corporeal form can take place only by a putrefaction that is inconceivable to mortals.

每一上位、高位与下位的行星之灵，既被拘于形体之中，便像人一样在其日常运作中受制于攻击与挣扎。但区别在于：这些灵不屈服于群魔的攻击，因为它们不易受败坏或诱惑，它们所居之形也不易腐化。人则相反，每日偏离其里面的属灵律法，非经长久而艰难的努力便不能指望其修和，其形体的重新融合也惟有藉一种为必死者所不能想象的腐化才能发生。

The Advent of Enoch Predicts the Universal Reconciliation in Three Operations
哈诺客的降临预示三重运作中的普世修和

It was not the same for the Christ, Abel, Hely, or Enoch. In regard to Enoch, his advent into the world predicted the coming of the universal reconciler, and the sign which appeared at his birth predicted the one which appeared at the birth of the Christ. His example corresponds to the three separate operations that the Christ had to perform among men. The first operation was for the reconciliation of Adam, the second operation was for the reconciliation of human kind in the year 4000, and the third operation – which must occur at the end of time – will repeat the first reconciliation of Adam by reconciling all of his descendants with the Creator. That will be the time that the perverse spirits acknowledge their errors and abominations while remaining for an immemorial time in the shadow of death. I must speak to you again elsewhere about Enoch when I enter into an account of epochs, and so I shall pass on to an explanation of Noah.

对基督、亚伯尔、赫利或哈诺客而言，情形却不同。论到哈诺客，他降临于世，预示了那普世和解者的来临；他诞生时所现之兆，预示了基督诞生时所现之兆。他的例证对应基督必须在世人中施行的三桩分立的运作。第一桩运作是为亚当的修和，第二桩运作是为人类在第四千年的修和，第三桩运作——必在时间终结时发生——将重演对亚当的第一次修和，使他一切的后裔与造物主修和。那将是悖逆诸灵承认其谬误与可憎、同时又在死亡的阴影中滞留亘古之时。当我进入对「诸纪元」的叙述时，我必在别处再向你论及哈诺客；因此现在我要转到对诺厄的解释。

Part V — NOAH
第五部 — 诺厄（Noah；和合本：挪亚）
§113  Noah, as a Denary Number
§113  诺厄，作为十元数

Of all the apparent corporeal forms, Noah is a striking and significant example of universal, general, and particular creation. By his denary number, he is an example of the Creator, born as the tenth of the patriarchs. He was also the last head of the families of Adam's descendants before the flood, and, through his descendants, he perpetuated Adam's descendants, which the flood had otherwise wiped off the face of the earth.

在一切表面形体之中，诺厄是普世、总体与个别创造的一个鲜明而重要的例证。藉其十元数，他是造物主的例证，生为众圣祖中第十位。他也是大洪水之前亚当后裔各家族的末位家长；藉其后裔，他延续了亚当的后裔——若非如此，洪水本会把亚当的后裔从地面上抹去。

§114  Reasons for the Flood
§114  洪水的缘由

This decree was given only for the manifestation of divine justice against the demoniacal leaders, who had completely outraged the Creator by their ceaseless persecutions of minors. The enormous conquests that they had made over these unfortunates had made the demoniacal leaders so proud that they believed themselves invincible and even more powerful than the Creator. All of their conquests effectively showed their weaknesses rather than their strengths. You must not think that the earth was heavily populated at that time. They were a mere handful; but, nevertheless, to subjugate this small number of minors, the chief of the demons employed all of their powers. If the minors had made good use of their freedom, all of the insinuations of the demons would have been unable to prevail against them.

这一判令颁下，惟为向众魔性首领彰显神性的公义；它们藉其对诸次者无休止的逼迫，彻底地触怒了造物主。它们对这些不幸者所作的巨大征服，使这些魔性首领骄傲至极，以致自以为不可战胜、甚至比造物主更有权能。它们的一切征服，其实显出的是它们的软弱，而非它们的力量。你切不可以为那时地上人口稠密。他们不过是寥寥可数的一小群；然而，为制伏这一小群次者，群魔之首竟动用了它们的一切权能。倘若这些次者善用了他们的自由，群魔的一切潜移暗示便都不能胜过他们。

The more the princes of demons use their powers against the Creator, the more humiliated and punished they are; the more they achieve victories over spiritual minors, the more they are tormented, because the Creator, to their humiliation, snatches away their prey by bringing back the minors to His divine law. And what goal did the demons intend to achieve? To go beyond the limits placed upon them, by relentlessly seducing not only the earth's inhabitants, but also those of various celestial bodies. They mesmerized the understanding of the minors so as to make them believe themselves to be the only true gods, promising to obtain for them the same powers as those possessed by Divinity. Furthermore, these perverse spirits went so far as to persuade the minors that universal creation was falsely attributed to Divinity, and that the God of which they had heard was only one of them directing all of creation.

群魔之君越是动用其权能与造物主为敌，便越发蒙羞受罚；它们越是战胜属灵的次者，便越受折磨；因为造物主使它们蒙羞，把它们的猎物夺走，使诸次者归回祂的神性律法。群魔究竟想达到什么目的呢？就是越出加于它们的界限，不住地诱惑——不仅诱惑地上的居民，也诱惑各天体的居民。它们迷惑次者的悟性，使他们自以为是唯一真正的神，应许要为他们求得与神性所具同样的权能。此外，这些悖逆之灵甚至竟劝服次者说：普世创造被错误地归于神性，他们曾听说的那位「神」，不过是它们中的一个、主宰着一切创造罢了。

A regional prince of the Western part said to these minors, showing them the sun, "Look at the eye of this grand universal prince. It is the house of the one who directs everything that your sight can perceive." The prince of the Northern region said in turn, showing them the lunar body, "Turn yourselves towards this principal house, where every major spirit like me dwells. The glory of our great prince manifests itself there." These perverse leaders taught the poor minors to communicate with the inhabitants of the two houses – the moon, the greatest house of the earthly circle, and the sun, the greatest house of the heavens – recommending them to carry out operations only when the two were in conjunction and opposition, forming the solar and lunar eclipses. So the unfortunate minors, seduced by all of these promises, made great progress in perversion and also corrupted Cain's descendants and a large part of Seth's descendants in a short time.

西方之部分的一位地区之君指着太阳对这些次者说：“看这位普世大君之眼罢。它是那位主宰你视觉所能察知之一切者的居所。”北方之地的君王又指着月体说：“你们要转向这主要的居所，凡像我这样的高位之灵都住在那里。我们大君的荣耀在那里彰显。”这些悖逆的首领教这些可怜的次者与两处居所的居民——月亮（地界之圈最大的居所）与太阳（诸天最大的居所）——沟通，嘱咐他们惟当二者相合与相冲、形成日蚀与月蚀之时，才施行运作。于是，这些被种种应许所诱惑的不幸次者，在悖逆之事上大有长进，并在短期内把加音的后裔、以及舍特后裔的一大部分也一同带坏了。

§115  Lessons to Draw from the Role of the Demons during the Flood
§115  从洪水中群魔之角色所当汲取的教训

The words of the diabolical leaders must have been very alluring to have perverted nearly all of the inhabitants of the earth in such a short period of time, which should warn us to always keep ourselves on guard, because there is nothing that these perverse spirits cannot invent to corrupt the minor and join him with them. They persecute minors as soon as they start to come into this lower world even though they cannot yet use their physical senses. This can be plainly seen through the various movements, cries, and agitation of newborn children. From this stems the practice that the patriarchs had of exorcising their descendants by blessing them, so as to ward off the perverse spirits surrounding the corporeal form. From this too the practice of circumcision arose, or the baptism of blood, by which the covenant was revealed to Abraham. Lastly, the baptism of grace to the newly converted to the Christ came from this.

这些魔性首领的话必定极其诱人，才能在如此短的时间内把地上几乎所有居民都带坏；这应当警醒我们时时保持戒备，因为这些悖逆之灵没有什么诡计不能编造出来，以败坏次者、使他与它们联合。次者一开始进入这下界，即便尚不能使用其形体的感官，它们便已逼迫他。这可从新生婴孩种种的动作、啼哭与躁动清楚看出。由此，便有了众圣祖藉祝福为其后裔驱邪的做法，以驱赶环绕形体的悖逆之灵。由此也兴起了「割损」（circumcision；和合本：割礼），或曰「血的圣洗」，永恒主藉此向亚巴郎启示了盟约。最后，赐给新归化基督者的「恩宠之圣洗」（baptism；和合本：洗礼）也由此而来。

§116  The Creator Protects Fallen Man, and His Creation Is Only an Image
§116  造物主护佑堕落之人，其创造不过是一幅图像

The Eternal prevented the second operation of these bad demons, who wanted to merge the minor completely with them. The Creator therefore took the minor under his wing, and in this way all of the demons' attempts against His immutable laws became useless. They were held in a yet greater privation, and they had only one simple spiritual power left, which allowed them to act superficially in the universe. One should not look upon the corporeal form as a real existing material body; it comes from the primary spirituous essences destined by the Word of Creation to retain the various impressions as forms. He is called Creator because He created all from nothing, and because all of His creation came from His imagination; and since it derives from His divine thinking imagination, it is called image. The same divine faculty that brought forth everything will bring back everything to His essence.

永恒主阻止了这些恶魔的第二桩运作——它们本想把次者完全并入自己。造物主于是把次者护于其羽翼之下；如此，群魔一切违抗祂不可更易之律法的图谋都归于徒然。它们被拘于一种更深的匮乏之中，只余下一种单纯的属灵权能，使它们能在宇宙中表面地行动。人不应把形体看作一个真实存在的物质身体；它出自那些「原初精气本质」，这些本质由「创造之言」注定要保留种种印象，作为诸形之用。祂被称为造物主，因为祂从无中创造了万有，又因为祂一切的创造都出自祂的想象；既出自祂神性思想的想象，便称为「图像」（image）。那使万物产生的同一神性官能，也将使万物归回祂的本质。

§117  Privation and Blindness of People of the Present Age
§117  当今世代之人的匮乏与盲目

In spite of the force of the demoniacal persecutions against the first people, were there not some righteous men who defended themselves against the insinuations of bad intellect? You cannot deny that there were some, even if there were only nine patriarchs who followed Noah, thus completing the denary number. But if I were to ask you to find a righteous one in this age, you would not find any, because people, since the era of the Christ, have lost sight of the knowledge of divine worship, as they no longer see the divine manifestations of the Creator's law which occurred daily among the first peoples and among the people of Israel. It is therefore not surprising that the demoniacal intellects today are making even greater progress among minors than in the past. They have abandoned the spiritual sciences in favor of the negotiations and cupidity of worldly goods, which place such a heavy veil over their eyes as to make them nearly as blind as were Cain's and most of Seth's generation.

尽管魔性对最初之人的逼迫何等猛烈，难道就没有一些义人抵御了恶智识的潜移暗示吗？你不能否认确有一些，纵然只有跟随诺厄的九位圣祖、从而成全了十元数。但我若叫你在这世代中找出一个义人，你却找不到；因为自基督的时代以来，众人已丧失了对神性敬拜的知识，正如他们不再看见那曾在最初诸民、并在以色列民中每日发生的、造物主律法的神性彰显。因此，今日魔性的智识在次者中比往昔取得更大进展，也就不足为奇了。他们舍弃了属灵的学问，转而追逐世俗财货的营谋与贪婪，这在他们眼上蒙了一重如此沉重的帕子，几乎使他们像加音、并舍特世代的大多数一样盲目。

§118  Type, Symbol, Prophecy
§118  预表、象征、预言

A type is a real figure from a past event, as well as an event that must occur in the future. The symbol only provides information about the kind of event to come. A type is, at any rate, superior to prophecy in that prophecies are only warnings for the future, and which can be nullified by the Creator's mercy or by a change of conduct of the people on whom the prophecy is to fall, whereas a type heralds an inevitable event under the immutable decree of the Creator. We should know, therefore, that this first generation, as well as the people of Israel, were struck and horribly punished for abandoning the Creator and the devotion for which they had been formed.

「预表」（type）是一桩过去事件的真实表象，同时也是一桩必在将来发生之事的表象。「象征」（symbol）则仅提供关于将来事件之种类的信息。无论如何，预表高于「预言」（prophecy），因为预言仅是对将来的警示，可因造物主的慈悲、或因预言所要临到之民改变行为而被废除；而预表则在造物主不可更易的判令之下，预告一桩不可避免的事件。因此我们当知道：这最初的世代，连同以色列民，都因离弃造物主、并离弃他们受造所为之的虔敬，而遭击打、受可怕的惩罚。

§120  Israel, in Particular, Has Transgressed
§120  以色列尤其悖逆了

If we consider Israel, would we not see the same errors and crimes as amongst the first generations? It is these people, however, who were witnesses to the manifestation of complete justice and divine power. The Creator deployed all of His wonders for them, and in spite of that, they fell under the bad demons' sway and had the effrontery to renounce the Eternal in preference to false gods. The worship that they practiced demonstrated that they were led only by false principles and by the prince of darkness. They were slaves to the image of ceremonial law, but not to the truth of their soul and the laws of the Creator. They were subject only to the cupidity of material goods.

我们若考察以色列，岂不会看见与最初诸世代相同的谬误与罪行吗？然而正是这民，曾亲眼见证完全的公义与神性权能的彰显。造物主为他们施展了祂一切的奇事，尽管如此，他们仍落于恶魔的辖制之下，竟厚颜弃绝永恒主而偏向假神。他们所行的敬拜表明：他们只受虚假的本原、并受黑暗之君所引领。他们是礼仪律法之表象的奴隶，却非其灵魂之真理与造物主律法的奴隶。他们只服从对物质财货的贪婪。

§121  Ishmael Proves Divine Mercy
§121  依市玛耳证明神性的慈悲

The example of Ishmael is one which gives us the most striking proofs of divine mercy. As the first natural descendant of Abraham, he was the symbol of the type of Israel's election. His leaving his father's house represents Israel's shameful exit from the Temple into divine privation. According to the Scriptures, his mother Hagar had brought only bread for food and a jug of water, and, having consumed it all in one day, she was stricken with despair to see her son Ishmael near death from hunger and thirst. But, in her suffering, she did not forget the Creator; nor did the Creator abandon her, for the angel of the Lord appeared to her and said, "Woman! The plea that you have made to the Eternal to expiate your faults has been granted. Arise, take your child and follow me."

依市玛耳（Ishmael；和合本：以实玛利）的例证，是给我们神性慈悲最鲜明凭证的例证之一。作为亚巴郎第一个自然的后裔，他是以色列蒙拣选之预表的象征。他离开父家，代表以色列蒙羞地从圣殿走入神性的匮乏。按圣经所载，他的母亲哈加尔（Hagar；和合本：夏甲）只带了饼作食物、并一皮囊水；一日之内用尽之后，她眼见儿子依市玛耳因饥渴濒死，陷入绝望。但在其苦难中，她没有忘记造物主；造物主也没有撇弃她，因为上主的天使向她显现，说：“妇人哪！你向永恒主为赎你过错所作的祈求，已蒙应允。起来，带着你的孩子，跟我来。”

After the angel had satisfied Ishmael's hunger and thirst, he blessed them both in the name of the Eternal and instructed them about the road that they should take, saying to Hagar, "The Eternal shall take care of your son; he will prosper on Earth in your presence and he shall bring forth twelve princes on Earth, or twelve leaders of tribes." Should not this example show us to put all our faith in the Eternal, certain that He can never fail to make us happy?

天使满足了依市玛耳的饥渴之后，奉永恒主的名祝福了他们二人，并指教他们当走的路，对哈加尔说：“永恒主必看顾你的儿子；他必在你面前于地上兴盛，并要在地上生出十二位族长，即十二个支派的首领。”这例证岂不该向我们表明：当把我们全部的信心放在永恒主身上，确信祂绝不会不使我们得福吗？

§122  Noah's Type Floating on the Waters, Like the Creator
§122  诺厄漂于水面的预表，一如造物主

The Creator instructed Noah, his elected faithful, to ready himself to construct an ark of cedar wood. This construction was called an ark because it floated on water, and its bottom had the shape of a duck's underbelly. It had no masts, sails, or oars; thanks to the Creator, it was navigated by the force of water. When the time came, He sent to Noah an angel who told him in what manner he should enter the ark with his wife, children, and his children's wives, and about the provisions that he should make for the rational and the non-rational animals. More than two-thirds of the provisions remained in the ark when everyone had left it, for Noah and his entire family had been so frightened by the terrible punishment that they had witnessed that they could hardly think about their earthly existence.

造物主指示祂所拣选的信徒诺厄，预备自己建造一只香柏木的方舟。这建造之物被称为「方舟」，因为它漂于水上，其底部形如鸭腹。它没有桅、帆或桨；蒙造物主之恩，它是靠水力航行的。时候到了，祂差给诺厄一位天使，告诉他当以何种方式带着妻子、儿女与儿媳进入方舟，并当为有理性与无理性的动物预备何等口粮。众人离开方舟时，超过三分之二的口粮仍留在舟中；因为诺厄与他全家被他们所目睹的可怕惩罚吓得魂不附体，几乎无心顾及自己在地上的生存。

During the flood, Noah was being a true example of the Creator; he floated on the waters like the Creator had done before the chaos was ordered, according to the words of Genesis. The heavy aquatic veil that covered the earth and hid it from the face of the Creator alludes to the supra-heavens that most philosophers call the crystalline heaven, and which separates the Creator and His divine domain from His temporal universal creation. The obscuring of the luminous bodies during this event alludes to the privation of the divine spiritual light of chaotic bodies, which had not yet received the laws to act as the Creator had wished in order to serve as adornments and agents in the universal circle of His creation.

在洪水期间，诺厄正是造物主的真实例证；他漂于水面，正如按《创世纪》之言，造物主在混沌被整理之前所行的一般。那覆盖大地、把大地从造物主面前遮藏的沉重水之帷幕，影射那诸天之上、大多数哲人称为「水晶天」（crystalline heaven）之物；它把造物主及其神性领域，与祂属时的普世创造隔开。此事期间发光之体的隐晦，影射「混沌诸体」神性属灵之光的匮乏——这些混沌之体尚未领受律法，去按造物主所愿而行动，以在其创造的普世之圈中充作装饰与施动者。

§123  Three Parts of the Body and Three Operations of Universal Creation
§123  身体的三部分与普世创造的三桩运作

We divide the human body's frame into three parts: namely, 1, the head; 2, the trunk; and 3, the bones of the flanks. Each has its different properties, and the different faculties allude perfectly to the three kingdoms of nature that we recognize as the animal, vegetable, and mineral. The three principal parts of the human body afford me the opportunity to explain the three principal actions that caused the explosion of all chaotic bodies. The first is the descent of the minor into the general earthly corporeal form, the second is the joining of the divine major spirit with the minor or general soul, and the third is the limit and the extent to which the major spirit attached itself to the celestial and terrestrial bodies. By these three operations, universal creation received laws, precepts, and commandments, and when all things had received them, the explosion of chaos took place.

我们把人体的骨架分为三部分：即一、头；二、躯干；三、两胁之骨。各有其不同的属性，其不同的官能完满地影射我们所认知的自然三界，即动物界、植物界与矿物界。人体这三个主要部分，给了我机会解释那导致一切「混沌诸体」迸发的三桩主要行动。第一是次者降入总体地界之形体，第二是神性高位之灵与次者（即总体之灵魂）的联合，第三是高位之灵依附于天界与地界之体的界限与广度。藉这三桩运作，普世创造领受了律法、诫命与命令；当万物都领受了这些之后，混沌的迸发便发生了。

You must not think that the explosion of chaos was caused by the descent of the minor spirit, nor by its joining with the major spirit, but only by the retreat made by the major, or doubly strong spirit, from the chaotic envelope to reunite with its Father; and it was only at that moment that each thing appeared in passive and in active nature to the eyes of the Creator, according to the image in which He had conceived them.

你切不可以为混沌的迸发是由次者之灵的降入、或由它与高位之灵的联合所致，而只是由那高位者、即双重强力之灵从混沌外壳中退出、以与其圣父重新联合所致；正是在那一刻，每一样事物才按造物主所构思它们的形象，在被动与主动的自然之中显现于造物主眼前。

§124  Light and Darkness
§124  光与暗

This should make you think about the meaning of the scriptural words, "The light was in the darkness, and the darkness comprehended it not." Every corporeal form is always a chaos to the spiritual divine soul, because form of matter cannot receive communication from the divine spiritual intellect. The minor, on the other hand, by his emanation, is likely to receive this communication continuously. Does he not spend half of his temporal life in a feeble light which is only the reflection of the divine spiritual light, and the other half in terrible darkness? But when the minor is separated from his chaotic form, there is no more question for him of elementary temporal darkness. He rejoices fully in the impassive and unchanging light that is innate within him. The darkness to which the Scriptures threaten the condemned does not mean a privation of brightness, but only a privation of divine spiritual action within the immense divine circumference.

这应当使你思想圣经之言的含义：“光在黑暗中照耀，黑暗决不能胜过祂。”每一形体对属灵神性的灵魂而言，始终是一片混沌，因为物质之形不能领受神性属灵智识的传通。次者则相反，藉其流溢，能够不住地领受这传通。他岂不是把他属时生命的一半，耗于一种微弱之光——那不过是神性属灵之光的反照——而把另一半耗于可怕的黑暗之中吗？但当次者与其混沌之形分离时，对他便再无「元素性的属时黑暗」这回事了。他完全欢喜于那内具于他之内、无受苦而不变的光。圣经用以恐吓受罚者的黑暗，并不意味着光明的匮乏，而仅意味着在那广袤的神性周界之内、神性属灵行动的匮乏。

§125  The Angel at the Exit of the Ark
§125  方舟出口处的天使

If you doubt that the explosion of the chaos took place as I have described to you, then you need only to look carefully at the angel who opened the door of the ark to let out all of the animals upon the summit to witness the manifestation of divine justice, and you will clearly see the true figure of the major spirit of the chaotic envelope who made known to the Creator every being of temporal creation.

你若怀疑混沌的迸发是否如我所述般发生，那么你只须仔细看那位打开方舟之门、让一切动物出到山顶以见证神性公义之彰显的天使，你便会清楚看见那「混沌外壳之高位之灵」的真实形象——正是他把属时创造的每一存有向造物主显明。

§126  The Mysterious Ark
§126  奥秘的方舟

This mysterious ark housing all of the different types of animals really explains the chaotic envelope that contains every principle of creation of corporeal forms. The forty days that the animals were deprived of the elementary light clearly demonstrates the physical activity that men were forced to experience as part of their physical reproduction. The descent and joining of the rarefied waters with the heavy waters reminds us of the descent of the first minor into an earthly material body, and the forty days that the rarefied waters took to descend represent the forty years of pain and punishment that Adam felt in his soul and spirit after his transgression.

这奥秘的方舟容纳了各类不同的动物，实在解释了那含有形体一切创造本原的「混沌外壳」。动物被剥夺元素之光的四十天，清楚地表明了人在其形体繁衍中被迫经历的形体活动。稀薄之水与沉重之水的下降与相合，使我们想起首位次者降入属地物质之体；而稀薄之水下降所用的四十天，代表亚当在其悖逆之后于灵魂与心灵中所感受的四十年痛苦与惩罚。

§127  Punishment of Adam, Noah, as well as the Animals
§127  亚当、诺厄以及动物所受的惩罚

It is impossible to imagine the pain that Adam felt when, after having been entirely free and boundless through his nature as a pure thinking spiritual being, he found himself in a prison of matter and subject to time. He used forty years to bemoan his crime, and gained the Creator's mercy at the end of forty years of suffering necessary for his atonement, because it was at the end of this time that from him and Eve was to be born the spiritual sacrifice that would efface the horror of his crime. The troubles that Adam endured are shown by the forty days that Noah spent on Mount Ararat, called Mount of Armenia, even though Noah spent all of his time giving thanks to the Creator for the scourge that he and the animals had been spared.

亚当所感受的痛苦实非笔墨所能想象：他本以纯粹思想属灵存有的本性完全自由、无有边界，如今却发觉自己身陷物质的牢狱、受制于时间。他用了四十年为其罪哀恸，并在为其补赎所必需的四十年苦楚之末，得了造物主的慈悲；因为正是在这时限之末，那要抹去其罪之可怖的属灵祭献，将从他与厄娃而生。亚当所忍受的烦扰，由诺厄在阿辣辣特山（Mount Ararat；和合本：亚拉腊山，又称亚美尼亚之山）上所度的四十天所显明——尽管诺厄把全部时间都用来为他与动物得以幸免的那场灾祸而感谢造物主。

You may ask why non-rational animals were given the same punishment as rational ones. I answer that not only had men of that era renounced the Creator, but they had pushed their abominations to the point of enjoying animals as women are to be enjoyed, and also enjoyed passions between themselves, contrary to nature. These crimes have since been related to us by Sodom and Gomorrah, whose names are synonymous with these terrible transgressions. When He had stricken the earth with the flood, the Creator did the same thing in punishing the two cities; moreover, the fire that the Eternal caused to fall upon the two cities forecasted how universal creation will end.

你或许会问：无理性的动物为何受了与有理性者相同的惩罚？我回答：那时代的人不仅弃绝了造物主，更把其可憎推至这般地步——竟如享用女人一般享用动物，又彼此纵情、违逆本性。这些罪行，后来藉索多玛（Sodom；和合本：所多玛）与哈摩辣（Gomorrah；和合本：蛾摩拉）向我们道出，这两个名字已成为这些可怕悖逆的同义词。造物主既以洪水击打了大地，也照样惩罚了这两座城；此外，永恒主使降于这两城之火，预示了普世创造将如何终结。

§128  Forty Years, Forty Days
§128  四十年，四十日

To better convey that Adam's reconciliation was achieved only after forty years, you only need to consider the state of sterility into which the earth was plunged for forty years after the waters covered the earth's surface, making it like a corpse deprived of most of its vegetation. The earth restarted vigorous growth only after it was blessed again by the Creator. Similarly, only after forty years of suffering were Adam and Eve once again returned to a state of temporal divine spiritual power. The scourge of the waters lasted for such a long time so that it would be an immemorial example to the remaining mortals, and so that they could pass on to their descendants the memory of the first man's crime and his three generations under Cain, Seth, and finally Noah, whom the Creator found to be righteous.

为更好地使你明白亚当的修和惟在四十年之后才达致，你只须思量：大水淹没地面之后，大地陷入四十年的荒芜之态，如同一具被剥去大部分植被的尸体。大地惟有再次蒙造物主祝福之后，才重新恢复茂盛的生长。同样，惟有在四十年的苦楚之后，亚当与厄娃才重又被带回一种属时的神性属灵权能之态。水的灾祸持续如此之久，是要作那余剩之必死者亘古的例证，使他们能把首位之人之罪、及其在加音、舍特、并末了在诺厄（造物主见他为义人）之下的三个世代的记忆，传给他们的后裔。

§129  Noah's Speech: The Meaning of the Flood
§129  诺厄的讲辞：洪水的意义

When Noah came out of the ark, he spoke to the creatures who were with him: "Listen, Earth and mankind; hear me with your spiritual being, not your material one. The Creator is the eternal sovereign master of all that exists within the universal circle, and all has come from Him. May the ruins of this sad scourge teach you not to sin against the spirit of the Creator, and not to renounce His eternal might, as your predecessors have done. They looked upon the earthly body as eternal, and believed that their entire being came from it, knowing nothing about their superior aspect. This attracted such a terrible curse. The Creator allowed their corpses to remain scattered and mixed with those of the beasts, to be an immemorial example, and especially to show that the Creator has not differentiated between man and beast, because they recognized no other origin than their own."

诺厄出方舟时，对与他同在的众受造物说：“大地与世人哪，你们要听；要以你们属灵的存有、而非属物质的存有听我。造物主是普世之圈内一切所存之物永恒的至高主宰，万有皆出于祂。愿这场可悲灾祸的废墟教导你们：不要得罪造物主的灵，不要像你们的先人那样弃绝祂永恒的大能。他们视属地之体为永恒，以为自己的全部存在皆出于它，对自己更高的一面一无所知。这便招致了如此可怕的咒诅。造物主容许他们的尸体散乱、与野兽的尸体混杂，作亘古的例证，尤其要显明：造物主并未把人与兽分别看待，因为他们除自己以外，不认任何别的本源。”

"Beware of lures with which others will tempt you and which, under the pretext of keeping you in fear of the Creator, will fling you into the general path of matter. My message is simple and pure. The truth announced to you by my word is transparent and without deceit; to the man of desire, it is self-evident, because it speaks a language to him that he cannot ignore, being wholly spiritual and directly emanated from the Creator. It cannot be communicated to non-rational animals but only to the spiritual minor. Someone greater than myself, who shall be born among your future generations, will instruct you more specifically about the reward that He has in mind for His creatures at the end of time." After this exhortation, Noah divided the earth among his three sons.

“你们要提防他人用以试探你们的种种诱饵——它们以「使你们敬畏造物主」为借口，却把你们抛入物质的普遍之路。我的讯息单纯而洁净。我的言所向你们宣告的真理是透明而无欺的；对渴慕之人而言，它不言自明，因为它向他所说的是一种他无法漠视的语言——全然属灵，直接从造物主流溢而出。它不能传通给无理性的动物，而只能传通给属灵的次者。一位大过我的、将生于你们未来世代之中者，必更具体地教导你们：在时间终结时，祂为其受造物所存留的赏报。”这番劝勉之后，诺厄把大地分给了他的三个儿子。

§132  The Ark, Example of the Saturnian Circle Where the Righteous Reside
§132  方舟，义人所居之土星之圈的例证

Rational minors who were in the ark, and the time that they remained there in privation from the elementary light, represent the retreat of the reconciled and righteous minors under the shadows of the greater light where they will reside for a length of time waiting. Although these righteous beings were assured of their reintegration, their torments were nevertheless considerable. The place where the righteous wait is philosophically called the rational or Saturnian circle. This place is the ladder to the supercelestial circles, and the Scriptures describe it as the resting place of the holy fathers reconciled with the Creator. It teaches us that, for the reintegration of reconciled beings, they must act spiritually in all spaces within the universal circle until they have completed the lesson that the Creator has set for minors.

方舟中那些有理性的次者，以及他们在其中被剥夺元素之光所度的时日，代表已修和的义人次者退居于「更大之光」的阴影之下——他们将在那里等候、居留一段时日。这些义人存有虽已确知其重新融合，其苦楚却仍相当深重。义人等候之处，在哲学上称为「理性之圈」或「土星之圈」（Saturnian circle）。此处是通往超天界诸圈的阶梯，圣经描述它为与造物主修和之众圣祖的安息之所。它教导我们：为使已修和的存有得以重新融合，他们必须在普世之圈内的一切空间中属灵地行动，直到他们完成造物主为诸次者所定的功课。

§133  The Devotion Rendered by Noah Reconciled the World
§133  诺厄所呈献的虔敬使世界得以修和

Noah, which means rest or relief, practiced divine worship every ten days after leaving the ark, up to the forty days that he remained on Mount Ararat. By this devotion of the divine man, or Christ, the Creator once again blessed Adam whom He had cursed. Through his invocation, Noah interceded for the Creator's mercy so that he could reconcile the earth and all those inhabitants who had found grace before Him. He said: "Yes, Eternal, Thy divine justice is perfect and so recognized by every spiritual creature. The most just spirit before Thee cannot sustain Thy light without trembling; how would feeble mortals in this valley of tears make themselves worthy of their divine reintegration without the help of Thy grace? O life-giving Creator, give life again to the general body upon which Thy spiritual creature must practice Thy divine devotion."

诺厄——此名意为「安息」或「舒缓」——出方舟之后，每十日行一次神性的敬拜，直到他在阿辣辣特山上所度的四十日为止。藉这神性之人（即基督）的虔敬，造物主再次祝福了祂曾咒诅的亚当。诺厄藉其祈请，为造物主的慈悲代求，好使他能使大地、并一切在祂面前蒙恩的居民修和。他说：“是的，永恒主啊，你神性的公义是完全的，为每一属灵受造物所公认。在你面前至义之灵，一见你的光便不能不战兢；这泪谷中软弱的必死者，若无你恩宠的帮助，又怎能使自己配得其神性的重新融合呢？赐生命的造物主啊，求你再次给那总体之体以生命——你属灵的受造物必须在其上践行对你的神性虔敬。”

§134  The Crow Recalls Cain and Announces Ham; the Dove; the Ark's Number
§134  乌鸦追念加音并预告含；鸽子；方舟之数

The crow came out of the ark before the earth was again visible to remind us of Cain's transgression and to foretell that of Ham's. It flew towards the South, to show us the place to which Cain had retreated and where Ham and his descendants would also dwell. The dove that left the ark is the true figure of the divine angelic spirit that directed and protected the ark, and represents the companion spirit of minors that surrounds them so as to protect them from the demoniacal onslaught. The addition of the shape and proportions of the ark (length, breadth, and height in cubits) produces the number eleven, indicating that this construction was the house of confusion; this number eleven is opposed to all kinds of complete corporeal form.

乌鸦在大地重现之前飞出方舟，为要使我们追念加音的悖逆，并预告含的悖逆。它朝南方飞去，向我们指明加音退居之地，也是含及其后裔将要居住之地。那飞离方舟的鸽子，是那导引并护卫方舟之神性天使之灵的真实形象，并代表环绕诸次者、保护他们免受魔性攻击的「同伴之灵」。方舟形状与比例（长、宽、高，以肘计）之数相加，得出数字「十一」，表明这建造之物乃是「混乱之屋」；这数字十一与一切完整的形体相对。

Before dry ground was found, universal reconciliation was prophesied to Noah by the sign commonly called the rainbow. The seven principal universal spirits appeared to him through a great sign of differently colored flames shaped in a semicircle, one end reaching the top of Mount Ararat and the other the ark. The dove then brought back an olive branch, by which Noah understood that his deliverance was near. The olive branch pointed out the fruit that they were to use for the anointing and marking of signed powers, as was later practiced in Israel. Noah's dividing of the earth among his three children repeats what Adam had done: he relegated Ham to the southern part where Cain had been, gave Shem the Western part which had been Abel's, and Japheth the northern part which had been Seth's; Noah and his wife stayed in the middle. This shows that the form of the world is triangular.

在寻得干地之前，普世的修和藉那通称为「虹」的征象向诺厄预示。七位主要的普世之灵，藉一个由各色火焰构成、形如半圆的大征象向他显现，其一端及于阿辣辣特山之顶，另一端及于方舟。鸽子随后衔回一枝橄榄枝，诺厄由此明白他的得救已近。这橄榄枝指出他们将要用以傅抹、并标记受印之权能的果实，正如后来在以色列中所行的。诺厄把大地分给三个孩子，重演了亚当所行的：他把含贬置于南方——加音曾在之处，把西方（曾属亚伯尔）给了闪，把北方（曾属舍特）给了耶斐特；诺厄与其妻则居于中部。这表明世界的形状是三角形的。

Before dismissing the inhabitants of the ark, Noah said: "The waters which rose up to the doors of the firmament represent the void of nature that had existed before the Creator conceived to create all things in His imagination. Beware of confusing creation with emanation! Creation only belongs to apparent matter, which, having come from nothing but the divine imagination, must return to nothingness; but emanation belongs to spiritual beings who are real and imperishable. The Eternal is called Creator not only for having created the universe, but also because He does not cease to create virtues and powers of spiritual action for the benefit of beings that emanate from Him. Never forget that all has come from Him, and not from these cursed tempter spirits who cast your kind into the horrible abyss of matter. Live in peace under the protection of the Creator in the region of the earth granted to each of you!"

在打发方舟居民离去之前，诺厄说：“那涨到苍穹之门的大水，代表在造物主构想于其想象中创造万物之前所存的自然之虚空。你们要慎防把『创造』与『流溢』相混！创造只属于表面之物质——它既除神性想象外一无所出，便必归于虚无；而流溢则属于真实而不朽的属灵存有。永恒主被称为造物主，不仅因祂创造了宇宙，也因祂不住地为那些从祂流溢之存有之益，创造属灵行动的德能与权能。你们永不可忘记：万有皆出于祂，而非出于这些把你们一类抛入物质可怖深渊的、受咒诅的试探之灵。你们要在造物主的护佑之下、在分给你们各人的那一方地土中，平安度日！”

Part VI — THE NOACHIC WISE MEN
第六部 — 诺厄的众智者
§139  A Second Generation from Noah Reestablishes the Ceremonial
§139  诺厄的第二个世代重建礼仪

After his instructions, Noah emancipated all of his people from his spiritual care so that each was in full command of their earthly virtues. He remained with his wife in the center of the earth, and he had numerous children. He had ten children, namely seven males and three females. Under these descendants, worship of the Creator was restored and pure sacrifices were offered to the Eternal. Each of the seven sons received a special gift from the Creator: one received the gift of spiritual works for the instruction of his brothers, another the gift of prophecy, another the gift of interpretation, and so on. Through these gifts, Noah's children regenerated the various forms of worship, and reestablished the various ceremonials, prayers, and invocations, as well as the times, hours, days, weeks, months, and years as they had existed.
