诺厄训诲之后，把他一切的子民从其属灵的照管中解放独立出来，使各人完全掌管自己属地的德能。他与妻子留在大地的中部，并有众多子女。他有十个孩子，即七男三女。在这些后裔之下，对造物主的敬拜得以恢复，纯洁的祭献献于永恒主。七个儿子各从造物主领受了一项特殊的恩赐：一个领受了为教导弟兄而行属灵之工的恩赐，另一个领受了预言的恩赐，又一个领受了释义的恩赐，如此等等。藉这些恩赐，诺厄的儿女重生了各样的敬拜形式，并重建了各样的礼仪、祈祷与祈请，以及曾经存在的时、刻、日、周、月、年。

§140  Noah and Adam's Two Generations
§140  诺厄与亚当的两个世代

It is not surprising that Noah had this second generation, which he called God-men of the earth, since he himself was the Creator's kind. Adam, having become impure by taking on a physical body, could only produce physical descendants who were condemned to practice a mixture of physical and spiritual worship. Noah repeated the same thing with his first three male descendants whom he had before the revelation of divine justice. Although these three children had not committed any abominations, they were nevertheless tarnished by the crimes committed in their presence. They purified themselves by fasting, prayer, and suffering. This atonement teaches us that no matter how just the minor is, he must always purify himself by spiritual fire from the tainting acquired while in the physical body. When Adam was reconciled, he had spiritual descendants bearing the name of the posterity of God; in the same way, Noah, after his spiritual election, had a second generation destined to practice only purely spiritual works.

诺厄有这第二个世代（他称之为「大地的神-人」），并不足为奇，因为他自己便是造物主之类。亚当既因取了形体之身而变得不洁，便只能产生属形体的后裔，这些后裔被判定要世世代代践行一种形体与属灵相混的敬拜。诺厄在其头三个男性后裔身上重演了同样之事——这三子是他在神性公义显明之前所生的。这三个孩子虽未曾犯任何可憎之事，却仍被那些在他们面前所犯之罪玷污。他们藉禁食、祈祷与苦楚洁净了自己。这补赎教导我们：无论次者何等公义，他都必须藉属灵之火，洁除其在形体之身时所沾染的玷污。亚当蒙修和时，有了承「天主后裔」之名的属灵后裔；同样，诺厄在其属灵的拣选之后，有了一个注定只践行纯属灵之工的第二世代。

§141  Relapsed Men and Their Punishment
§141  重蹈覆辙之人及其惩罚

The seven children of Noah's second generation became the seven spiritual divine pillars to sustain the universe. However, these wise men did not practice their mission for long. Those that they instructed soon turned to all sorts of passions and criminal greed. Not only were the transgressors affected by divine justice, but also their towns were annihilated. The city of Enoch built by Cain, the cities of Egypt, Sodom, Gomorrah, Jericho, Jerusalem, and so many others all suffered this fate. Their destruction proved that these works of men were only material things made according to the demoniacal intellect. No just person had been born in these cities; on the contrary, their inhabitants had always tried to get rid of all those who advocated spiritual practice. If we look around, we will see the same cupidity ruling today that gripped former centuries.

诺厄第二世代的七个孩子，成了扶持宇宙的七根属灵神性柱石。然而这些智者行使其使命未久。他们所教导的人，不久便转向种种情欲与罪恶的贪婪。悖逆者不仅受神性公义的影响，他们的城邑也被毁灭。加音所建的哈诺客城，以及埃及（Egypt）、索多玛、哈摩辣、耶里哥（Jericho；和合本：耶利哥）、耶路撒冷（Jerusalem；和合本：耶路撒冷）等诸城，无不遭此下场。它们的毁灭证明：世人这些工作不过是按魔性智识所造的物质之物。这些城中没有一个义人诞生；相反，其居民一向千方百计要除掉一切在他们中间倡导属灵修习的人。我们若环顾四周，便会看到今日主宰之贪婪，正与昔日诸世纪所盘踞的相同。

§142  The Destruction of Cities, in Former Times and Now
§142  古今城邑的毁灭

Do not think that the same catastrophes that fell upon ancient cities are falling upon our cities today, even though they are equally criminal. Just as it is within God's power to give new rewards to faithful minors, so it is within His power to exercise new punishments upon transgressors, which shows that it is impossible to escape divine justice. These ancient cities were struck in this way because the perfect septenary number of righteous men no longer existed on Earth, because the Creator had taken back the largest part of them. This was a warning that He was going to abandon people to their unfortunate fate; the only righteous descendants of Seth that He found were the blessed Noah and his children.

你切不可以为，那降于古城的同样灾难今日正降于我们的城邑之上，尽管它们同样有罪。正如把新的赏报赐给忠信的次者在天主的权能之内，把新的惩罚施于悖逆者也在祂的权能之内；这向我们表明：神性的公义无可逃避。这些古城之所以如此遭击打，是因为地上不再有义人的完满七元数，因为造物主已把他们中的大部分收回。这是一个警示：祂将把那时代的人交予其不幸的命运；祂所找到之舍特唯一的义裔，只有蒙福的诺厄与他的儿女。

§143  About the Calendar Observed by Noah's Second Generation
§143  论诺厄第二世代所遵行的历法

Noah's second generation of children had each received a divine spiritual gift, and they settled on the different intervals of time suitable for their various forms of devotion. However, to make this spiritual division of time, they followed a method of calculation quite different from the one that their elder worldly brothers had used, which was a mixture of the spiritual and the earthly. For the basic growing of crops, do you not need to observe the intervals of time and use different methods? If a farmer neglected to observe these things, his sowing would have been in vain. This is an indispensable law from the Creator. Why then would you not expect divine spiritual worship to be equally subject to law, to a precise ceremonial, and to a faithful observance of time and the seasons?

诺厄第二世代的儿女各领受了一项神性属灵恩赐，他们订定了适于其各样虔敬之不同的时段。然而，为作这属灵的时间划分，他们所循的计算方法，与其属世的兄长们所用的（那是属灵与属地相混的）大不相同。就基本的庄稼耕种而言，你岂不需要观察时段、并使用不同的方法吗？农夫若疏于观察这些，他的撒种便归徒然。这是造物主一条不可或缺的律法。既然如此，你又怎会不指望神性的属灵敬拜同样受制于律法、受制于精确的礼仪、并受制于对时间与节令的忠实遵守呢？

§144  The Superiority of the Devotion Rendered by Noah's Second Generation
§144  诺厄第二世代所呈虔敬的优越

Whoever wishes to practice two talents at the same time can practice none with perfection, but one who has only one talent and follows it with precision is sure to end up having it more perfectly. For this reason, the children of Noah's second generation excelled in spiritual devotion and surpassed their elder brothers. Noah's second generation was the great example of the seven principal, superior, and divine spirits, and represented the true type of seven principal spiritual beings who work to sustain this universe. These wise ones, in their state of divine justice, could not be limited by a time of worldly darkness, as are ordinary mortals. This darkness, called night, would not have existed if the first father had not transgressed.

凡想同时操练两种才能者，便一种也不能操练到完美；而只有一种才能、并精确地专注于它者，终必将它掌握得更为完美。为此缘故，诺厄第二世代的儿女在属灵虔敬上出类拔萃，胜过了他们的兄长。诺厄的第二世代，是那七位主要、上位、神性之灵的伟大例证，代表着那七位为维系此宇宙而工作之主要属灵存有的真实预表。这些智者处于其神性公义之态中，不能像寻常必死者那样受制于属世黑暗之时。这黑暗——名为「夜」——倘若元祖不曾悖逆，本不会存在。

§145  Noah's Second Generation Is Equal to Glorious Adam
§145  诺厄的第二世代与荣耀的亚当同等

Noah's second generation was truly one from God, in that it was conceived without the excesses of the material senses. Furthermore, although these beings were enveloped in a corporeal form, they enjoyed the same virtues and powers that Adam possessed in his state of glory. For these wise men, devoted entirely to divine practice for the greatest glory of the Creator, times were set down for their spiritual acts according to the will of Divinity. At the same time, they received all of the immutable laws to observe, each according to their special gift.

诺厄的第二世代真正是出于天主的，因为它是不藉物质感官之过度而孕育的。此外，这些存有虽被裹于形体之中，却享有亚当在其荣耀之态中所具的同样德能与权能。对这些全然献身于神性修习、为造物主至大荣耀的智者，其属灵之举的时限乃按神性的意志所定。同时，他们领受了当遵守的一切不可更易之律法，各按其特殊的恩赐。

§146  Gifts and Procedures of the Four First-Born of Noah's Second Generation
§146  诺厄第二世代四位长子的恩赐与程序

The first-born of this generation was an example of the interpreter spirit, having received the gift of interpreting the talents of his six brothers. He fixed the intervals of time necessary for his practice at quarterly intervals of our ordinary day. The second-born's gift was prophecy; he fixed half of the period by joining to the first interval a similar interval of six hours. It was the four first-born who fixed the four intervals of the day by each practicing their spiritual works during six hours. The third received the gift of astronomy, and the fourth the gift of knowledge of the powerful word the Creator used for His temporal creation, who also acted for the healing of illnesses. These four first-born were the type of prophets from the past and the future; from their work came the present twenty-four-hour day of day and night, though the wise ones themselves were not subject to time.

这一世代的长子是「诠释者之灵」的例证，领受了诠释其六位弟弟才能的恩赐。他把其修习所需的时段定为我们平常一日的四分之一。次子的恩赐是预言；他把前一时段再接上一个相似的六小时时段，从而定出半个周期。正是这四位长子，各在六小时内践行其属灵之工，从而定出一日的四个时段。第三子领受了天文学的恩赐，第四子领受了对造物主用于其属时创造之大能之言的知识之恩赐——他也曾为医治疾病而行事。这四位长子是过去与未来众先知的预表；今日的昼夜二十四小时之日即由其工而来，尽管这些智者自身并不受制于时间。

§147  Importance of the Spiritual Division of Time
§147  属灵时间划分的重要

It is impossible to suppose that for the spirit time exists. The intervals that the sages marked for their spiritual works cannot be taken into account in regard to their nature as thinking beings, and the temporal day could not be a limitation on their spirit as it is for corporeal nature. By marking out their spiritual intervals, they intended that it was the spirit itself that had marked out the temporal intervals of time. The nations among whom these sages lived could not properly differentiate between this spiritual division and the ordinary division of time; this caused gross errors of calculation, and caused them to mistake one of these spiritual intervals for one of our temporal days.

断不能设想时间对灵而言存在。贤者为其属灵之工所标定的时段，就其作为思想存有的本性而言，不可计入考量；属时之日不可能像对形体之自然那样，成为对其灵的限制。他们标定其属灵时段，其用意乃是：正是灵本身标定了那属时的时间之段。这些贤者所居其中的诸民族，无法正确区分这属灵的划分与那寻常的时间划分；这造成了严重的计算错误，使他们把这些属灵时段之一误当作我们属时的一日。

§148  Gifts of the Three Last-Born of Noah's Second Generation
§148  诺厄第二世代三位幼子的恩赐

The fifth of these descendants received the gift of planting and agriculture. The sixth, knowledge of literary and hieroglyphic characters of divine nature, and also of every demoniacal spiritual being. The seventh received the gift of constructing spiritual buildings, just as Adam, Seth, Enoch, and Noah had. Moses showed us that he had the same gift through his construction of the mysterious ark, altar, and tabernacle: Moses drew up the building plans, and Bezaleel constructed them. The first four, who fixed the day into four periods of work, did not marry, being entirely devoted to worship. They represented the just such as Enoch, Melchizedek, Eli, and the Christ. Two of these were raised from the center of the earth by spiritual fire, and the other two were raised in their own bodies of divine glory, as the Christ demonstrated through his resurrection.

这些后裔中的第五子领受了种植与农耕的恩赐。第六子领受了对属神性之文字与象形字符、并对每一魔性属灵存有之字符的知识。第七子领受了建造属灵殿宇的恩赐，正如亚当、舍特、哈诺客与诺厄所曾有的。梅瑟藉其建造奥秘的约柜、祭坛与会幕，向我们显明他有同样的恩赐：梅瑟绘制建造图样，贝匝肋耳（Bezaleel；和合本：比撒列）负责建造。那把一日定为四段工作的头四位，并未婚娶，因他们全然献身于敬拜。他们代表诸义者，如哈诺客、默基瑟德、厄里（Eli；和合本：以利）与基督。这四位中有两位藉属灵之火从大地中心被提升，另两位则以其神性荣耀之身被提升，正如基督藉其复活所清楚展示的。

§150  The Inferiority of Noah's First Descendants
§150  诺厄头一批后裔的次等

Noah emancipated his first three children, Shem, Ham, and Japheth. They needed only to concern themselves with settling down and cultivating the part of the Earth apportioned to them. For quite a long time, they did not meditate upon the spiritual instructions that Noah had given them. Their divine devotion was limited to a firm belief that an all-powerful being existed above all created things, whom they called Abarin 8, which, in the language of Noah, means doubly powerful spirit by whom the Creator has accomplished all things. This word, whether Chinese or Noachite, is the same as the one the Jews used to utter. Adam and his descendants also spoke this word, having been the first to use the Judaic language which divine spiritual nature had kept for her minor creature.

诺厄把他头三个孩子——闪、含、耶斐特——解放独立出去。他们只须操心安顿下来、耕种分给他们的那一方地土。有相当长的时间，他们并不默想诺厄给他们的属灵教诲。他们的神性虔敬，仅限于坚信有一位全能的存有存在于一切受造之物之上，他们称之为「阿巴林」（Abarin，8）——此词在诺厄的语言中意为「双重大能之灵，造物主藉之成就了万事」。这个词，无论是中国的还是诺厄裔的，都与犹太人（Jews）过去所诵之词相同。亚当及其后裔也说这个词，他们是最先使用犹太语（Judaic language）的——那是神性属灵之自然为其次者受造物所保存的语言。

§151  Jew, Hebrew, Israelite
§151  犹太人、希伯来人、以色列人

I am making a distinction here between the word Jew and the Judaic tongue, as with the word Hebrew and the Hebraic language. The word Jew means righteous, and the Judaic tongue signifies the language of the holiness of the divine spirit. The word Hebrew signifies the descendants of a wise man that the Scriptures call Eber, and the Hebraic tongue means the language of Eber's descendants. This language is very different from the Judaic one, because among Eber's descendants there are none who are righteous. True Jews recognize that the alphabetical origin of their language is celestial; they find all of the characters of this language clearly written in the arrangement of the stars. Israel and Israelite do not have the same meaning: Israel means striving against God, while Israelite means striving in God. That is why I give this word to the Noachite generation of wise men.

我在此把「犹太人」（Jew）一词与「犹太语」（Judaic tongue）区分开来，正如把「希伯来人」（Hebrew）一词与「希伯来语」（Hebraic language）区分开来。「犹太人」一词意为「义人」，「犹太语」指那导引义人之工的神性之灵之圣洁的语言。「希伯来人」一词指圣经所称厄贝尔（Eber；和合本：希伯）之一智者的后裔，「希伯来语」意为厄贝尔后裔的语言。这语言与犹太语大不相同，因为厄贝尔的后裔中没有一个是义人。真正的犹太人认得：他们语言之字母的本源是属天的；他们在星辰的排列中清楚地看见这语言的一切字符。「以色列」与「以色列人」意义不同：「以色列」意为「与天主相争」，「以色列人」意为「在天主里奋力」。为此，我把这称号给诺厄裔的智者世代。

All of this tells us, then, that the word Hebrew means confusion, just as it tells us that the word Israel, given to these people by order of the Creator, means firmly against the Eternal. Nothing in the world is more pleasing and powerful towards the Creator than the prayer of the Jews, and nothing is more indifferent and destructive than the heart of the Hebrew. This should not surprise you since these people are no longer in possession of the divine laws, and are happy with a ceremonial which has been ignominiously taken away from them.

这一切告诉我们：「希伯来」一词意为「混乱」，正如它清楚告诉我们：奉造物主之命赐给这民之「以色列」一词，意为「坚决与永恒主相争」。世上没有什么比犹太人的祈祷与祈请更蒙造物主喜悦、更有力量的了；也没有什么比希伯来人的心更冷漠、更具毁灭性的了。这不该使你惊讶，因为这民已不再拥有神性的律法，却满足于一套已被可耻地从他们手中夺走的礼仪。

§152  Religion of the Second Noachites, of Moses, and of the Church
§152  第二批诺厄裔、梅瑟与教会的宗教

Noah instructed his second generation for 130 years. He raised seven male children; he rendered the four first-born of true thinkers to the sole Divinity, devoted only to divine worship. The first-born was of the type of the high priests of the future. Noah's first-born was the first to use the censer and make the sacrificial offering to the Creator, and the only one to utter softly the great invocation for the descent of the spirit; his three brothers stood behind him as chief assistants. This was repeated by Moses, who was assisted by Aaron, Ur, and Bezaleel. The same order has been followed in the service of the Temple of Solomon. The Church of the Christ still demonstrates this in the sacrament offered at the altar by the celebrant, assisted by the first, second, and third deacons. All things of this kind have really been handed down from former times, and did not come from men's imagination but from the eternal Creator.

诺厄教诲其第二世代达一百三十年。他养育了七个男孩；他把其中四位真正思想者的长子献于独一的神性，使之只献身于神性的敬拜。长子属于将来大司祭的预表。诺厄的长子是第一个使用香炉、向造物主献上祭献的人，也是唯一一个轻声诵念那使圣灵降临之大祈请的人；他的三个弟弟作为主要辅礼者，紧列于其身后。这一切由梅瑟所重演——他有亚郎（Aaron；和合本：亚伦）、胡尔（Ur/Hur；和合本：户珥）与贝匝肋耳协助。撒罗满圣殿的礼仪中也遵循同样的次序。基督的教会至今仍在祭台上所献的圣事中向我们展示这一点——由主礼者，并由第一、第二、第三位执事协助。凡此种种，实在都是从古时传下来的，并非出于人的想象，而是出于永恒的造物主。

The three last-born sons were commanded to visit the three earthly regions – West, North, and South – inhabited by Noah's first descendants Shem, Ham, and Japheth. They left with their sisters, whom they took as wives. The eldest of the three went to live in the South, the second in the West, and the third in the North, or the Aquilon. They each went to carry on the ceremonial of divine devotion among their brothers, so that these peoples would not entirely lose sight of the devotion due to the Creator. They had to start by preaching a purely temporal doctrine, so as to make themselves understood, and to then elevate these nations from the worldly to the divine spiritual religion.

三个幼子受命去探访三个地界之区域——西、北、南——即诺厄头一批后裔闪、含、耶斐特所居之地。他们带着自己的姊妹同去，娶她们为妻。三人中的长者去南方居住，次者去西方，第三者去北方（即「北风之地」Aquilon）。他们各自去在其弟兄中间延续神性虔敬的礼仪，好使这些民族不致完全丧失对造物主当尽之虔敬。然而他们必须先从宣讲一套纯属时的道理入手，好使自己被人理解，然后再把这些民族从属世的、提升到神性属灵的宗教。

§154  Successful Preaching of the Three Last-Born
§154  三位幼子成功的宣讲

These peoples did not have their hours, days, weeks, months, and seasons fixed; they lived more or less like animals, other than recognizing a Being superior to themselves. The three sages started by establishing a measurement of time regulated on the spiritual divisions that their four brothers had set up. They then began to initiate some of the inhabitants, making them understand that although the night was made for the rest of man's body, it was not made for the divine spiritual minor, who could not remain idle. From among their disciples, they chose four of the most capable. Each disciple was placed in a mysterious circle of activity for six hours, so that the four operations started at sunrise and finished the following sunrise. From this activity came the first calculations by Noah's children, also called Noachites or Chinese, because the nations of the Chinese and the Japanese came directly from the descendants of Shem, Ham, and Japheth, who lived in the region of China from which all peoples on earth came.

这些民族没有固定的时、日、周、月与节令；他们活得与动物相差无几，只不过承认有一位高于自己的存有。三位贤者先着手确立一种时间的度量，以其四位兄长所设立的属灵划分为准。随后他们开始引领一些居民入门，使他们明白：夜虽是为人身体的安息而设，却不是为神性属灵的次者而设——次者因其属灵本性不能闲置。他们从门徒中拣选了四位最有能力的。每位门徒被安置于一个奥秘的活动之圈中六小时，以致这四桩运作始于日出、终于次日日出。这一活动便生出诺厄儿女——亦称诺厄裔或中国人——最初的计算；因为中国与日本的民族，直接源于闪、含、耶斐特的后裔，他们住在中国这一区域，地上一切民族皆由此而出。

§155  Daily Calculations and the Four Daily Practices
§155  每日的计算与每日四课

The work carried out by the first four disciples was the principle behind their calculation of the day. Each divine spiritual procedure, carried out at intervals of six hours, made up a day. Four intervals of spiritual practice establish a complete time in the spirit. Hence the four procedures divided ordinary days into four equal parts; by this means, these masters made four days of one of our ordinary ones. The Chinese introduced this spiritual reckoning into their daily temporal calculations. The reason we know that this division was established among these nations is that this usage was exactly recalled in the various divine religions practiced by Abraham, Ishmael, Isaac, and Jacob. Do we not see this division observed today in the four intervals of prayers held in our churches?

头四位门徒所行之工，乃是他们计算一日的原理。每一桩以六小时为间隔所行的神性属灵程序，构成一日。四个属灵修习的时段，在灵中构成一个完整的时。因此，这四道程序把寻常之日分为四个相等的部分；藉此，这些师父把我们平常的一日化作了四日。中国人把这属灵的推算引入了他们每日的属时计算中。我们何以知道这一划分曾在这些民族中确立？因为这一用法，在亚巴郎、依市玛耳、依撒格（Isaac；和合本：以撒）与雅各伯（Jacob；和合本：雅各）所行的种种神性宗教中被精确地重现。我们今日岂不也在我们教会所举行的四段祈祷中，看见这一划分被遵行吗？

§156  The Septenary Applied and Its Multiple Symbolism
§156  七元数的应用及其多重象征

The three spiritual masters joined three more disciples to the four, so that each sage had seven disciples. They thus fixed the septenary number, because the Eternal had operated six divine thoughts for universal creation and, on the seventh day, bestowed seven spiritual gifts upon all of His creation, and linked seven principal spirits to sustain it. This joining is shown by the seven planets. The Holy Scriptures also teach this by the seven angels, seven archangels, seven seraphim, seven cherubim, seven thrones, seven dominations, seven judges of Israel, the seventy years of captivity of Israel, the seven weeks of Daniel, the seven days of the week, the seven gifts that the Christ gave to his disciples, the seven branches of Moses's candelabra put in Solomon's temple, still represented in the church of St. Peter in Rome. And so the universe, having been conceived by the septenary number, will also be reintegrated by this same number.

三位属灵师父又在那四位之外增添了三位门徒，使每位贤者都有七位门徒。他们如此定下了七元数，因为永恒主为普世创造运作了六个神性思想，并在第七日把七种属灵恩赐赐予祂一切的创造，又联结了七位主要之灵来维系它。这一联结由七大行星所显示。圣经也藉以下诸「七」教导此点：七位天使、七位总领天使、七位色辣芬、七位革鲁宾、七个宝座、七个统辖、以色列的七位士师、以色列七十年的被掳、达尼尔的七个七、一周的七日、基督赐予门徒的七种恩赐、梅瑟置于撒罗满圣殿之七枝灯台（至今仍在罗马圣伯多禄 St. Peter；和合本：彼得 大殿中有其表征）。如此，宇宙既藉七元数而被构想，也将藉这同一数而被重新融合。

§157  The Months of the Noachites or Chinese
§157  诺厄裔或中国人的月份

For each of the seven disciples, the wise ones traced out four consecutive days of divine spiritual practices, so that each was entirely devoted from one sunrise to the next. Each of the seven disciples then had six whole ordinary days of physical rest. When they calculated their activities, they found that they numbered twenty-eight intervals. They reflected that the effect of the moon's activity on Earth came to the same number of twenty-eight. Seeing that the lunar action and their own were equal, they adopted twenty-eight spiritual days as a spiritual month, introduced into their civil history as ordinary temporal months. This is how the Chinese made four months for one of those that we use today. Thus, the Noachites or Chinese calculated their 13 years for one of our ordinary years, and followed this for the first four centuries.

贤者为七位门徒中的每一位，划出连续四日的神性属灵修习，使各人从一次日出到下一次日出全然投入。七位门徒随后各有连续六整个平常之日的形体安息。当他们计算其活动时，发现共有二十八个时段。他们思量到：月亮在地上活动的效果，恰好也是二十八这个数。见月之作为与他们自己的相等，他们便采用二十八个属灵之日为一个属灵之月，并引入其民间历史作为寻常的属时月份。这便是中国人如何以四个月抵我们今日所用一月之法。如此，诺厄裔或中国人以他们的十三年抵我们平常的一年，并在最初四个世纪中遵循这一计算。

§158  Lunar Calculation and Solar Calculation
§158  太阴计算与太阳计算

I am not hiding from you the fact that the lunar calculation is the first given to man by the Creator, and that the solar calculation was adopted almost only by Christians. Lunar calculation came through the wise men of whom we have just spoken, and is the one which raises man to the highest level of knowledge of universal nature and its cycles. It is essential that the man of desire be instructed about the four different ways in which the moon operates in the universe: as new moon, first quarter, full moon, and last quarter. I shall explain this when I deal with the course of various planetary bodies.

我不向你隐瞒这一事实：太阴计算是造物主最先赐予人的，而太阳计算几乎只为基督徒所采用。太阴计算是藉我们方才所说的贤者而来的，也是那把人提升到对普世自然及其周期之最高知识的计算。渴慕之人必须受教，知晓月在宇宙中运作的四种不同方式：即新月、上弦、满月与下弦。当我论及各行星之体的运行时，我将解释这一点。

§159  Chinese Elections and Recalculation of the Times of Activities
§159  中国人的拣选与活动时间的重算

As the descendants grew, a succession to the temporal and spiritual father was created in each lineage. These new leaders chose the seven most zealous from among their disciples. They changed the times of their activities, practicing devotion only once a week, thereby increasing their years to seven weeks. As descendants increased, three new leaders conducted larger elections; the number of elected was increased to twenty-one, making sixty-three in all. Later successors fixed the time to once every two weeks, at the new moon and just before the full moon. Once the successor of the Western region deceased, his successor practiced devotion only twice a year, on the equinoxes; the successor of the Northern region, only once a year, on the March equinox. The successor of the South, however, persuaded Ham's nation never to change the order, threatening them with the Creator's curse.

随着后裔增多，各支系中都设立了对属时与属灵之父的继承。这些新首领从门徒中拣选七位最热心的。他们更改了活动的时间，一周只行一次虔敬，从而把他们的「年」增至七周。后裔既增，三位新首领便举行了更大规模的拣选；被选者增至二十一人，共计六十三人。后来的继任者把时间定为每两周一次，即在新月、并满月之前。西方之区域的继任者去世后，其后继者一年只行两次虔敬，即在两分之时（春分、秋分）；北方之区域的继任者，一年只行一次，即在三月之春分。然而南方的继任者，却劝服含之民族永不更改此次序，以造物主的咒诅相威吓。

The three nations each followed their last spiritual calculation: Ham taking four seasons for four years, Shem following the two equinoxes for two years, and Japheth the March equinox until the next as one year, which we follow today. They persevered with them for a long time after the iniquitous activities of Nembrod in Babylon, until they were shamefully separated from divine devotion. By their divisions, one can see the reason for their saying that they preceded Adam by fifteen to twenty thousand years. These nations do not regard the flood as universal, because the three men who witnessed it took every measure to erase it from their memories, so as not to frighten their descendants. This weakness can still be seen in their Noachite or Chinese descendants who live in terrible fear and who worship animals with superstitious devotion, looking upon them as gods or demons. I cannot ignore these accounts, having seen them myself.

这三个民族各自遵循其最后一次属灵计算：含以四季抵四年，闪按两分抵两年，耶斐特以三月春分至次年三月春分为一年——这正是我们今日所遵循的。在尼默洛得（Nembrod/Nimrod；和合本：宁录）于巴比伦（Babylon）行不义之事之后，他们仍长久固执地坚持这些计算，直到他们可耻地与神性虔敬分离。从他们的这些划分，人便可看出他们何以说自己比亚当早一万五千至两万年。这些民族不把洪水视为普世性的，因为那三位目睹洪水之人，曾竭尽一切措施把它从记忆中抹去，以免惊吓其后裔。这一软弱，至今仍可在其诺厄裔或中国后裔身上看到——他们活在极大的恐惧中，以迷信的虔敬崇拜动物，把它们看作神或魔。我不能无视这些记述，因为我亲眼见过。

§161  Traditional but Eminent, the Devotion Practiced by the Christ
§161  基督所行的虔敬——传承而卓越

Meditate deeply on all of the different rules of dividing time for divine devotion established by the three nations. You will find everything that the wise men of Egypt practiced, and by Abraham, Moses, and Solomon. The sages of Egypt excelled in astronomy; the religion of Abraham was superior to that of the Egyptians; Moses surpassed Abraham and the Egyptians; the devotion in Solomon's temple was above those before; and the Christ's devotional practice was infinitely greater than all. Through his divine institution, the Christ left his disciples the prayer for every six hours which completes the day; the Christian Church still conducts its prayers four times a day. Secondly, the Christ fixed the time to practice four great observances, and the Church follows this by its four annual celebrations, two at the solstices and two at the equinoxes. This shows that the ceremonial and its times were fixed from the beginning, transmitted by the divine spirit, and did not come from human conventions.

深深默想那三个民族为神性虔敬所确立之划分时间的种种不同规则罢。你将从中找到埃及智者、并亚巴郎、梅瑟与撒罗满所践行的一切。埃及的贤者精于天文；亚巴郎所行的宗教胜过埃及人的；梅瑟又超过亚巴郎与埃及人；撒罗满圣殿中的虔敬又在此前诸者之上；而基督的虔敬修习则无限地大过这一切。基督藉其神性的设立，把每六小时一次、以完成一日的祈祷留给了他的门徒；基督的教会至今仍每日祈祷四次。其次，基督定下了践行四大敬礼的时间，教会藉其每年的四次庆节遵行之——两次在至日（冬至、夏至），两次在分日（春分、秋分）。这表明礼仪及其时间自起初便已定妥，是由神性之灵所传，而非出于人的成规。

Part VII — ABRAHAM
第七部 — 亚巴郎（Abraham；和合本：亚伯拉罕）
§162  The Abram-Abraham Type
§162  亚巴兰—亚巴郎的预表

You know that the name Abram was changed to Abraham. The former means earthly carnal father, exalted above ordinary fathers. There has never been a man more exalted in carnal posterity than Abraham. This is why the Scriptures call him Abram, or exalted father, and not Abraham, or exalted father of a multitude of descendants in God, as Adam should have been in his state of glory, but who instead became exalted father of an earthly material posterity through his transgression. Abraham succeeded here in spite of Adam, because Abraham truly came from God's posterity. The Creator made His particular and general choice from Abraham's descendants, firstly to reveal His justice and secondly His glory.

你知道，「亚巴兰」（Abram；和合本：亚伯兰）之名被改为「亚巴郎」（Abraham；和合本：亚伯拉罕）。前者意为「属地属肉身之父，被高举于寻常父亲之上」。在属肉身的后裔上，从未有人比亚巴郎更被高举。这便是圣经何以称他为亚巴兰、即「被高举之父」，而非亚巴郎、即「在天主里众多后裔之被高举之父」——后者本是荣耀之态中的亚当所当是的，亚当却因其悖逆，反成了属地物质后裔之被高举之父。亚巴郎在此虽亚当而成功，因为亚巴郎真正出自天主的后裔。造物主从亚巴郎的后裔中作了祂个别与总体的拣选，其一为彰显祂的公义，其二为彰显祂的荣耀。

§163  Ishmael and Hagar
§163  依市玛耳与哈加尔

The Scriptures also give Abraham the name of exalted father of a multitude of confusion. These three explanations come from Abraham's first three descendants: Ishmael, Isaac, and Jacob. Ishmael, a type of divine mercy, also represents Adam's physical activity for his carnal posterity. Their son Ishmael, a result of the cupidity of the material senses, was excluded from his father's house because he had been conceived with no involvement of divine will, following the pattern of the exclusion of Cain. The bread and water that Ishmael and Hagar received from Abraham represented the last spiritual nourishment they received, like the last nourishment Cain received after conceiving of Abel's murder. The angel who appeared to Hagar and satisfied their hunger reminds us of the grace that the Eternal extended to Cain and his sister, giving them the invincible seal of His divinity. Hagar and Ishmael's place of residence was the same as where Cain had been banished.

圣经也给亚巴郎起名「混乱众人之被高举之父」。这三种解释出自亚巴郎最初的三个后裔：依市玛耳（Ishmael；和合本：以实玛利）、依撒格（Isaac；和合本：以撒）与雅各伯（Jacob；和合本：雅各）。依市玛耳是神性慈悲的预表，也代表亚当为其属肉身后裔所行的形体活动。他们的儿子依市玛耳，是物质感官贪欲之果，被逐出父家，因为他的孕育无神性意志的参与，正循加音被逐的样式。依市玛耳与哈加尔（Hagar；和合本：夏甲）从亚巴郎所得的饼与水，代表他们所得的最后属灵滋养，正如加音起意谋杀亚伯尔之后所得的最后滋养。那向哈加尔显现、满足他们饥渴的天使，使我们想起永恒主向加音及其姊所施的恩宠——赐给他们祂神性不可胜的印记。哈加尔与依市玛耳的居所，正是加音被逐之处。

§164  Abraham and Adam, Ishmael and Cain
§164  亚巴郎与亚当、依市玛耳与加音

Abraham and Ishmael's activities and those of Adam are similar. Abraham was exalted in a material posterity by the birth of his son Ishmael, as Adam had been. By his son, Abraham found himself the father of twelve tribes, as the angel had announced to Hagar. In Adam, too, there were twelve tribes if we count him, the eight patriarchs from Seth up to and including Noah, and Noah's three children. The twelve tribes of Ishmael are a precursor for those of Israel and of the Christ. They had no connection with the tribes of Israel, because Ishmael, father of these twelve tribes, had repeated Cain's pattern of transgression and reconciliation.

亚巴郎与依市玛耳的活动，同亚当的相似。亚巴郎藉其子依市玛耳的诞生，在物质的后裔上被高举，正如亚当曾被高举一般。藉其子，亚巴郎成了十二支派之父，正如天使向哈加尔所宣告的。在亚当身上也有十二支派——若把他自己、从舍特直到诺厄（含诺厄在内）的八位圣祖、并诺厄的三个孩子算在一起。依市玛耳的十二支派，是以色列诸支派、并基督诸支派的先导。它们与以色列诸支派并无关联，因为这十二支派之父依市玛耳，重演了加音悖逆与修和的样式。

§165  Transgression of the Tribes of Ishmael
§165  依市玛耳诸支派的悖逆

The Ishmaelite tribes remained under divine protection so long as they observed the rite that the Creator had outlined through His angel. However, having made an alliance with the descendants of Ham and those from Canaan, these tribes were abandoned by the Creator. By their transgression, they became like Enoch's descendants when they were linked with Cain's. The Creator had excluded them from the society of children of God.

依市玛耳诸支派，只要遵守造物主藉其天使所勾画的礼仪，便一直在神性的护佑之下。然而，它们既与含的后裔、并来自客纳罕（Canaan；和合本：迦南）之人结盟，便被造物主撇弃。藉其悖逆，它们变得如哈诺客的后裔与加音的后裔相联时一般。造物主已把它们从「天主子女」的团体中排除出去。

§166  The Typology Is Permanent
§166  预表是恒常的

You can see by this linking that all eras and first elections are repeated, and they will continue until the end of time. The rest of this treatise will make this even better understood when I show clearly that, in the end, everything will return as it was in the beginning. Let us pass on to Abraham's second descendant.

藉这一关联，你可以看出：一切纪元与最初的拣选都在重演，并将持续到时间的终结。本论的余下部分将使此点更加明了——那时我将清楚显明：到末了，一切都将回归到它起初的样子。让我们转到亚巴郎的第二个后裔。

§167  Isaac, Second Descendant of Abraham, in Part Reconciled
§167  依撒格，亚巴郎的第二个后裔，部分蒙修和

Having been partly reconciled with the Creator, Abraham had a son with his wife Sarah, by divine authority, although her advanced age should have placed her outside the possibility of conception. This child, named Isaac, was conceived without the passion of the material senses, and perfectly repeats the birth of Adam's second descendant in his son Abel. Isaac followed exactly the spiritual instructions that his father gave him about the various forms of devotion that he was destined to fulfill. This again repeats the Abel type under the spiritual guidance of Adam.

亚巴郎既已部分地与造物主修和，便凭神性的权柄与其妻撒辣（Sarah；和合本：撒拉）生了一子，尽管撒辣年事已高，本应无受孕之可能。这孩子名叫依撒格，是不藉物质感官之情欲而孕育的，完满地重演了亚当在其子亚伯尔身上第二个后裔的诞生。依撒格丝毫不差地遵行了他父亲就他注定要履行之种种虔敬所给的属灵教诲。这又在亚当的属灵引导之下，重演了亚伯尔的预表。

Part VIII — ISAAC
第八部 — 依撒格（Isaac；和合本：以撒）
§168  Isaac's Sacrifice
§168  依撒格的献祭

Having reached the age of thirty and been perfectly instructed in the spiritual divine sciences, Isaac let his father know of his desire to participate in the great divine rite for the glory of the Creator, and to offer a sacrifice to the Eternal. Abraham answered, "That it be so, my son, and may the sacrifice you propose serve to atone for people of the world so that they may regain grace." They left at sunrise to go to Mount Moriah (this word is divided into two parts: Mor, which means "destruction of apparent corporeal forms," and iah which means "vision of the Creator"). They took two servants and a donkey. Some distance from the mountain, they left their two servants behind to represent the future abandonment of the two nations of Ishmael and Israel from the divine religion. They kept the donkey to represent the ignorance in which these two nations would find themselves — this is what the Christ represented upon entering Jerusalem mounted on a donkey.

依撒格年满三十、并在属灵神性之学上受了完满的教导之后，便向父亲表明他愿参与那为造物主荣耀而设的伟大神性礼仪，并向永恒主献一祭。亚巴郎回答：“我儿，就照你所愿的罢；愿你所拟献的祭，能为世上的人赎罪，使他们重得恩宠。”他们于日出时出发前往摩黎雅山（Mount Moriah；此词分为两部分：Mor 意为「表面形体之毁灭」，iah 意为「造物主之异象」）。他们带了两个仆人和一头驴。离山尚有一段距离时，他们把两个仆人留在身后，以代表依市玛耳与以色列这两个民族日后从神性宗教中的离弃。他们留下那驴，以代表这两个民族日后所陷的无知——这正是基督骑驴进入耶路撒冷时所代表的。

When they reached the summit, Abraham made his invocation to the Creator and, with complete resignation, offered Him his son Isaac. Isaac agreed to offer himself and placed himself in a position for sacrifice. Abraham took the knife and was ready to strike when the spirit of the Lord, who saw the purity of these two men's intent, struck Abraham to the ground so that he was unable to complete his sacrifice. The spirit said the Creator was satisfied with their motives. Abraham lifted his son off the pyre and said, "Remember, my child, that the greatest sacrifice that one can make to the Creator is the word and the motive." Then, seeing a ram in a bush, they caught it and offered it in sacrifice to complete the operation. It was then that they had perfect knowledge of the Creator's will and the different animals to be used for sacrifice. This shows us that true worship of the Creator has always survived among men.

他们到了山顶，亚巴郎向造物主祈请，怀着完全的顺服把他的儿子依撒格献给祂。依撒格同意献上自己，摆好了受献的姿势。亚巴郎举刀正要下手之际，那真在场、并看见这两人意向之洁净的上主之灵，把亚巴郎击倒在地，使他无法完成其献祭。那灵说：造物主已满意于他们的动机。亚巴郎把儿子从柴堆上扶起来，说：“我的孩子，你要记得：人所能献给造物主的最大祭献，乃是言与动机。”随后，他们见丛林中有一只公绵羊，便捉住它献为祭，以完成这一运作。正是那时，他们对造物主的意志、并当用于献祭的各样动物有了完满的知识。这向我们表明：对造物主的真敬拜，在世人中一向存续。

§169  The Sacrifice of Isaac and the Sacrifice of Abel
§169  依撒格的献祭与亚伯尔的献祭

Although Abraham's sacrifice is a representation of the one made of Abel, there is a great difference: Abel was truly sacrificed to accomplish the complete reconciliation of his father Adam, while Isaac was sacrificed only in the thought and motive of his father Abraham. This thought and intent were enough for Abraham to be reconciled with the Creator. This should not surprise you, given that Adam's crime, greater than Abraham's, demanded greater atonement.

亚巴郎的献祭虽是亚伯尔献祭的表征，二者却有一大差别：亚伯尔是真正被献上，以成就其父亚当的完全修和；而依撒格只是在其父亚巴郎的思想与动机中被献上。这思想与意向便足以使亚巴郎与造物主修和。这不该使你惊讶，因为亚当之罪大过亚巴郎之罪，便也要求更大的补赎。

§170  The Mount Moriah Type and the Ten Kinds of Worship
§170  摩黎雅山的预表与十种敬拜

The mountain represents spiritual shelter where deceased minors shall go in divine privation to complete the rest of their simple spiritual activities — this is commonly called purgatory. It also represents the sentient circle. The wood upon which Isaac was laid indicates the kind of wood to be used to set fire to the sacrifice, and which perfumes to offer in the various rites of worship. These rites are: 1. the rite of atonement; 2. of general and particular grace; 3. against demons; 4. of protection and preservation; 5. against war; 6. to oppose the enemies of divine law; 7. of faith and perseverance in divine virtue; 8. to make the divine spirit descend; 9. to fix the spirit of divine reconciliation within oneself; 10. the annual rite or dedication of all forms of worship to the Creator. All of these were included in the two rites conducted by Moses in Israel and by Solomon in the Temple, and each was performed at the new moon.

那山代表属灵的庇护之所——已逝的次者将在神性匮乏中前往那里，完成其单纯属灵活动的余下部分；此处通称为「炼狱」（purgatory）。它也代表「感识之圈」。依撒格所被安放于其上的柴，指出将来用以点燃祭物的木材、并当在各样敬拜礼仪中所献的馨香。这些礼仪是：一、赎罪之礼；二、总体与个别恩宠之礼；三、抵御群魔之礼；四、护佑与保守之礼；五、御战之礼；六、抵挡神性律法之敌之礼；七、在神性德能上持守信心与坚忍之礼；八、使神性之灵降临之礼；九、把神性修和之灵固定于己内之礼；十、每年一次、把一切敬拜形式奉献于造物主之礼。凡此皆包含于梅瑟在以色列、并撒罗满在圣殿中所行的两大礼仪之内，且每一样都在新月时举行。

The Two Types that Abraham and Isaac Represent
亚巴郎与依撒格所代表的两层预表

The first type: Abraham, who removed his son from the pyre, represents the spirit that the Creator sends to minors when they pay tribute to divine justice through their practices within the three circles (sentient, visual, and rational). The second type is the divine covenant with men. Abraham represents the reconciliation of the first man Adam. As a mark of faith, he asked the Creator to make a covenant with him, and was told: "Abraham, circumcise your flesh, and the blood that your flesh will spread upon the earth will be genuine proof of the covenant." This is commonly called a baptism of blood. The Christ himself, by the circumcision he suffered, assured us of the covenant of Adam, Noah, and Abraham with the Creator. Isaac personifies the Christ, and this son is representative of the true type that the Creator subsequently sent to Earth to carry out the true sacrifice.

第一层预表：亚巴郎把儿子从柴堆上扶起，代表当诸次者藉其在三圈（感识、视识、理性）中的修习向神性公义纳贡时，造物主所差给他们的灵。第二层预表是天主与人的盟约。亚巴郎代表首位之人亚当的修和。作为信心的印记，他求造物主与他立约，并被告知：“亚巴郎，你要割损你的肉身；你肉身之血洒在地上，将成为这盟约真实的凭证。”这通称为「血的圣洗」。基督本身藉祂所受的割损，向我们确保了亚当、诺厄与亚巴郎同造物主的盟约。依撒格是基督的化身，这儿子代表造物主随后差往地上、去成就那真祭献之真正的预表。

Part IX — JACOB
第九部 — 雅各伯（Jacob；和合本：雅各）
Jacob, Type of Bad Spirits, and Esau, Type of Minor
雅各伯，恶灵的预表；厄撒乌，次者的预表

The third type is found in Isaac's descendants. You know that he had twin sons, Jacob and Esau. Jacob was conceived first and Esau second. Abraham represents the Divine Father and Isaac the son of Divinity; likewise, Isaac's two children are examples of the first and second spiritual emanations. The first spiritual emanation made by the Creator was that of spirits who transgressed. Even though Jacob was the second-born, he was conceived first, while Esau, the first-born, was conceived second. The first spirits, having transgressed, were supplanted spiritually by the minor or first man, who thus became their elder. Jacob, by his rank of conception, represents the transgressor spirits, while Esau represents the minor type.

第三层预表见于依撒格的后裔。你知道他有一对双生子，雅各伯与厄撒乌（Esau；和合本：以扫）。雅各伯先受孕，厄撒乌后受孕。亚巴郎代表神性之父，依撒格代表神性之子；同样，依撒格的两个孩子是第一与第二次属灵流溢的例证。造物主所作的第一次属灵流溢，是那些悖逆之灵的流溢。雅各伯虽是次生的，却先受孕；而厄撒乌虽是长子，却后受孕。最初的诸灵既已悖逆，便在属灵上被那次者、即首位之人所取代，次者由此成了它们的尊长。雅各伯以其受孕的位次，代表悖逆诸灵；厄撒乌则代表次者的预表。

§174  Jacob's Transgression
§174  雅各伯的悖逆

Jacob's real transgression was having abused the faith of his good father. He used all possible means to read the thoughts of his brother Esau, and, opposing his brother's good acts, supplanted him in every spiritual right, placing himself and his descendants into subjection and spiritual privation for a very long time. In this way, Jacob repeated the transgression of the first spirits towards the Creator. We see in him the double transgression of the demons: against the Creator, and against His creature. Did not Jacob transgress against his father in the first place and secondly against his younger brother Esau, just as the demon transgressed against his Divine Father and his spiritual brother, the minor? And do not men repeat this pattern every day by their wrong conduct towards the Creator and their brothers?

雅各伯真正的悖逆，在于滥用了他良善父亲的信任。他用尽一切可能的方法去窥读其弟厄撒乌的思想；他既反对其弟良善的作为，便在一切属灵权利上取代了他，把自己与其后裔长久地置于臣服与属灵匮乏之中。如此，雅各伯重演了最初诸灵对造物主的悖逆。我们在他身上看见群魔的双重悖逆：对造物主的，并对祂受造物的。雅各伯岂不是先得罪其父、其次得罪其弟厄撒乌，正如魔鬼得罪其神性之父、并其属灵之弟次者一般吗？人岂不也每日藉其对造物主、并对其弟兄的错误行为，重演这一样式吗？

§175  Error, Punishment, and Esau's Repentance
§175  错误、惩罚与厄撒乌的悔改

You should not be surprised that Jacob behaved towards Esau as he did. Esau preferred worldly worship, interested only in hunting instead of combating the demoniacal intellect that had taken hold over his brother. Esau's abandonment of divine worship attracted deserved punishments and stripped him of all spiritual rights. However, he returned to the correct path and by divine mercy realized the extent of his transgressions. He complained to his father about how Jacob had usurped his birthright, of his pain of having been born first yet being last in spiritual blessings. This foreshadowed what would happen to Israel, which, although the spiritual elder, would be supplanted by those to come after, confirming the prophecy that "the first shall be last."

你不该惊讶于雅各伯待厄撒乌的方式。厄撒乌偏爱属世的敬拜，只顾狩猎，而不去与那已辖制其弟的魔性智识争战。厄撒乌离弃神性的敬拜，招致了应得的惩罚，剥夺了他一切属灵的权利。然而他重回正路，藉神性的慈悲认清了自己悖逆的深重。他向父亲抱怨雅各伯如何篡夺了他的长子名分，抱怨自己虽先出生、在属灵祝福上却居末的痛苦。这预示了以色列日后的遭遇：以色列虽是属灵的长者，却将被后来者所取代，印证了那「在先的将要在后」的预言。

§176  Esau Blessed by Isaac and the Great Reconciliation to Come
§176  厄撒乌蒙依撒格祝福，及将来的大修和

Esau angrily told his father, "Have you then not reserved any blessing for me?" Seeing he could gain nothing from his father's spirit, he said, "Since you have no spiritual gift remaining for me, I beseech you to bless me, at least, in the name of the Eternal." Isaac replied, "I have appointed your brother Jacob as lord of men in this world; nothing remains in my power for you." Esau uttered a great cry and groaned bitterly. But Isaac, touched by his son's situation, said, "Esau, the blessing that you ask is in the fat of the land because of your transgression. The blessing that I give you comes from the Eternal like the dew from heaven." Notice that the conduct of his father is an example of the immutability of the Creator in His decrees of justice, while the mercy that Isaac showed at the end of his days represents the mercy of the Divine Father towards His creature. This foreshadows the great reconciliation to come.

厄撒乌愤怒地对父亲说：“难道你没有为我留下任何祝福吗？”见从父亲的灵那里一无所获，他说：“既然你没有属灵恩赐留给我，我求你至少奉永恒主的名祝福我。”依撒格回答：“我已立你弟弟雅各伯为这世上众人之主；我手中再没有什么留给你了。”厄撒乌放声大哭，痛苦地呻吟。但依撒格被儿子的处境所动，说：“厄撒乌，因你的悖逆，你所求的祝福在于地上的肥土。我给你的祝福出于永恒主，如同天上的甘露。”须注意：他父亲的行为，是造物主在其公义判令上不可更易之性的例证；而依撒格在其暮年所显的慈悲，则代表神性之父对其受造物的慈悲。这又预示了将来的大修和。

§177  Jacob Goes Astray and Fights with the Angel
§177  雅各伯误入歧途，并与天使角力

Jacob had numerous descendants, and, seeing they prospered in temporal riches, inspired them in earthly ambitions, so that all forgot about divine devotion. Jacob allowed himself to be persuaded by the demoniacal spirit that his well-being was provided by the great prince of demons, and that he and his descendants would be rewarded according to the devotion offered to this prince. He renounced his first emanation and the Creator Himself, believing himself a passive being. Pursuing the demoniacal material sciences, he resolved to go to the land of Haran. Nightfall stopped his journey near Mount Moriah, or Mahanaim (meaning two camps, that of the demons and of the Creator). He made his invocation as the sun was setting; the Creator sent him an angel in the form of a man, whose presence made such a strong impression on Jacob's corporeal essence that it knocked him to the ground.

雅各伯有众多后裔；见他们在属时的财货上兴盛，便怂恿他们追逐地上的野心，以致众人都忘记了神性的虔敬。雅各伯任凭自己被魔性之灵劝服，以为他的安乐乃是那群魔之大君所供给，并以为他与其后裔将照所献给这大君的虔敬而得赏。他弃绝了自己的第一次流溢、并弃绝了造物主本身，自以为是一个被动的存有。他既追逐魔性的物质之学，便决意前往哈兰（Haran；和合本：哈兰）之地。夜幕在摩黎雅山、即玛哈纳因（Mahanaim；和合本：玛哈念，意为「两营」，即群魔之营与造物主之营）附近止住了他的行程。他在日落时作了祈请；造物主差给他一位形如人的天使，这天使的临在在雅各伯的形体本质上留下如此强烈的印象，把他击倒在地。

§178  Repentance of Jacob Who Becomes Israel
§178  雅各伯悔改，成为以色列

Jacob called upon the Creator and renounced the demoniacal things he had adopted. The angel reproached him for his deeds towards the Creator, his brother, and his father. Frightened, Jacob threw himself upon the angel and wrestled him the entire night until dawn. When the struggle was over, the angel asked Jacob his name; instead of answering, Jacob asked the angel to bless him. After the angel asked a third time, Jacob answered his name, to which the angel remarked, "Jacob, supplanter against the Creator, renouncing the spirit of the Lord." Then the spirit made such an attraction upon Jacob that his Achilles' tendon became shriveled. "You are called Jacob; in the future, you shall be called Israel, which means striving against the Creator's spirit." They parted, and Jacob was confused to find himself marked by the spirit that he had renounced.

雅各伯求告造物主，弃绝了他所采纳的魔性之物。天使责备他对造物主、对其弟、对其父的行为。雅各伯惊惧之下，扑向天使，与他角力整整一夜，直到黎明。角力结束时，天使问雅各伯的名字；雅各伯不作答，反求天使祝福他。天使问到第三次，雅各伯才答出自己的名字，天使便说：“雅各伯，与造物主相争、弃绝上主之灵者。”随即那灵对雅各伯施以如此的牵引，使他的脚跟腱萎缩。“你名叫雅各伯；将来你要称为以色列，意为『与造物主之灵相争』。”二者遂分手，雅各伯困惑地发觉自己竟被他所弃绝的那灵所标记。

§179  The Rule of the Letter B
§179  字母 B 的规条

Jacob's mark is in his posterity forever, and is proof of Israel's transgression. Since this time, it has been forbidden by the Eternal, either in the temple of Moses or of Solomon, that no person marked by the letter B by birth shall be admitted to the divine work under any circumstance. This law, given under the threat of the most severe punishments, was confirmed by the Christ, so that all who are responsible today for the practice of the divine religion in his spiritual temple observe this command with the greatest strictness.

雅各伯的印记永在其后裔身上，是以色列悖逆的凭证。自此，永恒主便禁止——无论在梅瑟的圣殿还是撒罗满的圣殿中——凡生而带有字母 B 之印记者，在任何情形下都不得被接纳进入神性之工。这条以最严厉惩罚相威吓而颁下的律法，为基督所确认，使今日一切在其属灵圣殿中负责施行神性宗教之人，都极严格地遵守这一命令。

§180  Jacob's New Ordination and His Vision of the Ladder
§180  雅各伯重新受命及其天梯之异象

Despite his despondency, Jacob again found the true desire to be within the grace of the Creator. A vision in human form — the same spirit who had marked his tendon — confirmed that his wishes were granted, and again ordained him, giving him divine spiritual power to carry out worship for forty years. He went to the summit of Mount Moriah, prayed until midnight, and four angels came to instruct him. On the tenth day, Jacob received confirmation of his perfect reconciliation; resting his head on an ordinary stone, he slept and saw seven spirits ascending and descending above him. Among them he recognized the one who had injured him and the four angels. He again saw the glory of the Creator. He said, "This is the place of perfect vision, for I saw the Eternal face-to-face. It is here that the house to the Creator shall be built." With three stones placed in a triangle, he marked the place where the temple was to be built on Mount Moriah — achieved later by Solomon, Chiram, and Hiram, king of Tyre.

尽管沮丧，雅各伯重又生出真心渴望，要在造物主的恩宠之中。一个人形的异象——正是先前标记其脚跟腱的那灵——确认他的心愿已蒙应允，并再次给他授命，赐他神性属灵之权能，去行敬拜四十年。他上到摩黎雅山之顶，祈祷至午夜，四位天使前来教导他。到第十日，雅各伯得了他完全修和的确认；他把头枕在一块普通石头上睡了，看见七位灵在他上方上下往来。其中他认出那伤他的一位、并那四位天使。他又看见造物主的荣耀。他说：“这是完美异象之地，因为我面对面见了永恒主。归于造物主的殿必在此建造。”他把三块石头摆成三角形，标出了那要在摩黎雅山上建殿之处——后来由撒罗满、希兰（Chiram/Hiram；和合本：希兰）、并提洛（Tyre；和合本：推罗）王希兰所成就。

§181  Worship and Invocation Carried Out by Jacob
§181  雅各伯所行的敬拜与祈请

Jacob's placing of the stones in a triangle symbolized the corporeal form of the earth. He remained in the center to show that the Creator had placed God-man in the center of the universe. He carried out four operations every six hours during the day; then, for six consecutive days, a divine vigil was held, making ten sessions in the span of seven days — the denary number consecrated to Divinity and the septenary dedicated to the spirit. In the first four procedures, Jacob called upon the Creator by His first ineffable name, saying "Help, God of Abraham!"; then by His second, "Help, God of Isaac!"; and a third time, "Help, God of Jacob who is the same as Abraham's and Isaac's God!" Jacob recognized Abraham as the type of the Creator, Isaac as the type of the divine son, and in himself the true type of spirit.

雅各伯把石头摆成三角形，象征大地的形体。他留在中央，以表明造物主曾把神-人置于宇宙的中央。他白天每六小时行四桩运作；然后连续六日举行一次神性守夜，使七日之内共有十次聚会——十元数奉献于神性，七元数奉献于灵。在头四道程序中，雅各伯以造物主第一个不可名状之名呼求祂，说：“亚巴郎的天主，求你帮助！”；再以其第二个名，说：“依撒格的天主，求你帮助！”；第三次说：“雅各伯的天主——与亚巴郎、依撒格的天主同一者——求你帮助！”雅各伯认亚巴郎为造物主的预表，认依撒格为神性之子的预表，并在自己身上认出灵的真实预表。

§182  About the Holy Trinity
§182  论天主圣三

From Jacob's last works we learn that God was in three persons, because the Creator had carried out three divine and distinct actions, each for the benefit of the three minors, in accordance with the three universal types. The three persons are in God only in regard to their divine operations, and cannot be understood otherwise without lessening Divinity, which is indivisible. If it were possible to describe distinct personalities within the Creator, it would be necessary to describe four instead of three, in line with the quadruple divine essence: the divine spirit (10), the major spirit (7), the inferior spirit (3), and the minor spirit (4). This makes it impossible for the Creator to be divided into three persons.

从雅各伯最后的工作，我们得知天主是三位一体的；因为造物主曾施行了三桩神性而分别的行动，各为那三位次者之益，并合于三种普世的预表。这三位在天主之内，仅就其神性运作而言；若非如此理解，便会减损那不可分的神性。倘若真能在造物主之内描述出分别的位格，那便须描述四位、而非三位，以合于那四重神性本质：神性之灵（10）、高位之灵（7）、下位之灵（3）、次位之灵（4）。这便使造物主不可能被分为三个位格。

Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob; Adam, Abel, and Seth
亚巴郎、依撒格、雅各伯；亚当、亚伯尔、舍特

These three minors were the true symbols of Adam, Abel, and Seth. The three former, like the three latter, had seen the glory of the Creator. Noah, Shem, and Japheth had the same privilege. As for Esau, who remains without any particular heritage, he is of the likes of Cain in Adam and of Ham in Noah. These types were foretold by Adam and Noah and their descendants. The Christ, Moses, and Elijah confirmed this through their work on Mount Tabor, where together they saw the glory of the Creator. The kiss that Jacob gave to Isaac announced the betrayal that the God-man was to experience from among his disciples in Judas Iscariot. The former is the supplanter of the material and the latter is the supplanter of the spiritual. Take care that the cupidity of matter does not lead you to repeat such an inimical example.

这三位次者是亚当、亚伯尔与舍特的真实象征。前三位如同后三位，都曾见过造物主的荣耀。诺厄、闪与耶斐特也有同样的殊荣。至于厄撒乌，他没有任何特别的产业，正如亚当中的加音、诺厄中的含之流。这些预表由亚当、诺厄及其后裔所预告。基督、梅瑟与厄里亚（Elijah；和合本：以利亚）藉他们在大博尔山（Mount Tabor；和合本：他泊山）上的工作确认了此点——他们在那里一同看见了造物主的荣耀。雅各伯给依撒格的那一吻，预告了神-人将要在其门徒中、从犹达斯·依斯加略（Judas Iscariot；和合本：加略人犹大）经历的背叛。前者是物质的篡夺者，后者是属灵的篡夺者。你要当心，不可让物质的贪欲引你重演这般可憎的例证。

翻译进度

已完成：导论 + 第一部「亚当」（§1–50）+ 第二部「加音」（§51–53）+ 第三部「亚伯尔」（§54–82）+ 第四部「哈诺客」（§83–111）+ 第五部「诺厄」（§113–138）+ 第六部「诺厄的众智者」（§139–161）+ 第七部「亚巴郎」（§162–167）+ 第八部「依撒格」（§168–170）+ 第九部「雅各伯」（§174–182）。仅余篇幅最大的第十部「梅瑟」（§184–279，约占全书三分之一）与第十一部「撒乌耳」（§280–283）正依同一体例译出。

帕斯夸利术语（保留原文）

英文原词 (English)本稿中文译名类别说明
reintegration重新融合核心概念将已分崩离析者复归为统一整体；全书主题与终极目标
reconciliation修和核心概念天主教术语；人与神性恢复关系的预备阶段，由基督（修和者）促成
regeneration重生核心概念介于「分离」与「重新融合」之间，唤醒沉睡官能的过程
to emanate / emanation流溢形上动作神由自身发出存有，区别于「创造（create）」
to emancipate / emancipated解放独立形上动作被赋予自主、从神性无量界中分立而出
operation / to operate运作核心动作灵藉其意志所施之举；亦含神工术的礼仪运作
the Word / Word of creation言／创造之言核心概念由「意向＋意志＋言」三者所成的创造之力
the minor次者存有等级帕斯夸利对「人／受造之灵」的专称（法 le mineur）
minor spirits次级精神体存有等级
major (being / spirit)高位者存有等级数字象征为 8
superior上位者存有等级数字象征为 10
inferior下位者存有等级数字象征为 7
secondary cause / primary cause第二因／第一因形上学受造之灵仅为第二因，神为第一因
divine immensity神性无量界处所／范畴神性自身无量的范围
Supercelestial Immensity超天界无量处所／范畴人类与神性原初共在之境
Domination统辖界处所／范畴最初诸灵所居的神性周界，带「十进之数」
privation匮乏状态受罚之灵被剥夺神性、陷于黑暗之境
impassive无受苦的／不受动的性质impassive form＝无受苦之形；与「被动／会朽」相对
glorious form荣耀之形概念堕落前纯灵性、无受苦的形体
God-Man神-人称谓亚当原初的尊贵身份（homme-Dieu）
homme Réaux / royal man王者之人称谓意为「充满智慧、权能与德能的神-人」
intellect智识概念善／恶灵向人传通「思想」的媒介；非通常意义的「理智」
pensive思虑（者）概念堕落后受智识左右、退化的思想状态
thinking (being)思想（之存有）概念原初不假外求、直接读知的认知状态
thought / will思想／意志概念思想来自外在之灵；意志为人自身、自由的
quadruple divine essence四重神性本质数理神学上位、高位、下位、次位四重权能
ternary三元数数理神学意向(1)＋意志(2)＋言(3)
quaternary四元数数理神学赐予次者（人）的权能，数字 4
senary / the number six六（元）数理神学创造的六个思想；1+2+3
septenary七元数数理神学赐予灵的数字 7
octonary八元数数理神学两个四元数之和，与基督相关（8）
nonary九元数数理神学
denary十元数／十进数理神学Domination 之数（10）
Omneity全有神性称谓源自拉丁 omnia「一切」；神性即「全体」
theurgy神工术修习选立祭司团借以追求重新融合的礼仪修习
Ordre des Élus-Cohen选立祭司团组织Order of Elect Priests，蒙拣选的祭司之团
man / men of desire渴慕之人称谓走灵性道路、渴慕重新融合的修行者
emulator效法者称谓效法造物主而行的人
the tree of evil life恶之生命树概念群魔之首；与「生命之树」相对
filiation传承谱系概念德能与启引代代相传的脉络
Hély / the Cosmic Christ赫利／宇宙性的基督称谓帕斯夸利所用之名，受神所遣使亚当得宽恕
State of Grace恩宠之境概念人原初所处、当重返的状态
Illumination光照概念「心之道」所趋向的目标

凡例与说明

1. 圣经人名、地名采用【思高本（天主教思高圣经学会译本）】译名，与帕斯夸利的天主教背景一致；并附「和合本（新教）」译名作参考对照。

2. 正文中圣经专名首次出现时，在思高本译名后以括号保留英文原文，例如：亚当（Adam）。

3. 帕斯夸利自创或赋予特殊含义的术语，给出本稿中文译名后，于正文中以括号保留英文原词，例如：次者（minor）、重新融合（reintegration）。

4. 「出现于本试译」列：√ 表示该名出现在本次试译范围（导论＋第一部「亚当」第1–31节）；「后段」表示见于后续章节，先行列出以便统一体例。

5. 数字象征（三元、四元、十进等）为帕斯夸利数理神学的核心，译名暂定，欢迎据你的偏好调整后我再统一全书。

6. 如你对任一译名（尤其是「次者 minor」「思虑 pensive」「智识 intellect」「言 Word」等）有偏好，请告知，我将在续译中全局统一。

版权与用途　本页译文为译者个人翻译，非商业性质，仅供学习与研究参考，不作任何商业用途。原著及原文译本的著作权归原作者与出版社所有；引文、页码与专名请一律以原书为准。译文中的错漏由译者负责。若权利人认为本页不宜公开，请与站主联系，将立即撤下。 Martinès de Pasqually, Treatise on the Reintegration of Beings · English translation © Traditional Martinist Order, 2018.
